### [1] [The stability conjecture for the Bernoulli problem in dimension four](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05669)
*Xavier Fernández-Real,Joaquim Serra*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在四维欧几里得空间中，单相Bernoulli问题的一切全局经典稳定解都是一维的（即自由边界由一条或两条平行超平面构成），解决了四维情形下的稳定性猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Bernoulli问题在四维空间中的稳定性猜想，该猜想此前在低维（如二维、三维）已有结果，但四维尚未被解决。

Method: 通过严格的数学分析证明方法，利用经典的PDE理论和稳定性分析技术，证明四维空间中全局经典稳定解必然是一维结构的。

Result: 在R^4中证明了：1）一切全局经典稳定解是一维的；2）自由边界（若非空）由一条或两条平行超平面构成；3）作为推论，获得了稳定解的全局限部Hessian估计和自由边界曲率估计；4）有限Morse指标的全局经典解具有轴对称性。

Conclusion: 该结果彻底解决了四维空间中单相Bernoulli问题的稳定性猜想，并为相关问题（如有限Morse指标解的对称性）提供了新的结论。

Abstract: We prove that every global classical stable solution to the one-phase Bernoulli problem in $\mathbb R^4$ is one-dimensional. In particular, if nonempty, its free boundary consists of one or two parallel hyperplanes. This settles the stability conjecture for the Bernoulli problem in dimension four. As consequences, we obtain universal local Hessian and free-boundary curvature estimates for classical stable solutions in $\mathbb R^4$, as well as axial symmetry of global classical solutions with finite Morse index in $\mathbb R^4$.

</details>


### [2] [Global asymptotic stability of KdV-Burgers fronts in a weakly two-dimensional model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05697)
*Jared C. Bronski,Olivia Clifton,Vera Mikyoung Hur*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二维通道中非线性色散-耗散PDE模型（KdVB方程的弱横向扩展）的锋面型解稳定性。证明在通道足够窄且相对色散参数处于一维稳定范围内时，一维锋面是弱二维扰动下的全局渐近吸引子，且在空间平移意义下唯一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维通道中涌潮（undular bore）传播的数学建模与稳定性分析。KdVB方程描述一维涌潮传播，但实际物理场景中存在横向运动，需要将其扩展至二维并验证一维锋面解在二维扰动下是否保持稳定，从而为更接近真实物理场景的涌潮动力学提供严格数学基础。

Method: 将此前在一维情形下发展的能量方法扩展至二维，以处理时间调制的扰动解。通过引入弱横向运动的系统扩展KdVB方程，在通道足够窄的假设下，利用能量估计论证一维锋面解在一般二维扰动下的全局渐近稳定性。分析相对色散参数的范围以确保一维扰动的稳定性条件。

Result: 证明在一维扰动稳定所要求的相对色散参数范围内，且通道横向足够窄的条件下，一维锋面是弱二维设置下的全局渐近吸引子。进一步证明该锋面在空间平移意义下是唯一的。

Conclusion: 研究建立了非线性色散-耗散PDE系统中一维KdVB锋面在弱二维扰动下的全局稳定性，扩展了能量方法的应用范围，为二维通道中涌潮传播的动力学行为提供了数学保证。

Abstract: We study front-type solutions of nonlinear dispersive-dissipative PDEs modeling the propagation of undular bores in a channel in two dimensions. The system extends the Korteweg-de Vries-Burgers (KdVB) equation by incorporating weak transverse motion, and admits the KdVB fronts as one-dimensional solutions. We investigate their stability under general two-dimensional perturbations. We prove that a one-dimensional front is a global asymptotic attractor in the weakly two-dimensional setting when the channel is sufficiently narrow in the transverse direction and the relative dispersion parameter lies in a range for stability to one-dimensional perturbations. Particularly, the front is unique up to spatial translations. The proof extends the energy method for temporally-modulated perturbed solutions, developed previously in the one-dimensional setting, to accommodate the transverse dynamics.

</details>


### [3] [On Decay of the Local Mean Oscillations of the Vorticity Direction in Critical Navier-Stokes Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05720)
*Zoran Grujic*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程中涡量方向演化的几何PDE，聚焦临界空间点奇异性（涡量强度以O(|x|^{-2})集中于临界Lorentz空间）。该PDE由球面调和映射热流（HMHF）加上流体输运、交叉扩散和切向应变组成。核心发现是O(|x|^{-2})浓度释放出核心处向外的径向漂移4νx/|x|^2，且HMHF非线性量对子解无害，从而建立传递定理：核心处方向的bmo_{1/|log r|}正则性由内尺度方向的时间对数模量及物理应变控制。关键在于应变仅通过切向分量P_{ξ⊥}Sξ进入方向方程，当方向恰为应变的特征向量（含最大拉伸特征向量）时该项为零，这与依赖耗尽全涡旋拉伸项的经典几何正则判据形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析三维NSE中涡量方向在临界点奇异性情形下的演化规律，判断NSE力学能否传播方向局部平均振荡的对数衰减条件ξ∈bmo_{1/|log r|}——该条件在先前工作中已被证明可防止有限时间爆破。目标是超越局限于弱拉伸构型的经典几何正则性判据。

Method: 从三维不可压缩NSE中分离并分析涡量方向演化的几何PDE，将其识别为球面HMHF与流体输运、交叉扩散及切向应变的组合。通过提取O(|x|^{-2})浓度带来的径向漂移项4νx/|x|^2，利用1/2|ξ−e|^2在任何半球上为子解的性质消除HMHF非线性的影响，进而建立由内尺度时间对数模量控制核心处bmo正则性的传递定理。

Result: 建立了传递定理：核心处涡量方向的bmo_{1/|log r|}正则性（可一致延伸到奇异时刻）由内尺度的方向时间对数模量和物理应变共同控制。且应变仅通过切向分量P_{ξ⊥}Sξ起作用，当方向是应变张量的特征向量（包括最大拉伸特征向量）时该贡献消失。

Conclusion: 该结果与此前经典的基于耗尽全涡旋拉伸项的几何正则判据形成鲜明对比——后者仅适用于弱拉伸构型，而本工作表明即使在强拉伸（含最大拉伸特征向量）情形下，方向方程的结构仍可为防止爆破的正则性条件提供控制途径。

Abstract: We isolate and analyze the geometric PDE governing the evolution of the vorticity direction in the 3D incompressible Navier-Stokes equations (NSE), restricted to the case of a critical spatial point singularity where the vorticity magnitude concentrates as $O(|x|^{-2})$, inhabiting the critical Lorentz space $L^{3/2, \infty}$. The PDE consists of the Harmonic Map Heat Flow (HMHF) into the sphere supplemented with the fluid transport, cross-diffusion and tangential strain. The question is whether the NSE mechanics can propagate logarithmic decay of the local mean oscillations of the direction -- the condition $\xiVec \in \bmo_{1/|\log r|}$ which (in this setting) was shown in the companion paper to prevent finite time blow-up. The key observations are that the $O(|x|^{-2})$ concentration, factored out of the viscous cross-diffusion, generates an outward radial drift $4ν\, x/|x|^2$ at the core and that the HMHF nonlinearity is harmless for the quantity $\frac12|\xiVec - e|^2$, which is a subsolution on any hemisphere. This yields a transfer theorem: the $\bmo_{1/|\log r|}$ regularity of the direction at the core, uniformly up to the singular time, is controlled by a logarithmic modulus in time of the direction at the inner scale, together with the physical strain. Crucially, the strain enters the direction equation only through its tangential component $P_{\xiVec^\perp} S\xiVec$, which vanishes precisely when the direction is an eigenvector of the strain tensor. Since this includes the eigenvector carrying the maximal stretching, the result is in contrast to the classical geometric regularity criteria which are built on depleting the full vortex-stretching term and thus confined to configurations of weak stretching.

</details>


### [4] [Global existence and finite-time blow-up for a strongly damped wave--MGT system with fully subcritical logarithmic nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05819)
*Tae Gab Ha*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究具有对数源的耦合强阻尼波-Moore-Gibson-Thompson（MGT）系统，在完整的Sobolev次临界范围内（2<γ<2*）证明了局部存在性、唯一性、解的延拓准则，并建立了低于井深d_α的动力学二分法：稳定集内的初值解全局存在且指数衰减，不稳定集内的解有限时间爆破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅能在较低次临界区间处理对数非线性项，因为在该完整范围2<γ<2*内，对数非线性失去了标准的紧致性和差分估计。本文旨在克服这一困难，在完整Sobolev次临界范围内研究该耦合强阻尼MGT系统。

Method: 引入增广变量w=v+τv_t，推导强阻尼系统的精确能量耗散恒等式；利用耦合势阱结构。局部存在性采用Faedo-Galerkin方案结合闭非线性微分不等式与空间域分裂论证；强阻尼提供额外正则性用于证明唯一性。爆破论证通过精确能量恒等式重建对数贡献，揭示增广形式特有的结构消去，使Levine凹性泛函中不利的MGT残差被代数吸收，从而无需对初速度施加额外符号条件即可闭合凹性方法。

Result: ①在完整次临界范围内建立了局部存在性、唯一性及延拓准则；②低于井深d_α时获得尖锐动力学二分法：稳定集初值→全局存在且指数衰减，不稳定集初值→有限时间爆破；③爆破证明不依赖初速度的额外符号条件。

Conclusion: 通过增广变量和精确能量恒等式，成功将对数非线性处理扩展到完整的Sobolev次临界范围，克服了紧致性和差分估计缺失的根本困难，并给出了清晰的全局-爆破动力学图景。

Abstract: We study a coupled strongly damped wave--Moore--Gibson--Thompson (MGT) system with logarithmic source $f(u)=|u|^{γ-2}u\ln|u|$ in the full Sobolev-subcritical range $2<γ<2^*:=2n/(n-2)$. The main difficulty is that, in this full range, the logarithmic nonlinearity does not admit the standard compactness and difference estimates available in the lower subcritical regime. Using the augmented variable $w=v+τv_t$, we derive the exact energy-dissipation identity for the strongly damped system and exploit the associated coupled potential-well structure.
  For the local theory, a Faedo--Galerkin scheme combined with a closed nonlinear differential inequality and a spatial domain-splitting argument yields local existence. The strong damping provides the additional regularity needed to prove uniqueness throughout the full subcritical range and to establish a continuation principle. Below the well depth $d_α$, we then obtain a sharp dynamical dichotomy: solutions with initial data in the stable set exist globally and decay exponentially, whereas solutions with initial data in the unstable set blow up in finite time.
  The blow-up argument reveals a structural cancellation specific to the augmented formulation: once the logarithmic contribution is reconstructed through the exact energy identity, the unfavorable MGT residual in Levine's concavity functional is absorbed algebraically. This allows the concavity method to close without imposing any additional sign condition on the initial velocities.

</details>


### [5] [Long-time asymptotics of nonlinear Fokker-Planck equations with inhomogeneous diffusion and natural boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05873)
*Kouta Araki,Masashi Mizuno*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究具有空间非齐次扩散的非线性Fokker-Planck方程的长时渐近行为，基于能量耗散律，先后推导了解的先验正下界和上界、给出了耗散函数指数衰减的充分条件，并通过数值计算考察该条件的本质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非线性Fokker-Planck方程在空间非齐次扩散情形下的长时间渐近行为，特别是解的耗散函数是否能够指数衰减，这对于理解物理系统的平衡态收敛性质具有重要意义。

Method: 首先推导方程解的先验正下界和上界；然后基于能量耗散律给出耗散函数指数衰减的充分条件；最后通过数值计算检验该充分条件是否本质（必要条件或接近必要）。

Result: 得到了方程解的全局正上、下界估计；建立了耗散函数指数衰减的充分条件；数值实验验证了该条件的必要性与适用范围。

Conclusion: 该研究为空间非齐次扩散的非线性Fokker-Planck方程提供了长时渐近行为的分析框架，所给出的指数衰减充分条件经数值验证具有本质性或较好的紧致性，为后续理论推广和应用提供了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the long-time asymptotics for nonlinear Fokker-Planck equations with spatial inhomogeneous diffusion, based on the law of energy dissipation. First, we derive a priori positive lower and upper bounds for solutions of the equations. Next, we give a sufficient condition for deriving the exponential decay of the dissipation function. Finally, we investigate whether the given sufficient condition is essential or not by numerical computation.

</details>


### [6] [Sharp dispersive estimates for wave equation on lattice graphs with high dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05930)
*Zhe You,Xiongfeng Zhan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文解决了离散波动方程在格图Z^d上色散估计的所有剩余维度问题，包括d=5及更高维度，并证明了估计在d≥5时是最优的，同时该方法也适用于d=4的上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schultz在1998年首次建立了Z^d格图上离散波动方程基本解的色散估计（d=2,3），随后Bi、Cheng和Hua将其推广到d=4和5。然而剩余维度的问题尚未完全解决，本文旨在给出所有剩余维度的完整答案。

Method: 本文采用了新的分析技术来处理高维情形下的色散估计，该方法不仅解决了d≥5的所有维度问题，还统一适用于d=4的上界估计。

Result: 对所有剩余维度（包括d=5及以上）给出了完整解答，证明了所得色散估计对每个d≥5都是最优的（sharp），且证明方法对d=4的上界情形同样有效。

Conclusion: 该结果完善了离散波动方程色散估计的完整图景，填补了此前未知维度的空白，所有结果在维度意义上已是最优的。

Abstract: Schultz [Comm.~Pure~Appl.~Math., 1998] first established dispersive estimates for the fundamental solution of the discrete wave equation on lattice graphs $\Z^d$ for $d=2,3$, which can be seen as a discrete analogue of the classical dispersive estimate for the Euclidean wave equation. His result was extended by Bi, Cheng and Hua to $d=4$ and $5$. In this paper, we give complete answers to all the remaining dimensions, including $d=5$. Our estimate is sharp for every $d\geq 5$. Moreover, the proof remains valid for the upper bound of the case for $d=4$.

</details>


### [7] [Nonlinear profiles on solutions to the surface quasi-geostrophic equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05943)
*Masakazu Yamamoto*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究表面准地转方程（surface quasi-geostrophic equation）解的长时间行为，构建了基于抛物线尺度从慢到快衰减排列的非线性渐近展开式，并证明该展开的唯一性。由于方程在长时间尺度下是次临界的，主导分量呈现线性特征，而非线性特征出现在次要分量中，特别地揭示了由科里奥利力引发的细微但唯一的旋转流动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面准地转方程是预测高纬度地区低压系统周围位势温度以及海面位势温度的经典模型，其中非线性项代表科里奥利力的效应。理解该方程解的长时间行为及其非线性渐近结构，对于深入认识大尺度大气/海洋动力学至关重要。作者试图从大时间行为的视角唯一地刻画解的非线性渐近形貌，并揭示非线性效应与线性效应的本质区别。

Method: 作者从大时间渐近分析的视角出发，将解分解为按抛物线尺度从慢到快衰减的多个分量，构建非线性渐近展开式。利用方程在长时间效应下的次临界性，确定主导分量为线性结构，并在次要分量中识别非线性特征。通过时空变量相关性分析，对比非线性廓线与线性廓线的差异，最终确定由科里奥利力引起的旋转流动的唯一形式。

Result: 该方程解的渐近展开在长时间行为意义上被唯一确定。主导分量呈现线性特征，非线性特征体现在对解影响较小的分量中。非线性廓线在时空相关性上与线性廓线存在显著区别，且由于方程的空间结构，其非线性效应弱于具有相同尺度的其他方程。主要定理揭示了由科里奥利力导致的细微但唯一的旋转流动。

Conclusion: 本文完成了表面准地转方程解的长时间非线性渐近展开及其唯一性证明，表明该方程在长时间尺度下主导行为为线性，非线性效应较弱但不可忽略，并以唯一旋转流动的形式出现。这为理解科里奥利力在该类大气/海洋模型中的作用提供了精确的数学刻画。

Abstract: The quasi-geostrophic equation is well known as a model for predicting potential temperature around low-pressure systems in high-latitude regions. When diffusion effects are added to this equation, it serves as a model for potential temperature at the sea surface. In either case, the nonlinear term represents the effect of the Coriolis force. This paper yields nonlinear profile in solutions to the surface quasi-geostrophic equation. This profile is determined uniquely from the perspective of large-time behavior of solutions. Components of solutions are arranged sequentially from slow to fast decay based on the parabolic scale. Then such an expansion is determined uniquely. Since the equation is subcritical in the context of large-time effects, the main components exhibit linear features. Nonlinear characteristics appear in components that have a smaller effect on solutions. From the perspective of the correlation between spacetime variables, nonlinear profiles exhibit characteristics that are distinctly different from those of linear profiles. Moreover, the nonlinear effects of this equation are expected to be weaker than those of other equations with the same scale due to their spatial structure. The main theorem publishes minute, but unique rotational flow arising from the Coriolis force.

</details>


### [8] [Global Dynamic Patterns of Entropy Solutions for One-dimensional Pressureless Euler System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05948)
*Gaowei Cao,Kai Hu,Feimin Huang,Guirong Tang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维无压欧拉系统柯西问题的熵解的精细性质。在初始密度为局部有限Radon测度、初始速度属于L^∞的有界函数空间中，作者利用Huang-Wang引入的解公式，从四个角度（特征线与初始波、局部精细结构、分水岭与全局结构、不变量与渐近行为）系统地刻画了熵解的全局动力学模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维无压欧拉系统是描述宇宙尘埃粒子动力学、黏性消散等物理现象的经典模型，其初始数据包含Radon测度密度（可含点质量）时，系统呈现出delta波、真空态等复杂奇异性结构。此前已有解公式的提出，但对熵解的精细性质（如特征线几何、局部结构分类、全局动力学模式、渐近行为等）尚缺乏系统深入的刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用F.M. Huang和Z. Wang在Comm. Math. Phys. 222(1)(2001)中建立的解公式作为核心分析工具，对给定一般初始数据（ρ₀为局部有限Radon测度，u₀∈L^∞_{ρ₀}）的柯西问题熵解进行严格的数学分析。分析工作从四个维度展开：特征线与初始波的性质、解的局部精细结构、全局分水岭与整体结构、不变量与渐近行为（含渐近剖面和相应衰减率）。

Result: 获得了熵解在四方面的新精细性质：(i) 特征线与初始波的完整描述；(ii) 熵解的局部精细结构分类；(iii) 分水岭与全局结构特征；(iv) 熵解的不变量及渐近行为，包括精确的渐近剖面与对应的衰减速率。由此确立了一维无压欧拉系统在一般测量初值下熵解的完整全局动力学模式。

Phys. 222(1) (2001), 117--146.] for this Cauchy problem to analyze the entropy solutions and obtain various new fine properties of entropy solutions; these can be summarized in four aspects: (i) Characteristics and initial waves for the Cauchy problem; (ii) Fine local structures of entropy solutions; (iii) Divides and global structures of entropy solutions; (iv) Invariants and asymptotic behaviors of entropy solutions including the asymptotic profile and the corresponding decay rates. Abstract: Polyconvexity is a standard ingredient in the variational existence theory of finite elasticity, whereas true-stress-true-strain monotonicity (TSTS-M++) requires a positive incremental Cauchy-stress response. These two constitutive restrictions are independent, and Wollner, Holzapfel and Neff left open whether a compressible isotropic energy defined on the whole of $\mathrm{GL}^{+}(3)$ can satisfy both. Abstract: The Vlasov-Hartree system models a mixture of bosons and fermions interacting via Coulomb forces in which the bosons are described quantum-mechanically, while the fermions are described classically. Abstract: We consider the wave equation with variable wave speed in Euclidean space, with point sources modeled by Dirac delta initial displacement data. The inverse problem is to reconstruct the wave speed uniquely in the unknown set from an unlabeled collection of waves generated by point sources and measured in the receiver set. We use propagation of singularities and sharp finite speed of propagation to reduce this data to geometric travel-time data, whose form depends on how the three sets lie in relation to each other. Abstract: In this paper, we study the existence, uniqueness, and quantitative estimates for weak solutions to linear elliptic Dirichlet problems of the form \[ -\operatorname{div}(γ\nabla u)+\langle \nablaφ+\mathbf{H},\nabla u\rangle+(c+α)u=f \quad\text{ in }U, \quad\; u=0 \quad\text{on }\partial U, \] where $U\subset \mathbb{R}^d$ is bounded, $γ\in[1,\infty)$ is a constant, $φ\in H^{1,2}(U)\cap L^\infty(U)$, $\mathbf{H}\in L^p(U,\mathbb{R}^d)$ for some $p \in (d, \infty)$, and $c\in L^1(U)$ with $c\ge0$. Abstract: We develop an AI-assisted search-and-certification workflow for constructing Carleman weights for wave equations on semi-infinite domains. Abstract: This paper is concerned with the Cauchy problem for the one-dimensional bipolar Euler-Poisson equations with space-dependent damping, which arise in the hydrodynamic model of semiconductors. Abstract: This paper studies the spectral properties and nonlinear asymptotic stability of a family of finite-time self-similar blow-up solutions to the three-dimensional Keller--Segel system constructed by matching interior and exterior profiles within the framework of matched asymptotic expansions. For every sufficiently large matching index $n$, the full linearized operator around the stationary state $U_n$ in self-similar variables is analyzed on $L^2(\mathbb R^3)$. Sturm zero counting in the radial mode, a wave operator that reduces the nonlocal $l=1$ equation to a local equation, and a Mellin--Newton quadratic form for all $l\ge2$ show that, after the scaling and translation modes and the finitely many genuinely unstable radial modes are removed, the remaining spectrum is separated from the imaginary axis by a positive distance. In addition, the optimal lower bound on the real parts of the spectrum is $1/4$ in every mode $l\ge2$. On the stable subspace, an exponentially decaying semigroup and a modified energy equivalent to the $L^2$ norm are constructed, and the logarithmic asymptotic decay rate of the semigroup norm is proved to equal the stable spectral gap. Finally, modulation equations, $H^2$ energy estimates, control of the scaling derivative, and Brouwer's no-retraction theorem yield nonradial nonlinear asymptotic stability of the corresponding self-similar blow-up solutions after the initial coefficients in the finitely many unstable radial directions have been chosen suitably.

</details>

Abstract: Let $R\subset\mathbb{R}^d$ be a reduced root system, $G$ the associated finite reflection group, and $k\ge0$ a $G$-invariant multiplicity function. Abstract: We prove that the Dirichlet $p$-torsion function $u_p$ converges to the distance to the boundary $d$ at the rate $O(1/p)$ in the uniform norm, on every bounded domain and with a constant depending only on the dimension and the diameter. The upper bound comes from a radial barrier with its pole at a boundary point, which is an admissible comparison function for $p>n$ and needs no boundary regularity; the lower bound comes from an inscribed ball. On the ball the error equals $(1+\log n)/p+O(p^{-2})$, so the order $1/p$ cannot be improved in general. Abstract: We study Maxwell-Stefan diffusion with additive friction coefficients $f_{ij}=g_i+g_j$. In mass fractions, the system isolates the constrained pair-friction block; in mole fractions, it is the classical ideal isothermal/isobaric Maxwell-Stefan system at constant total molar concentration. Additivity makes the constrained pair-friction dissipation species-diagonal; conversely, species-diagonality on one interior barycentric constraint space forces a pair-sum representation. At operator level, the positive constrained relaxation operator is a scalar shift of a compression of $G=diag[g_1,\ldots,g_N]$. Its scalar resolvent yields both an explicit constrained inverse and interlacing spectral roots, which form global real-analytic coordinates on the open simplex and whose differentials are left eigen-covectors. In root coordinates the principal part is diagonal, no self-square gradient term occurs, and scalar comparison yields invariant rectangles and separation from the simplex boundary. For regularity we introduce entropy-stabilized one-sided multi-EPD truncations: Euler-Poisson-Darboux entropies cancel mixed quadratic production, while for $N\ge4$ a truncation-weighted mixing-entropy correction supplies transverse coercivity. The mixing entropy also symmetrizes the moment system; frozen conormal estimates give spatial Lipschitz bounds. Together with time Hölder control and short-interval maximal regularity, this yields global strong solvability on bounded $C^{2+α}$ domains with $0<α<1$, for each $N\ge2$, $d\ge2$, and $p>d+2$, for all uniformly positive, compatible initial concentrations in the natural trace class. Solutions become classical for positive times and converge exponentially to equilibrium in relative entropy, $L^2$, and $C^1(\barΩ)$.

Abstract: We investigate the existence and nonexistence of least energy solutions for a doubly critical Neumann problem with a critical absorption term. In particular, the absorption term has the same critical trace exponent as the boundary nonlinearity, but with the opposite sign and acting in the interior of $Ω$. Moreover, the problem also has a least energy solution at $α=α_0$ provided $α_0> C(n)\max_{\partial Ω} H$, where $C(n)$ is a positive constant depending only on $n$ and $H$ is the mean curvature on $\partialΩ$.

Finally we provide examples of domains for which synchronization of positive solutions fails to hold if the interaction matrix has at least two positive eigenvalues. Abstract: We study the initial-boundary value problem for the Short Wave equation on the half-line $x\ge 0$. This leads to different analyticity properties of the associated eigenfunctions and, consequently, to different spectral formulations in the regimes $u(0,t)\le0$ and $u(0,t)\ge0$.

Abstract: This monograph presents a systematic account of several classical and recent developments in the theory of rotating vortex patches, or V-states, for the two-dimensional Euler equations and related active scalar models. Its purpose is both expository and structural: we revisit some of the foundational results of the subject, provide detailed proofs and alternative formulations, and present more recent results within a common analytical framework. We begin with Euler vortex patches and the contour dynamics formulation of rigidly rotating solutions. We then develop two complementary approaches to the V-state equation, based on Cauchy integrals and conformal mappings, and discuss their connections with potential theory and Faber polynomials. These tools are used to revisit classical examples, including Rankine vortices and Kirchhoff ellipses, as well as rigidity and classification results. A substantial part is devoted to Burbea construction of noncircular V-states via bifurcation theory. The final part develops a unified approach to rotating patches for a broad class of incompressible active scalar equations. Structural properties of the interaction kernel, in particular complete monotonicity and the resulting spectral factorization, yield a common bifurcation framework encompassing the Euler, generalized surface quasi-geostrophic, quasi-geostrophic shallow-water, and related models.

Abstract: DNA replication requires the coordination of origin firing and fork progression to ensure the entire genome is timely duplicated before cell division. Yet origin firing is stochastic, giving rise to the classical random completion problem of how probabilistic local events can nevertheless ensure reliable genome duplication. Although several biological mechanisms have been proposed to resolve this problem, a quantitative account of how heterogeneous initiation and fork speed govern the persistence of the final unreplicated regions is still lacking. To address this gap, we introduce a population-level kinetic framework that extends KJMA nucleation-and-growth models by tracking unreplicated intervals over size, genomic position and time. We establish well-posedness of the resulting mean-field system and global existence for compatible data. Notably, by introducing a local initiation mass function, we quantify how the density and spatial organisation of origin firing constrain replication completion, yielding novel and sharp upper bounds on both the worst-locus unreplicated fraction and locuswise near-completion time. These results provide a rigorous and computable foundation for mapping vulnerabilities in replication completion and relating persistent unreplicated regions to replication stress and genome instability.

Abstract: We develop a constructive method for solving semilinear elliptic equations $Δu(x)=f(x,u(x))$ on locally finite, connected infinite graphs with layered structure. Using Eidelheit's theorem, we establish coupling criteria ensuring that arbitrary initial-layer data extend to global solutions for every $f$. In particular, $Δu=e^u$ has infinitely many solutions on $\mathbb Z^2$, but none of finite energy. Abstract: We prove global existence of mild solutions to the spatially inhomogeneous Fermi--Dirac BGK equation for arbitrary-size Pauli-admissible initial data with finite mass and kinetic energy, allowing both vacuum and locally saturated zero-temperature states. The solutions satisfy mass, momentum, and energy conservation and, under an additional finite spatial second-moment assumption, an entropy-gap H-theorem on the full Pauli interval. Abstract: We prove a pair of complementary small-data stability theorems describing the global future and past structure of classical solutions to the isentropic, spherically symmetric $3D$ compressible Euler equations. We allow any equation of state with positive sound speed, except that our shock-formation results do not apply to the Chaplygin gas.

The perturbed data have finite kinetic energy, and the perturbed density is equal to a positive constant plus a signed ``asymptotically flat'' tail. Our main theorem yields a complete description of the existence and uniqueness of the maximal globally hyperbolic development (MGHD) of the data. The boundary of the MGHD contains hypersurfaces that extend to spatial infinity on which our solutions develop gradient-singularities. This is the first MGHD existence-uniqueness-stability result for shock-forming solutions for any multi-$D$ quasilinear wave system. While prior works have yielded local portions of ``candidate'' MGHDs, neither the existence nor the uniqueness of an MGHD can be inferred from local considerations.
  Our second theorem considers analogous data with the opposite sign of the $1/r$ density tail. We prove global existence throughout spacetime, to both the future and past. The solution's asymptotic behavior towards null infinity is not linear, but rather is distorted by a logarithmic bending of the sound cones away from the flat Minkowski ones, leading to logarithmically enhanced dispersion. This is the first small-data global existence result in the entire spacetime for any $3D$ quasilinear wave system that fails to satisfy the null condition and the weak null condition. The main mechanism of stabilization is a global-in-spacetime rarefaction effect, tied to the $1/r$ tail.

</details>

Abstract: We study a fourth-order variational model for two-dimensional frustrated ferromagnets with competing exchange interactions and an applied magnetic field of strength $H>0$. For every $H>1/4$, we prove that the energy admits minimizers in the topological classes $Q=\pm1$ and that every minimizing sequence is precompact in $H^2$ modulo translations. The main difficulty is that spectral coercivity degenerates as $H\downarrow1/4$. Using a Helmholtz circle-mean identity, we prove that the residual energy of every nonzero-degree configuration has a uniform positive lower bound, even at the degenerate endpoint. Together with a sphere-valued $H^2$-splitting construction, this provides a threshold-stable binding inequality and yields compactness throughout the coercive regime. We also identify $H=1/4$ as the sharp spectral threshold. Below it the energy is unbounded from below, whereas at the threshold nonzero-degree configurations retain a positive energy barrier and degree-zero Weyl sequences lose compactness. Finally, in the strong-field regime, rescaled minimizers approach those minimizers of the limiting functional that maximize the Dirichlet energy, while topological-charge and normalized-energy measures concentrate on the $H^{-1/4}$-scale.

Motivation: 该研究的动机在于：已有文献中类似加权退化椭圆问题的右端通常不依赖解本身，且权重匹配；而本文考虑右端显式含解u且左右权重互不匹配的情形，这在数学上更具挑战性，需要新的技术处理以获得更高的可微性估计。

Method: 研究目标方程为 div(|x|^β(|Du|-1)^(p-1)_+ Du/|Du|) = |u|^(q-2)u/|x|^α，其中2≤p<n，n>2。作者考虑（p,q）类型带有双权重的非齐次源项。方法上，通过选取恰当依赖于梯度的函数（在单位球内消失），对其与梯度的复合建立更高可微性，并在适当的关于数据的假设下完成证明（典型的技术包括差分商方法、加权Sobolev嵌入以及退化椭圆的正则性技巧）。

Result: 在适当的假设下，作者得到了梯度与特定函数复合后的更高可微性结果，该函数在梯度的单位球内取零值。这一结果推广了此前文献中仅处理右端与u无关或权重匹配情况的相应结论。

Conclusion: 本文对右端显式依赖解u且左右权重不匹配的广泛退化椭圆方程，首次建立了相应的更高可微性结论，弥补了该方向研究中的空白。

Abstract: We consider local weak solutions of widely degenerate elliptic PDEs of the type \begin{equation}
  \label{equazione mia}
  \mathrm{div}\Biggl(|x|^β(|Du|-1)^{p-1}_+\frac{Du}{|Du|}\Biggr)=\frac{|u|^{q-2}u}{|x|^α} \ \ \text{ in }Ω,
  \end{equation} where $2\leq p<n$, $α,β>0$ are fixed exponents, $Ω$ is an open subset of $\mathbb{R}^n,$ that contains the origin, $n>2,$ and $( \ \cdot \ )_+$ stands for the positive part. We establish a higher differentiability result for the composition of the gradient with a suitable function that vanishes in the unit ball for the gradient, under appropriate assumptions on the datum. The novelty here with respect to previous papers on the subject is that the right-hand side explicitly depends on the solution $u$ and we have a mismatch between the weight on the left-hand side and the right-hand side.

</details>

*Jihong Zhao,Chao Deng*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NS-NPP方程描述了带电粒子在流体中的扩散与电场耦合的物理过程。以往关于此类方程的结果通常要求所有初值分量都满足小性条件，而本文试图放宽这一限制，仅要求粒子密度之差（N₀-P₀）与速度场u₀满足一个带有指数因子的条件，从而允许粒子密度之和（N₀+P₀）可以取任意大的值，扩展了全局适定性理论的适用范围。

Method: 本文采用Fujita-Kato型函数框架，基于Sobolev空间中的临界空间设置（Ṫ^{-1+d/2}和Ṫ^{-2+d/2}），通过构造适当的迭代方案并结合精细的能量估计，证明在初值满足所给的大初值条件（即带有指数型权重）下，解的局部存在性可以延拓为全局存在性。关键方法在于利用粒子密度之和的较高正则性释放了对其小性条件的要求。

Result: 本文成功证明了NS-NPP方程在满足给定大初值条件下存在唯一的全局解。这一结果的一个重要推论是：对于正负电荷粒子密度之和N₀+P₀，不需要施加任何小性假设，仅需对密度之差N₀-P₀与速度场施加适当条件，即可保证全局适定性。

Conclusion: 本文显著推广了NS-NPP方程现有的全局适定性理论，突破了以往要求所有初值分量同时小性的局限，为带电多组分流体的数学理论提供了新的重要进展。该结果也为后续研究更一般初值条件下的全局解打下基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the global well-posedness of the Cauchy problem for the NS-NPP equations with some large Fujita-Kato type initial data. Specifically, we show that there exist two positive constants $c_{0}$ and $C_{0}$ such that if the initial data $(u_{0}, N_{0}, P_{0})$ satisfies the following condition: \begin{equation*}
  \left(\|u_0\|_{\dot{H}^{-1+\frac{d}{2}}}+\|N_{0}-P_{0}\|_{\dot{H}^{-2+\frac{d}{2}}}\right)\exp\left\{C_{0}\big(\|N_{0}+P_{0}\|_{\dot{H}^{-2+\frac{d}{2}}}^2+1\big)\right\}
  \leq
  c_{0}, \end{equation*} then the NS-NPP equations admits a unique global solution. This result implies global existence of solutions without any smallness conditions imposed on the sum of initial particle densities of negative and positive electric charge in the framework of Sobolev spaces.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TDDFT是现代量子化学和凝聚态物理中计算电子结构的重要工具，但其数学基础尚不完善。Manfredi将TDDFT引入Vlasov方程框架，本文旨在为该框架提供严格的数学分析，特别是解决势-密度映射的可逆性和满射性这一核心数学问题。

Method: 采用数学分析的方法，在\mathbb{T}^d \times \mathbb{R}^d上的Vlasov-Poisson系统中，利用实解析性假设，建立势-密度映射的可逆性；通过逆问题求解方法，证明势-密度映射的满射性，即寻找产生给定密度轮廓的电场。

Result: 在外部势空间变量实解析的条件下，建立了Vlasov-Poisson系统势-密度映射的可逆性；在外部势时间上实解析的条件下，恢复了Runge-Gross型定理；并确立了势-密度映射的满射性。

Conclusion: 本文为Manfredi提出的含时Vlasov框架下的TDDFT提供了严格的数学基础，证明了势-密度映射在一定解析条件下的可逆性与满射性，为TDDFT在该框架中的理论合法性提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: We develop the mathematical analysis of the Time-Dependent Density Functional Theory introduced by Manfredi [J. Plasma Phys., 86.2 (2020), 825860201] in the setting of Vlasov equations with a time-dependent exterior potential. In this context, we establish the invertibility of the potential-density mapping for the Vlasov-Poisson system on $\mathbb{T}^d \times \mathbb{R}^d$ when the exterior potential is (real-)analytic in the space variable. We also recover a Runge-Gross type Theorem for the Vlasov-Poisson equation for smooth (real-)analytic in time exterior potential.
  The surjectivity of the potential-density mapping is an inverse problem for the Vlasov-Poisson equation: we find the electric field that generates a given density profile on a given time interval.

</details>

Method: 1) 引入针对张量化初始数据的调制Gibbs测度；2) 利用Wang-Zhao的一致N阶配分函数估计，证明调制Gibbs测度与参考张量化分布在归一化相对熵意义下的O(N^-1)接近性；3) 通过稳定性论证，将混沌传播性质从调制Gibbs测度扩展到张量化数据及其他初始测度；4) 结合Bresch-Jabin-Soler的加权BBGKY层次估计，在N独立的短时间区间上建立VPFP方程的无截断均值场极限。

Result: 该文首次实现了三维VPFP方程在N独立短时间区间上的无截断（cutoff-free）均值场极限与混沌传播定理，并通过相对熵框架将调制Gibbs测度上的结果稳定地推广到更广的初始分布类别。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种统一的相对熵-调制Gibbs测度-稳定性论证框架，解决了奇异排斥相互作用下随机牛顿系统无截断均值场极限的开放问题，为三维VPFP方程的高精度动力学结果奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We introduce a modulated Gibbs measure for the usual tensorized initial data for stochastic Newton's systems with singular repulsive interactions. Using the uniform-in-$N$ partition-function estimates of Wang--Zhao \cite{wang2026uniform}, we show that the modulated Gibbs measure and its tensorized reference are $O(N^{-1})$-close in normalized relative entropy. A stability argument then transfers propagation of chaos from modulated Gibbs measures to tensorized data and other initial laws at the same entropy scale. Combined with the weighted BBGKY estimates of Bresch--Jabin--Soler \cite{bresch2025new}, this yields the first cutoff-free mean-field limit and propagation of chaos for the three-dimensional Vlasov--Poisson--Fokker--Planck (VPFP) equation on an $N$-independent short-time interval.

</details>


### [88] [An Inverse Random Source Problem for the Moore-Gibson-Thompson Equation Driven by Fractional Brownian Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08643)

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了由分数布朗运动驱动的随机Moore-Gibson-Thompson方程的随机源反问题，证明了温和解的存在唯一性，并利用边界通量数据证明了当Hurts指数H∈(0,1)时，反演空间强度函数f_i(x)（已知时间函数g_i）和时间函数g_i(t)（已知空间函数f_i）的唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机源反问题在数学物理和工程应用中具有重要价值。Moore-Gibson-Thompson方程描述了粘弹性介质中的声波传播等物理现象，而分数布朗运动作为噪声驱动项能更真实地刻画非白噪声的随机扰动。如何从边界观测数据中唯一恢复随机源的时空分量是一个具有理论挑战性和实际意义的问题。

Method: 论文首先針對带分数布朗运动（Hurst指数H∈(0,1)）驱动的随机Moore-Gibson-Thompson方程，建立了温和解的存在唯一性理论。对于反演问题，利用特殊非空开子集上的边界通量作为观测数据，分别考察两种情形：当时间函数g_i已知时反演空间函数f_i，以及当空间函数f_i已知时反演时间函数g_i(t)，并证明了两类反问题的唯一性。

Result: (1)证明了由分数布朗运动驱动的随机Moore-Gibson-Thompson方程温和解的存在唯一性；(2)对于H∈(0,1)的所有情形，证明了在已知时间函数g_i时，空间强度函数f_i(x)可由边界通量唯一确定；(3)在已知空间函数f_i时，时间函数g_i(t)同样可由边界通量唯一确定。

Conclusion: 该工作将随机源反问题的研究从高斯白噪声情形拓展到了分数布朗运动驱动的情形，覆盖了Hurst指数H∈(0,1)的全部取值范围，建立了对应随机Moore-Gibson-Thompson方程反源问题的唯一性理论，为该类方程的进一步数值反演和应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider an inverse random source problem for the stochastic Moore-Gibson-Thompson equation driven by fractional Brownian motion with Hurst index $H \in (0, 1)$ of the form $f_1(x)g_1(t)\dot{B}^H(t)+f_2(x)g_2(t)$. Given the random source, existence and uniqueness of mild solutions are verified. For the inverse problem, the uniqueness of recovering the strength $f_i(x)$ if the time functions $g_i$ are known and $g_i(t)$ if the spatial functions $f_i$ are known when $H \in(0,1)$ from the boundary flux on a special nonempty open subset is proved.

</details>

*Tim Binz*

Main category: math.AP

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究CAO系统的长期动力学行为，分析其收敛性与衰减速率，填补非梯度、非局部耗散系统在有界域上全局多项式衰减结果的研究空白。

Result: CAO系统表现出多项式衰减速率且该速率是最优的，这一结论与一致抛物系统在有界域上典型的指数收敛截然不同；这种代数减速被证明是纯非线性效应，由非线性耦合条件诱导的慢流形（中心流形）所驱动。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为有界域上的非梯度、非局部耗散系统建立了全局多项式衰减结果，揭示了CAO系统的慢流形机制，深化了对非均匀抛物系统长期动力学行为的理解。

Abstract: This article is concerned with a definitive analysis of its long-term dynamics of the CAO-system. We establish the convergence of global strong solutions to constant equilibria in the full $H^1$-topology in the non-perturbative regime. Remarkably, we demonstrate that the CAO-system exhibits a polynomial rate of decay and we determine the optimal rate. This phenomenon stands in stark contrast to the exponential convergence typically expected for uniformly parabolic systems on bounded domains. This algebraic slowing is shown to be a purely nonlinear effect, driven by the emergence of a slow manifold (center manifold) induced by nonlinear coupling conditions. To the best of our knowledge, this provides the first instance of a global polynomial decay result for a non-gradient, non-local dissipative system on a bounded domain.

</details>


### [90] [Shape optimization of light structures with general Hooke's laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08732)
*Dimitrios Andreakis,Flaviana Iurlano,Filip Rindler*

TL;DR: 本文证明了Bouchitté关于最小合规性形状优化问题的猜想在一般情况下的成立：将松弛结果从各向同性二次能量密度（二维和三维）扩展到任意各向同性或各向异性弹性张量和任意维度，并在一般情形下填补了之前工作依赖显式公式的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的研究（Duke Math. J. 2023）证明了近似最优形状在体积趋于零时收敛于广义形状（表示为可能弥散的概率测度），该广义形状最小化light-structures合规泛函。但该结果仅适用于最简单的各向同性二次能量密度（Frobenius范数平方）且仅限于二维和三维，而一般情形由于缺乏松弛被积函数的显式公式（Allaire, Kohn和Strang的结果），需要全新的论证方法。本文旨在完全解决Bouchitté猜想。

Method: 由于一般情形下松弛被积函数没有显式公式可用，作者提出了若干新的论证工具并对原方法进行了实质修改。具体包括：引入不依赖显式公式的新论证方式来建立松弛泛函的表示，同时大幅简化和流线化原始论证的其他部分，并澄清了文献中某些相关陈述。

Result: 将松弛结果推广到了完整的（各向同性或各向异性的）弹性张量类别以及任意维度，从而在完全一般性下证明了Bouchitté的猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了Bouchitté猜想，确认了light-structures合规泛函的松弛表示在一般各向异性弹性和任意维度下均成立，并提供了比原方法更简化的论证路径。

Abstract: For the minimum-compliance shape optimization problem it was established recently [\textit{Duke Math.\ J.} 172 (2023), 43--103] that a sequence of approximately optimal shapes of exact volume \(\e\) converges to a limiting generalized shape, which is represented by a possibly diffuse probability measure, as \(\e \todown 0\). This generalized shape minimizes the so-called light-structures compliance functional, which --- as conjectured by Bouchitté --- is expressed in terms of a relaxed integrand, reflecting that optimal shapes must only involve ``lower-dimensional'' structures, like the Michell-type trusses that are well known in the engineering domain. However, the aforementioned result only applies for the simplest isotropic quadratic energy density, that is, the squared Frobenius norm, in dimensions two and three. The present work extends the relaxation result to the entire class of (isotropic or anisotropic) elasticity tensors and to any dimension, thus solving Bouchitté's conjecture in full generality. Since the previous work relied on explicit formulas for the relaxed integrands (due to Allaire, Kohn, and Strang), which are not available in the general case, several new arguments and substantial modifications of the original approach are required. We also significantly simplify and streamline other parts of the original argument, in addition to clarifying some related statements in the literature.

</details>


### [91] [Transport and flow for horizontal Sobolev contact velocities in Carnot groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08738)
*Gianluca Somma,Simone Verzellesi,Davide Vittone*

TL;DR: 本文在Carnot群上研究了由接触向量场驱动的输运方程和流方程的适定性问题，速度场仅需沿水平方向（由群的层状几何决定）具有Sobolev正则性。此前在更广泛的次黎曼框架中，此类结果仅在Heisenberg群上已知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在次黎曼几何（sub-Riemannian geometry）背景下，输运方程和流方程的速度场通常只沿水平方向具有正则性，而垂直方向可能不光滑。此前的适定性结果仅限于Heisenberg群，作者希望将其推广到更一般的Carnot群上。

Method: 作者的方法依赖于DiPerna-Lions在欧几里得空间中提出的重整化解（renormalized solutions）理论，将其推广到Carnot群上的接触向量场情形。

Result: 在Carnot群上，对具有水平Sobolev正则性的接触向量场所驱动的输运方程和流方程，建立了新的适定性结果，将先前仅在Heisenberg群上已知的结果推广到了更一般的层状群结构。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在Carnot群上，水平Sobolev正则的接触向量场足以保证输运方程和流方程的适定性，显著扩展了次黎曼框架下此类问题的已知结果范围。

Abstract: We establish new well-posedness results for transport and flow equations driven by contact vector fields on Carnot groups. The velocity fields are assumed to have horizontal Sobolev regularity, namely Sobolev regularity only along the horizontal directions determined by the stratified geometry of the group. In the broader sub-Riemannian setting, results of this type were previously known only for Heisenberg groups. Our proof relies on the theory of renormalized solutions as originally introduced by DiPerna and Lions in the Euclidean setting.

</details>


### [92] [Low-Temperature Large Deviations for the Two-Dimensional Wick-Ordered Cubic Wave Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08829)
*Daniel Heydecker*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了二维Wick排序散焦三次非线性波动方程在低温极限下的轨迹大偏差原理，初始数据取自不变Gibbs测度；并进一步表明，具有相同静态速率函数的渐近消失高频扰动会生成连续的互不相同的质量偏移轨迹速率函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究低温和低正则情形下非线性波动方程的动力学大偏差行为，理解Gibbs测度初始数据下系统在低温极限的宏观涨落规律，特别是高频扰动如何影响轨迹级别的速率函数。

Method: 采用轨迹大偏差原理（trajectory large deviation principle）的框架，在低温极限下对二维Wick排序散焦三次非线性波动方程进行分析，结合不变Gibbs测度的性质，通过考察高频扰动导致的静态速率函数不变而轨迹速率函数发生质量偏移现象来刻画系统的涨落行为。

Conclusion: 该结果表明在二维Wick排序三次非线性波动方程中，微小的高频初始扰动可在动力学层面引起不可忽略的大偏差行为变化，体现了轨迹级速率函数对高频结构的敏感性以及质量偏移的非唯一性特征。

Abstract: We prove a trajectory large deviation principle, in the low-temperature limit, for the two-dimensional Wick-ordered defocusing cubic nonlinear wave equation, with initial data drawn from the invariant Gibbs measure. We further show that asymptotically vanishing high-frequency perturbations of the Gibbs initial data, with the same static rate function, generate a continuum of distinct mass-shifted trajectory rate functions.

</details>

Abstract: We study modified scattering for the two-dimensional defocusing nonlinear Schrödinger equation (NLS) with the gauge-invariant, scattering-critical nonlinearity $|u|u$. For radial data, we prove convergence in $L^p(\R^2)$ for every $2<p<\infty$.

Method: 论文的核心方法是建立一个新的能量估计E(ψ)≤C_J(I_c(ψ)+ind(ψ))，其中I_c为速度c下的作用量，ind为实Morse指标。该估计在任意紧区间J⊂(0,√2)内一致成立。在此基础上，论文还证明了有限气泡紧致性结果，包括能量、作用量、势能和动量的分裂性质，并利用这些工具在Morse指标至多为1的非平凡波中建立作用量的可达性。

Result: 对每个亚音速c∈(0,√2)，证明了二维Gross–Pitaevskii方程存在有限能量行波解，彻底解决了两维度中给定速度行波解存在性的长期开放问题。同时建立了有限气泡紧致性和作用量的可达性等重要结构性质。

Abstract: For every subsonic speed $c\in(0,\sqrt2)$, we prove the existence of a finite-energy traveling wave for the planar Gross--Pitaevskii equation. This resolves the longstanding problem of the existence of prescribed-speed traveling-wave solutions in two dimensions, explicitly stated as open by Mariş (Ann. of Math., 2013) and Bellazzini and Ruiz (Amer. J. Math., 2023). The proof relies essentially on the energy estimate \begin{equation*}
  E(ψ)\le C_J\bigl(I_c(ψ)+\ind(ψ)\bigr),
  \qquad c\in J, \end{equation*} where $E$ is the energy, $I_c$ the action at speed $c$, $\ind$ the real Morse index, $J$ is any compact interval contained in $(0,\sqrt2)$, and $C_J$ is a positive constant depending only on $J$. We also prove finite-bubble compactness, including splitting of the energy, action, potential energy, and momentum, and attainment of the action among nonconstant waves of Morse index at most one.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 本文采用解析与变分相结合的方法：(1) 对具有循环对称的可容许核壳几何，证明存在唯一正涂层电导率使结构对所有均匀入射场弱中性；(2) 在严格星形对称域生成的位似多层（不要求接近同心圆）上，建立可局部唯一地消隐完整K-CGPT块的电导率；(3) 用"小核条件"替代"近背景条件"，覆盖有限、绝缘及完美导电核；(4) 通过耦合界面模式与涂层电导率，在径向GPT消隐结构附近构造无旋转/反射对称的精确族；(5) 对三层结构给出严格可验证的非退化条件与手性示例；最后以数值算例加以佐证。

Result: 理论结果包括：均匀入射场下弱中性的唯一涂层电导率存在性定理；高阶K-CGPT块的局部唯一消隐电导率；基于小核条件在有限、绝缘和完美导电核情形下的推广；无旋转与反射对称的精确消隐族的构造以及三层手性结构的严格认证。数值实验完全验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 该工作将GPT消隐理论从近似、近圆情形推广至精确、非圆、非对称的一般情形，建立了完整的数学框架，并证明由此产生的消隐可实现对所有全调和入射场的增强近隐身，为该领域提供了统一的理论基础与构造方法。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with exact high-order generalized polarization tensor (GPT) cancellation in two-dimensional noncircular multilayers with finite positive isotropic coating conductivities. We first prove that, for every admissible cyclically symmetric core--shell geometry, there exists a unique positive coating conductivity for which the structure is weakly neutral to every uniform incident field. For higher orders, we establish locally unique coating conductivities that cancel the complete \(K\)-contracted GPTs (K-CGPTs) block on a broad class of fixed noncircular multilayers with cyclic symmetry. This includes homothetic multilayers generated by strictly star-shaped symmetric domains, without assuming that the geometry is close to concentric disks. We also replace the near-background condition by a small-core condition covering finite, insulating, and perfectly conducting cores. By varying selected interface modes together with the coating conductivities near a radial GPT-vanishing structure, we further construct exact families with neither rotational nor reflection symmetry. For three coatings, the relevant nondegeneracy conditions and an explicit chiral example are rigorously certified. Finally, we show that the resulting cancellation yields enhanced near-cloaking for every entire harmonic incident field. A set of certified numerical examples fully corroborates the theoretical predictions established in this work.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半线性波动方程的反问题在医学成像、地球物理等领域有重要应用。尽管逆问题的唯一性研究已被广泛开展，但关于同时恢复时间无关阻尼、线性势和非线性势系数的稳定性估计此前尚无结果，而这对于实际数值重构的可靠性至关重要。

Method: 将二阶线性化与适当构造的几何光学解和渐近解相结合，从Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射中提取关于各系数的信息，并在适当的先验界条件下推导出每种系数的Hölder型稳定性估计。

Result: 在合适的先验条件下，建立了同时恢复半线性波动方程中三种系数（时间无关阻尼、线性势、非线性势）的Hölder型稳定性估计。据作者所知，这是半线性波动方程中同时确定这三种系数的首个稳定性结果。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了半线性波动方程反问题稳定性分析领域的空白，为同时重构多个物理参数提供了理论保证，也为后续数值算法的设计提供了坚实的数学基础。

Abstract: We consider an inverse problem for a semilinear wave equation with time-independent damping, linear potential, and nonlinear potential coefficients in a bounded domain of $\mathbb{R}^n$ for $n\geq 2$. The main objective is to establish stability estimates for the simultaneous recovery of these coefficients from the associated Dirichlet-to-Neumann map. Our approach combines second-order linearization with suitably constructed geometric optics and asymptotic solutions. We establish Hölder-type stability estimates for the recovery of each of the three coefficients appearing in the semilinear wave equation under suitable a priori bounds on these coefficients. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first stability result for simultaneous determination of time-independent damping, linear and nonlinear potentials in a semiliner wave equation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究调和映射/能量极小映射到具有非光滑边界流形（特别是带边界的目标区域）的正则性问题。此前关于目标为正则流形的理论已成熟，而针对目标带有C^{1,1}以下正则性边界的紧致区域，极小映射的极值正则性尚未被精确刻画。此工作旨在确定目标边界在Hölder尺度下的最优化正则条件，以保证极小映射的C^{1,1}正则性。

Abstract: Let $K\subset\R^m$ be the closure of a bounded domain whose boundary is a compact embedded hypersurface, and let \(
  \overline M=\R^m\setminus\operatorname{int}K \) be the allowed target. We prove that if $\partial K$ is of class $C^{1,1}$, then every $\overline M$-valued local minimizer of the Dirichlet energy $E(u;B):=\int_B|Du|^2\dd x$ is locally $W^{2,\infty}$, and hence locally $C^{1,1}$, on its continuity set. For every $0<α<1$, we also construct a convex body with $C^{1,α}$ boundary and an everywhere continuous global
  minimizer that is not $C^{1,1}$. Thus $C^{1,1}$ is the sharp target
  regularity threshold in the Hölder scale.

</details>


### [98] [A growth gap for anisotropic minimal graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08972)
*Yang Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了各向异性极小图梯度增长的下界间隙：对于任意维度n≥2和任意光滑一致椭圆参数积分Φ，任何光滑非仿射的完整Φ-极小图u:R^n→R都满足sup_{B_R^n}|Du|≥cR^α，其中指数α>0仅依赖于n和Φ。该结果解决了Mooney和作者提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极小曲面理论中，理解完整极小图在无穷远处的梯度行为是一个基本问题。对于欧氏面积积分，已有经典的Bernstein型结果和梯度增长估计，但对于一般的各向异性积分，非仿射极小图是否必须在线性增长上"浪费"某些梯度增长尚不清楚。作者希望在没有Φ接近欧氏面积积分假设的情况下，在所有维度证明梯度的增长间隙，从而回答非仿射极小图能否以任意慢的梯度增长趋向无穷的问题。

Method: 论文通过建立梯度增长的间隙定理来证明：若梯度增长慢于o(R^α)，则解必为仿射的。关键指数α=α(n,Φ)>0依赖于维度和积分。证明在统一椭圆参数积分的一般框架下进行，不依赖Φ与欧氏面积的接近性，利用各向异性极小图的几何与分析性质构造所需的矛盾论证。

Result: 证明了对于任意n≥2和任何光滑一致椭圆参数积分Φ，存在仅依赖n和Φ的正指数α，使得任何非仿射的完整Φ-极小图在半径R的球上梯度上确界至少以cR^α增长。梯度增长o(R^α)蕴含解为仿射的（平坦性），验证了Mooney和作者的猜想。该结果对所有维度成立，且没有对Φ接近欧氏面积积分做任何假设。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了各向异性极小图梯度增长的一个普适间隙现象：非仿射完整极小图的梯度不可能比R^α增长得更慢。这一结论在一般各向异性积分设置下、所有维度中成立，是向理解各向异性Bernstein问题迈出的重要一步。

Abstract: We prove a growth gap for the gradient of entire anisotropic minimal graphs. For each $n\geq2$ and each smooth uniformly elliptic parametric integrand $Φ$ on $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$, there is an exponent $α=α(n,Φ)>0$ such that every smooth nonaffine entire $Φ$-minimal graph $u:\mathbb{R}^n\to\mathbb{R}$ satisfies $\sup_{B_R^n}|Du|\geq cR^α$ for all $R\geq R_0$, for some constants $c>0$ and $R_0<\infty$ depending on the solution. In particular, gradient growth $o(R^α)$ forces flatness, resolving a conjecture of Mooney and the author. The result holds in every dimension, without any assumption that $Φ$ is close to the Euclidean area integrand.

</details>


### [99] [Non-uniqueness for the Complex Ginzburg-Landau Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08985)
*Joel Dahne,Vladimír Šverák*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了三维三次复数Ginzburg-Landau方程从光滑初值发展出的奇异性会在爆破时刻之后导致解的非唯一性。这种非唯一性源于线性化算子围绕前向自相似剖面存在不稳定特征值，作者通过计算机辅助证明在特定参数值下建立了该特征值的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作部分受到Navier-Stokes方程中猜想行为的启发。复数Ginzburg-Landau方程与Navier-Stokes方程共享若干重要性质，如能量不等式和尺度对称性，因此研究该方程中奇异性引发的非唯一性可能为理解Navier-Stokes方程中类似现象提供线索。

Method: 作者研究从后向自相似解开始的解，其奇异性产生的非唯一性来源于围绕前向自相似线性化算子的不稳定特征值。通过计算机辅助证明（computer-assisted proof），针对方程中特定参数值建立了该不稳定特征值的存在性，从而证明了非唯一性。

Result: 在三维三次复数Ginzburg-Landau方程中，光滑初值产生的奇异性可在爆破时刻之后引发解的非唯一性。非唯一性由线性化算子的不稳定特征值所导致，该特征值的存在性已通过计算机辅助证明在特定参数值下得到严格验证。

Conclusion: 该研究表明复数Ginzburg-Landau方程在爆破后解的非唯一性确实可以发生，为非唯一性现象提供了严格证明。由于与Navier-Stokes方程的相似性，这可能为研究Navier-Stokes方程中类似的行为提供重要参考。

Abstract: We show that singularities of the three-dimensional cubic complex Ginzburg-Landau equation developing from smooth initial data can induce non-uniqueness of solutions after the time of blowup. The solutions we study start as backward self-similar solutions, and the source of the non-uniqueness is an unstable eigenvalue of the linearization around the forward self-similar profile generated by the singularity. We establish the existence of this eigenvalue, and hence the non-uniqueness, by a computer-assisted proof for specific values of the parameters in the equation. The work is in part motivated by similarities to conjectured behavior for the Navier-Stokes equation, with which the complex Ginzburg-Landau equation shares several important properties, such as an energy inequality and the scaling symmetry.

</details>


### [100] [Well-posedness and exponential stability for abstract evolution equations with delay in the nonlinear source: frictional and viscoelastic cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08986)
*Houria Chellaoua,Yamna Boukhatem,Cristina Pignotti*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类带常数时滞的半线性中立型抽象演化方程的适定性与指数稳定性，考虑了摩擦阻尼（CC* u_t）和粘弹性记忆阻尼两种机制，在适当假设下证明了小初值问题的全局适定性和解的指数衰减，并通过具体例子加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中立型系统广泛出现在结构力学、地震隔离和控制理论等领域，但带时滞的半线性中立型抽象演化方程的全局适定性和稳定性分析缺乏统一的理论框架，需要建立一般化的数学结果。

Method: 结合半群理论、能量估计、Duhamel公式和Gronwall型不等式等工具，对两类阻尼机制（CC* u_t形式的摩擦阻尼和带记忆的粘弹性阻尼）下的方程进行分析，研究其全局适定性和小初值解的指数衰减。

Result: 在适当的假设条件下，成功证明了方程的全局适定性与解的指数衰减性，适用于小初值情形，并通过具体实例验证了抽象结果的适用性。

Conclusion: 该结果为一类广泛的中立型系统提供了统一的分析框架，对结构力学、地震隔离及控制理论中的相关动力学问题具有重要的理论意义和应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the well-posedness and exponential stability of a class of semilinear neutral abstract evolution equations with constant time delay. Two damping mechanisms are considered: frictional damping of the form $CC^*u_t$ and viscoelastic damping with memory. Under suitable assumptions, we prove global well-posedness and exponential decay of solutions for sufficiently small initial data. This result is of considerable importance, as it provides a unified framework for the analysis of a wide class of neutral systems arising in structural mechanics, seismic isolation, and control theory. The abstract results are illustrated by some concrete examples. The analysis relies on a combination of semigroup theory, energy estimates, Duhamel's formula, and Gronwall-type arguments.

</details>


### [101] [Controllability for 2D water waves: effects of bottom topography and constant vorticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09014)
*E. Haus,S. Pasquali*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了重力与表面张力作用下二维水波的可控性问题。对于有限水深且底部地形一般的无旋波，证明了在$H^{s+1/2}(\mathbb{T})$空间中的一个开且稠密的底部地形集合上可控性成立。对于平底条件下的常涡量波，也证明了可控性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水波控制问题是流体力学与偏微分方程控制理论中的核心问题。已有结果多假设底部为平底，而实际海洋/流体域中底部地形往往是一般的。此外，允许的底部地形并非平底的小扰动，这引入了本质性的技术困难——因为一般底部地形下静止自由界面上Dirichlet-Neumann算子的特征值不具有显式形式，低频下也不一定接近平底情形，迫切需要克服这些困难建立可控性。

Method: 论文采用以下方法：(1) 将可控性问题通过对偶性转化为可观测性估计；(2) 利用Dirichlet-Neumann算子的谱特性进行模态分解；(3) 由于一般底部地形下特征值非显式且不接近平底情形的特征值，需要发展更精细的Ingham型估计来证明可观测性；(4) 通过限制底部地形属于$H^{s+1/2}(\mathbb{T})$中的一个开且稠密集合（不接触自由表面）来克服低频率下特征值不确定性带来的困难；(5) 对常涡量波的情况，在平底地形下分别论证可控性。

Result: (1) 对一般底部地形（非平底小扰动）下的二维重力-表面张力无旋水波，在底部地形的某个开且稠密集合上获得了可控性结果；(2) 对常涡量波的平底情形也证明了可控性结果。证明过程中克服了特征值非显式带来的难题，并给出了适用于此类问题的推广Ingham型估计。

Conclusion: 该工作将水波可控性理论从平底情形推广到一般底部地形，虽然仅对开且稠密的底部地形成立而非全部地形，但这反映了谱方法在一般几何下的内在限制，是一个自然的且技术上本质的推广。

Abstract: In this paper we consider two-dimensional water waves, under the action of gravity and surface tension. We prove a controllability result for irrotational waves in a fluid domain with finite depth and general bottom topography. The result holds for an open and dense set of bottom topographies in $H^{s+1/2}(\mathbb{T})$ (where $s$ is sufficiently large) that do not touch the free surface. We point out that the bottom topographies that we allow for are not necessarily small perturbations of the flat bottom case: this leads to many technical difficulties, since the eigenvalues of the Dirichlet-Neumann operator at a still free surface with general bottom topography are not explicit, nor are they necessarily close (for low frequencies) to the eigenvalues of the corresponding operator for the flat bottom case. In turn, this leads to a more involved argument to prove Ingham-type estimates, which are needed to prove observability, and motivates the restriction mentioned above on the admissible bottom topographies.
  We also prove a controllability result for waves with constant vorticity in a fluid domain with flat bottom topography.

</details>


### [102] [Speed and stability of segregated waves in a pressure-based model of heterogeneous cell populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09043)
*Carles Falcó,Rebecca M. Crossley,Martina Conte,Tommaso Lorenzi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究异构细胞群体（增殖性与非增殖性细胞具有不同迁移率）的最小基于压力的模型，该模型由反应-交叉-扩散方程组描述。作者将系统重新表述为广义多孔-Fisher方程的自由边界问题，推导出波速的几乎显式变分界；分析了分离波的稳定性，证明仅当非增殖性细胞迁移率大于增殖性细胞时波才稳定；最后通过渐近形状扰动分析研究了不可压缩分离圆形波的稳定性，揭示了指状不稳定性的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型已知允许一维行波解，且组分严格分离（非增殖细胞占据前缘有限区域，增殖细胞位于后方），但波的传播速度、参数依赖性和稳定性此前尚未被充分理解。理解这些性质对揭示组织生长和肿瘤侵袭中的细胞迁移模式具有重要意义。

Method: 1) 将反应-交叉-扩散方程组重新表述为广义多孔-Fisher方程的自由边界问题；2) 推导波速的几乎显式变分上/下界，适用于一般压力律和生长动力学；3) 通过线性稳定性分析研究一维分离波的稳定性；4) 通过渐近形状扰动分析研究不可压缩分离圆形波的界面稳定性，推导压力、界面速度和角模态增长率的显式表达式。

Result: 1) 获得的波速估计与数值模拟结果高度吻合，并在不可压缩极限下恢复完全显式的波速特征，估计变得尖锐；2) 一维分离波仅当非增殖性细胞比增殖性细胞迁移率更高时才是稳定的；3) 二维圆形波的稳定性并非仅由迁移率系数的相对大小决定，无论哪种细胞类型具有更大迁移率，都可能出现指状不稳定性。

Conclusion: 作者系统地阐明了异构细胞群体分离行波的速度、参数依赖性和稳定性，发现一维与圆形（二维）波在稳定性判据上存在本质差异：一维波的稳定性仅取决于迁移率相对大小，而二维圆形波的稳定性还依赖于几何与模态效应，可能引发指状不稳定性的产生。

Abstract: We consider a minimal pressure-based model of heterogeneous cell populations consisting of proliferative and non-proliferative cells with different mobilities. The model is formulated as a system of reaction--cross--diffusion equations describing the spatio-temporal dynamics of the cell densities. The model is known to admit one-dimensional travelling wave solutions with strictly segregated components: non-proliferative cells occupy a finite region at the leading edge, while proliferative cells remain at the rear. However, the speed, parameter dependence, and stability of these waves remain poorly understood. In this work, we derive an almost explicit variational bound on the wave speed by reformulating the problem as a free-boundary problem for a generalised porous--Fisher equation. The estimates we obtain apply to general pressure laws and growth kinetics, agree closely with the results of numerical simulations, and become sharp in the incompressible limit, where we formally recover a fully explicit characterisation of the wave speed. We then analyse the stability of the waves to show that segregated waves are stable only when non-proliferative cells are more mobile than proliferative cells. Finally, motivated by numerical observations of finger-like protrusions, we investigate the stability of incompressible segregated circular waves through asymptotic shape-perturbation analysis. This yields explicit expressions for the pressure, interface velocity, and growth rates of angular modes, thereby making evident the destabilisation mechanisms that may lead to the emergence of fingering instability. Interestingly, we find that, in contrast with the one-dimensional case, the stability of such circular waves is not determined solely by the relative value of the mobility coefficients, and thus instabilities may arise irrespective of which cell type has the larger mobility.

</details>


### [103] [Pathwise Global-in-Time Existence for the generalised KPZ Equation in the Full Subcritical Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09096)
*Jonas Sauer,Rhys Steele*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过路径方式证明了广义KPZ方程在全次临界范围内的全局时间适定性，核心创新在于仅需L^∞初值而非通常的C^θ初值，通过对确定性轮廓展开并引入积分因子去除临界缩放项来处理初始层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义KPZ方程在次临界范围内的全局适定性尚未完全解决，现有的策略通常依赖于对supremum范数的控制来延续解，但这需要C^θ(θ>0)初值条件。作者希望放宽到仅L^∞初值，这在处理初始层时带来了新的挑战。

Method: 采用路径方式证明，推广了广义Parabolic Anderson Model中使用的策略。关键新增步骤是：1) 对仅具有L^∞初值的gKPZ进行初始层处理；2) 围绕确定性轮廓进行展开；3) 引入积分因子以去除在时间0处具有临界缩放尺度的项。教学中先在标准(1+1)维KPZ方程上演示技术，再推广到更复杂的广义KPZ情形。

Result: 在完整的次临界区域中建立了广义KPZ方程的全局时间适定性（路径方式证明），成功将初值条件从C^θ放宽至L^∞。

Conclusion: 通过确定性展开与积分因子技术，本文提供了一种处理L^∞初值下初始层的有效方法，为广义KPZ方程在次临界范围内的全局适定性提供了新的路径证明框架。

Abstract: We provide a pathwise proof of global-in-time well-posedness for the generalised KPZ equation in the full subcritical regime by an adaptation of the strategy recently applied to the generalised Parabolic Anderson Model in [ES26]. Since this strategy relies crucially on the assumption that control of the supremum norm is sufficient to continue the solution, the main additional ingredient required is a treatment of the initial layer for gKPZ with merely $L^\infty$ initial data, rather than the more usual setting of $C^θ$ initial data with $θ> 0$. Our approach is based on an expansion around a deterministic profile followed by the introduction of an integrating factor in order to remove the terms which have critical scaling at time $0$. For pedagogical purposes, we first demonstrate the proof techniques in the case of the standard $(1+1)$-dimensional KPZ equation before turning to the more computationally involved case of generalised KPZ.

</details>


### [104] [Differentiability of Lipschitz mappings into metric spaces: area and co-area formulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09121)
*Behnam Esmayli,Paweł Goldstein,Piotr Hajłasz*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文对从欧几里得空间的集合到任意度量空间的Lipschitz映射，系统阐述了度量可微性理论，并建立了相应的面积公式和余面积公式。其核心创新在于提出了一个面向度量空间的新的度量隐函数定理，并利用该定理给出了Lipschitz映射到度量空间的余面积公式的直接几何证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统余面积公式通常针对目标空间为欧几里得空间或具有一定光滑结构的流形来建立。当映射的目标空间是任意度量空间（可能非光滑、无限维或分形）时，经典分析方法不再适用，需要发展适用于度量空间框架的可微性理论与积分几何公式。

Method: 首先给出自包含的度量可微性理论的阐述，涵盖了从欧几里得空间中的集合到任意度量空间的Lipschitz映射。在此基础上，作者创新性地提出了一个度量隐函数定理，该定理适用于值域为度量空间的映射。随后利用该隐函数定理对余面积公式给出直接且具有几何直观性的证明。

Result: 建立了面向任意度量空间的Lipschitz映射的度量可微性理论及配套的面积公式与余面积公式；提出了新的度量隐函数定理，并通过该定理获得了余面积公式的几何化证明。

Conclusion: 该工作通过引入度量隐函数定理这一新工具，为Lipschitz映射到度量空间的余面积公式提供了直接而几何的证明路径，丰富了度量空间上的分析工具。

Abstract: We give a self-contained exposition of metric differentiability for Lipschitz mappings from sets in Euclidean spaces into arbitrary metric spaces, together with the corresponding area and co-area formulas. The novelty is a new metric implicit function theorem for mappings into metric spaces, which is then used to give a direct and geometric proof of the co-area formula for Lipschitz mappings into metric spaces.

</details>

### [105] [A class of port-Hamiltonian systems with general time delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05726)
*Rogelio Arancibia Bustos,Bálint Farkas,Birgit Jacob,Merlin Schmitz*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文定义了一类无限维线性端口哈密顿时滞系统，给出了该类系统的刻画条件，并提供了易于验证的充分条件以判定系统是否属于该类，从而推广了Breiten、Hinsen和Unger于2024年在IEEE Trans. Autom. Control上发表的研究成果。该结果基于线性时滞偏微分方程的算子半群理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展已有研究中关于端口哈密顿时滞系统的理论框架，从有限维或特定情形推广到更一般的无限维线性情形，并提供易于实际应用验证的条件。

Method: 采用算子半群理论来处理线性时滞偏微分方程，以此作为数学工具对无限维线性端口哈密顿时滞系统进行定义和刻画。

Result: 给出了一类无限维线性端口哈密顿时滞系统的完整定义、刻画判据，以及一个易于验证的充分条件。

Conclusion: 研究成果推广了Breiten、Hinsen和Unger的工作，为无限维线性端口哈密顿时滞系统的识别与验证提供了更具操作性的理论工具。

Abstract: We define a class of infinite dimensional linear port-Hamiltonian delay systems, give a characterization for such systems, and provide an easily verifiable sufficient condition to belong to this class, thereby extending the study by Breiten, Hinsen and Unger in [IEEE Trans. Autom. Control vol. 69, no. 12 (2024), pp. 8924-8930, doi: 10.1109/TAC.2024.3464332]. The results rely on the operator semigroup theoretic treatment of linear delay PDEs.

</details>


### [106] [Can one feel the existence of a non-trivial invariant measure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05735)
*Yannic Steenbeck*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明在复可分希尔伯特空间上，具有非平凡不变概率测度的有界线性映射不一定有特征值，解决了Flytzanis（1995）提出的问题以及Grivaux–López-Martínez（2023）和Grivaux–Matheron–Menet（2021）明确提出的疑问。同时证明了一个正面结果：在可分复巴拿赫空间上，若存在非固定不变概率测度的有界线性算子，则单位圆上除1以外的某点必属于其近似点谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在回答一个长期悬而未决的问题：一个具有非平凡不变概率测度的线性算子是否一定具有特征值。此前Flytzanis在1995年提出相关问题，后由Grivaux等人分别在2021和2023年明确表述。回答此问题对理解算子谱理论与遍历理论之间的关系具有重要意义。

Method: 作者首先通过构造反例表明在希尔伯特空间中，存在非平凡不变概率测度的有界线性映射可以完全没有特征值，从而给出问题的否定回答；随后利用谱分析和近似点谱的相关工具，证明了在更一般的可积巴拿赫空间背景下，存在非固定不变概率测度的算子必然在单位圆上除1以外的某点具有近似点谱这一正面结论。

Result: 主要结果为两个：一是否定了"有非平凡不变概率测度⇒有特征值"这一猜想，给出了无反例；二是建立了正面定理，即此类算子在单位圆上（扣除1）必有某个近似点谱点，表明不变概率测度的存在性仍对算子的谱结构施加了强力的约束。

Conclusion: 该工作完整回应了Flytzanis及Grivaux等人提出的问题，明确区分了特征值的存在性与近似点谱位置之间的关系：特征值不必然存在，但近似点谱在单位圆上的位置受到不变测度存在性的限制。这一结果深化了对线性算子的不变测度与谱性质间联系的理解。

Abstract: It is shown that a bounded linear map on a complex separable Hilbert space with non-trivial invariant probability measures doesn't have to possess eigenvalues. This resolves a question indicated by Flytzanis in 1995 and concretely asked by Grivaux--López-Martínez from 2023 resp. Grivaux--Matheron--Menet from 2021. Still, as a positive result, we prove that every bounded linear operator on a separable complex Banach space for which a non-fixing invariant probability measure exists, has to have some approximate point spectrum on the unit circle minus $1$.

</details>


### [107] [Quasi-compactness and uniform stabilization on general Banach spaces under $θ$-subordinate perturbations of semigroup generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05831)
*Shri Lal Raghudev Ram Singh,Roberto Guglielmi*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了在一般Banach空间上，解析半群在生成元的θ-次协调扰动下保持拟紧性，并在此基础上建立了均匀指数稳定性的永久性，随后将结果推广到Crandall-Pazy半群和立即可微半群，并给出反例说明更大类扰动的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究扰动下半群稳定性理论的保持性问题，特别是验证当生成元受到θ-次协调扰动时，解析半群的拟紧性和均匀指数稳定性是否得以维持，以及如何扩展该结果的适用范围。

Method: 首先证明解析半群在生成元受到沿轨线紧的θ-次协调扰动后仍保持拟紧性；利用此结果结合扰动生成元产生强稳定半群的条件，证明均匀指数稳定性的渗透性；再将解析性假设分别放宽至Crandall-Pazy半群（对应更小类θ-次协调扰动）和立即可微半群（对应有界扰动）；最后构造反例说明更大的A-有界扰动类下均匀稳定化不成立。

Result: 成功建立了解析半群在θ-次协调扰动下拟紧性和均匀指数稳定性的永久性定理，并将结果推广到更广泛的半群类别；以非自反Banach空间上一维Neumann拉普拉斯算子作为应用实例；同时通过反例揭示了A-有界扰动下均匀稳定化的失效。

Conclusion: θ-次协调扰动是保持半群拟紧性和均匀指数稳定性的合适扰动类，其适用范围可扩展到Crandall-Pazy和立即可微半群，但超出该扰动类（如更大的A-有界扰动）将无法保证均匀稳定化。

Abstract: We prove that the quasi-compactness of an analytic semigroup is preserved under $θ$-subordinate perturbations of its generator on general Banach spaces, provided the perturbations are compact along trajectories. This allows us to prove the permanence of uniform exponential stability for an analytic semigroup under similar perturbations of its generator, provided the perturbed generator generates a strongly stable semigroup. We then show how the analyticity assumption can be relaxed to the class of Crandall-Pazy semigroups under a smaller class of $θ$-subordinate perturbations, and to immediately differentiable semigroups under bounded perturbations. As an application of the stabilization result, we consider the one-dimensional Neumann Laplacian on a non-reflexive Banach space. Finally, we present counterexamples that demonstrate the lack of uniform stabilization under the larger class of $A$-bounded perturbations.

</details>


### [108] [From Majorization to Horn and Unitary-Orbit Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05854)
*Juntai Zhou*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个统一的框架，将majorization不等式提升到完整的Horn不等式层面（等价于酉轨道不等式）。该框架的核心是广义Hersch-Zwahlen变分公式和Schubert几何提升原理。作者将该框架应用于三类算子不等式：Aujla-Silva凸函数的弱majorization不等式的完整Horn扩展、Carlen-Frank-Lieb与Zhang的双变量正幂函数的凹凸性提升，以及Bourin-Hiai的加权Karcher均值和两变量几何均值的乘法Horn不等式。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Majorization不等式通过前导部分和编码谱比较，而完整的Horn不等式捕获与Hermitian矩阵和相关的所有可容许选定特征值约束。作者希望建立从majorization层面到完整Horn层面的通用推广框架，从而将更粗粒度的谱不等式细化为更精细的特征值约束，并推广到酉轨道不等式。



Method: 主要工具包括：(1) 针对选定特征值的坐标单调函数的广义Hersch-Zwahlen变分公式；(2) Schubert几何提升原理，将所需的Horn不等式归结为矩阵压缩上适当的标量化函数的凹凸性。框架通过这两个核心要素将低层级的majorization不等式系统地提升到完整的Horn/酉轨道水平。 

Result: 作者成功获得三类主要结果：(1) Aujla-Silva凸函数弱majorization不等式的完整Horn扩展；(2) Carlen-Frank-Lieb和Zhang关于双变量正幂函数凹凸性的Horn层面提升；(3) Bourin-Hiai行列式层面不等式的乘法Horn推广，涵盖加权Karcher均值和两变量几何均值。所得结果推广了已知的弱majorization、迹和行列式不等式。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种系统性的通用框架，将majorization不等式提升到完整的Horn（酉轨道）不等式层面，并通过谱几何方法在多个重要算子不等式族上得到完整推广，为算子谱理论提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: Majorization inequalities encode spectral comparisons through leading partial sums, whereas complete Horn inequalities capture all admissible selected-eigenvalue constraints associated with sums of Hermitian matrices [Fulton, 2000]. We develop a general framework for lifting majorization inequalities to this complete Horn level, which is equivalent to unitary-orbit inequalities. The main ingredients are a generalized Hersch--Zwahlen variational formula for coordinatewise monotone functions of selected eigenvalues and a Schubert-geometric lifting principle that reduces the desired Horn inequalities to the concavity or convexity of suitable scalarizer functions on matrix compressions. As examples we apply the framework to three families of operator inequalities. First, we obtain a complete Horn extension of the weak-majorization inequality for convex functions established by Aujla and Silva [Aujla and Silva, 2003]. Second, we lift the concavity and convexity results for two-variable positive-power functions due to Carlen, Frank, and Lieb and Zhang [Carlen, Frank, and Lieb, 2016; Zhang, 2020]. Third, we establish multiplicative Horn inequalities for multivariable geodesic means, including the weighted Karcher mean and the two-variable geometric mean, extending determinant-level inequalities of Bourin and Hiai [Bourin and Hiai, 2014]. The resulting statements yield unitary-orbit inequalities that generalize familiar weak-majorization, trace, and determinant inequalities.

</details>


### [109] [On Besov and Triebel-Lizorkin spaces associated with the Grushin operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05940)
*Sayan Bagchi,Nishta Garg,Rahul Garg*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文定义了与Grushin算子（带或不带漂移）相关的经典和非经典Besov空间及Triebel-Lizorkin空间，建立了这些空间的各种刻画，并研究了它们的复插值、嵌入性质及分数阶Leibniz法则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grushin算子作为一类重要的亚椭圆算子，在退化椭圆偏微分方程和调和分析中有广泛应用。然而，与其相关的函数空间（如Besov和Triebel-Lizorkin空间）尚未被系统建立和研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，为后续的偏微分方程分析和函数空间理论研究提供工具。

Method: 本文定义了Grushin算子（带或不带漂移）相关的经典与非经典Besov和Triebel-Lizorkin空间，并系统地建立了这些空间的多种等价刻画（如通过热半群、Littlewood-Paley分解等），同时运用复插值理论、嵌入定理和分数阶Leibniz法则等技术手段对空间性质进行了深入研究。

Result: 作者成功建立了与Grushin算子相关的完整Besov和Triebel-Lizorkin空间理论体系，包括空间刻画、复插值结果、嵌入性质以及分数阶Leibniz法则，这些结果同时覆盖了带有漂移和不带漂移两种情况。

Conclusion: 该工作系统性地建立了与Grushin算子相关的函数空间理论，为退化椭圆算子背景下的调和分析和偏微分方程研究提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: In this article, we define classical and non-classical Besov and Triebel--Lizorkin spaces associated with the Grushin operator with or without drift. We establish various characterisations of these spaces, and study their complex interpolation, embedding properties, and fractional Leibniz rules.

</details>


### [110] [Rough paths below the Young threshold: an exact scale calculus and the locality phase transition at one quarter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05974)
*Zongjian Han,Yuanhe Luo*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明H=1/4是分数布朗运动可测局部性的精确阈值：当0<H≤1/4时，d维分数布朗运动（d≥2）不存在在区间逐段意义下可测、满足Chen关系的二阶提升；而当1/4<H≤1/2时，标准Hölder范围内的粗糙路径提升自动平方可积。同时引入低于经典可微性的精确尺度演算，利用匹配二进差分实现锐利的O(ε²)误差恢复与无损重构，并构造强几何提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Young 1936年定理以来，不规则积分围绕1/2阈值组织：高于该阈值路径决定积分，低于则需高阶数据。但对分数布朗运动，H=1/4是典型高斯增强的阈值，而几何粗糙提升对每个H>0都存在。作者希望厘清H=1/4这一特殊阈值的真正数学意义，并将"存在性"与"可恢复性"分离，探究不可微性是否破坏精确微分信息。

Method: 作者证明在0<H≤1/4时，对d≥2维独立分量分数布朗运动，不存在在任意正测度Borel集上支撑、从路径增量可测且满足Chen关系的有限离对角二阶坐标（无需矩、Hölder、几何性、平稳性或缩放假设）。对1/4<H≤1/2情形证明标准Hölder范围内的全律局部粗糙路径提升自动平方可积，从而无需额外L²假设即可分类。此外引入低于经典可微性的精确尺度演算系统：匹配二进差分恢复归一化导数（误差O(ε²)），支持精确局域反演和无损重构；乘法与光滑函数演算在尺度坐标中精确传递，corona商给出精确泛推导；通过重新引入Hölder振幅并结合Fourier正规排序，对任意正输入正则性和任意更低粗糙指数构造强几何提升。

Result: （1）H=1/4精确地刻画了分数布朗运动可测局部性的边界——低于或等于该值无正测度域上的区间局部可测提升；（2）1/4<H≤1/2时标准提升自动平方可积，H>1/2时有限阶增强唯一地是Young签名；（3）建立了低于经典可微性的精确尺度演算，使微分信息在不可微情形下仍然无损可恢复；粗糙提升在四分之一以下仍然存在（强几何提升对每个H>0），但正测度域上不存在区间局部可测提升。

Conclusion: 本文从根本上厘清了H=1/4阈值的本质含义：它不是粗糙路径存在的界限（强几何提升对每个H>0存在），而是可测局部性的精确界限。研究将粗糙路径的存在性与局部可恢复性分离，证明非可微性并不破坏精确微分信息的可获取性——通过尺度演算可无损恢复。这一结果不仅完善了粗糙路径理论的阈值体系，也为低于经典可微性尺度下的分析提供了全新的精确工具。

Abstract: Since Young's 1936 theorem, irregular integration has been organized around the threshold 1/2: above it the path determines the integral, while below it higher-order data are needed. For fractional Brownian motion, H = 1/4 is the threshold for the canonical Gaussian enhancement, although geometric rough lifts exist for every H > 0. We prove that H = 1/4 is instead the exact threshold for measurable locality. For d-dimensional fractional Brownian motion with independent components, d at least 2, if 0 < H <= 1/4, no positive-measure Borel set supports even one finite off-diagonal second-level coordinate satisfying Chen's relation and measurable interval by interval from path increments. No moment, Holder, geometricity, stationarity, or scaling assumption is imposed. If 1/4 < H <= 1/2, every full-law local rough-path lift in the standard Holder range is automatically square-integrable and hence classified without an assumed L2 condition; for H > 1/2, every finite-step enhancement with natural graded Holder bounds is uniquely the Young signature.
  We also introduce an exact scale calculus below classical differentiability. Matched dyadic differences recover normalized derivatives with sharp O(epsilon^2) error, exact localized inversion, and lossless reconstruction. Multiplication and smooth functional calculus transport exactly to scale coordinates, while the corona quotient yields an exact universal derivation. Reinserting the Holder amplitude and using Fourier-normal ordering produces strong geometric lifts for every positive input regularity and every lower rough exponent, with explicit ultraviolet rates and stability. Thus rough lifts exist below one quarter, but no measurable lift on a positive-measure domain can be interval-local there. The results separate existence from local recoverability and show that nondifferentiability does not destroy exact differential information.

</details>


### [111] [Area operators on Hardy spaces of Dirichlet series II: counterexamples and compactness criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06033)
*Jiale Chen,Maofa Wang,Zixing Yuan*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究右半平面上正Borel测度诱导的面积算子A_{μ,l}在Dirichlet级数Hardy空间H^p (0<p<∞)上的有界性与紧性。作者首先构造概率测度反驳了自己先前提出的猜想（该测度对所有0<p,l<∞成立，算子有界但Carleson条件不成立）；其次对所有0<p<∞刻画了A_{μ,p}在H^p和H_0^p上的有界性与紧性（两者在此情形重合）；对一般0<p,l<∞，利用消失Carleson测度和紧H_ǐ^p-Carleson嵌入给出了紧性的充分条件；作为应用，对VMOA(C_0)中的Dirichlet级数符号给出了Volterra算子紧性的另一个证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前作者们提出了关于面积算子在Dirichlet级数Hardy空间上有界性的Carleson条件猜想，但本文发现该猜想并不成立，需要构造反例推翻。同时，对于这些面积算子的紧性刻画此前尚不完整，需要系统建立有界性与紧性的特征描述。

Method: 本文采用构造性反例（概率测度）来否决先前的猜想；通过引入消失Carleson测度、紧H_ǐ^p-Carleson嵌入等工具，分别对p=l和一般p,l情形进行紧性刻画；利用Hardy空间理论中的对偶与嵌入技巧，并结合Volterra算子的已有结果给出新证明。

Result: 对每个0<p<∞，A_{μ,p}在H^p和H_0^p上的有界性与紧性被完全刻画，且二者在此类算子情形下等价；对一般0<p,l<∞给出了基于消失Carleson测度和紧H_ǐ^p-Carleson嵌入的紧性充分条件；并通过反例否定了此前Carleson条件猜想。

Conclusion: 本文纠正了作者早期对面积算子有界性Carleson条件的猜想，系统建立了Dirichlet级数Hardy空间上面积算子的有界性与紧性理论，并通过统一框架重新推导了Volterra算子的紧性结果。

Abstract: We study the area operators $\mathbb{A}_{μ,l}$, $0<l<\infty$, induced by positive Borel measures on the right half-plane and acting on the Hardy spaces of Dirichlet series $\mathscr H^p$, $0<p<\infty$. We first disprove a conjecture proposed by the present authors in an earlier work by constructing a probability measure, valid for all $0<p,l<\infty$, for which the associated area operator is bounded although the measure fails the proposed Carleson conditions. We next investigate compactness of these operators. For every $0<p<\infty$, we characterize boundedness and compactness of $\mathbb{A}_{μ,p}$ on both $\mathscr H^p$ and the Hardy space $\mathscr H^p_0$ of Dirichlet series vanishing at $+\infty$; in particular, boundedness and compactness coincide for these operators. For general $0<p,l<\infty$, we further establish sufficient conditions for compactness in terms of vanishing Carleson measures and compact $H_{\mathrm i}^p$-Carleson embeddings. As an application, we also give a different proof of a known compactness result for Volterra operators on $\mathscr H^p$ with Dirichlet series symbols in $\operatorname{VMOA}(\mathbb C_0)$.

</details>


### [112] [Functional representation and functional calculus for controlled paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06228)
*Anna Ananova,Rama Cont*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究非预期函数演算与兼容受控路径族之间的关系，证明了水平Lipschitz正则的非预期泛函沿Hölder控制生成兼容高阶受控Taylor展开，并建立了逆表示定理：兼容受控系数族可表示为基泛函的迭代垂直导数，从而统一了Gubinelli导数与泛函Itô演算中的垂直导数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了建立非预期函数演算（泛函Itô演算）与粗糙路径理论中Gubinelli受控路径之间的深层联系，揭示Gubinelli系数族的本质结构，并赋予系数层级以内蕴的路径空间微分结构。

Method: 首先对水平Lipschitz正则的非预期泛函证明其迭代垂直导数生成的兼容高阶受控Taylor展开具有层级相关的余项指数；基于此推导粗糙积分判据，并验证γ-Hölder控制（1/2≥γ>√2-1）下恢复一阶余项估计。核心结果是通过相容性、连续性和可兼容高阶受控Taylor估计等自然假设下，证明G_0,...,G_p可表示为基泛函G_0的迭代垂直导数。进一步证明该系数族类在非预期泛函复合下稳定，并推导对应的泛函链式法则。

Result: 建立了兼容受控系数族的逆表示定理：Gubinelli系数由其基泛函唯一对称确定，Gubinelli导数与泛函Itô演算中的垂直导数一致。该兼容系数族类在非预期泛函复合下封闭，得到泛函链式法则，并应用获得带Volterra记忆的路径依赖粗糙微分方程的适定性及路径依赖粗糙系数的Gubinelli导数识别。

Conclusion: 本文架起了非预期函数演算与粗糙路径理论之间的桥梁，表明Gubinelli受控系数层级本质上是基泛函迭代垂直导数的体现，为路径空间上的微分结构提供了统一的、内蕴的刻画。

Abstract: We study the relation between non-anticipative functional calculus and compatible families of controlled paths. For a non-anticipative functional satisfying horizontal Lipschitz regularity, we show that the iterated vertical derivatives generate compatible higher-order controlled Taylor expansions along Hölder controls, with level-dependent remainder exponents. We derive a rough-integration criterion from these estimates and show that, for $γ-$Hölder controls with $\tfrac{1}{2}\geq γ>\sqrt{2}-1$, the first-order remainder estimate is recovered.
  Our main result is a converse representation theorem. We consider non-anticipative functionals $G_0,\ldots,G_p$ which satisfy compatible higher-order controlled Taylor estimates along $γ$-Hölder paths. Under natural continuity and compatibility assumptions, we prove that they can be represented as the iterated vertical derivatives of the base functional: $ G_j=\nabla_ω^jG_0,\qquad j=1,\ldots,p.$ Thus the Gubinelli coefficients of a compatible controlled family are symmetric and uniquely determined by its base functional; in particular, the Gubinelli derivative is identified with the vertical derivative introduced in functional Itô calculus, giving the coefficient hierarchy an intrinsic path-space differential structure.
  We show that this class of compatible coefficient families is stable under admissible non-anticipative functional composition and derive the corresponding functional chain rule. As an application, we obtain well-posedness for a class of path-dependent rough differential equations with Volterra memory, and identify the Gubinelli derivative of the resulting path-dependent rough coefficient.

</details>


### [113] [A Counterexample to Horn's Coincidence Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06247)
*Faruk Temur*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文给出了Horn猜想的一个反例：在可分Hilbert空间中，存在定义在非空紧凸子集上的两个连续可交换映射F和G，它们没有公共不动点（即不存在x使得F(x)=G(x)）。该构造基于Oversteegen和Rogers提出的有限树之间恰好可交换的、无重合点的阶梯映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Horn猜想声称：在Banach空间的非空紧凸子集K上，任意两个连续且可交换的自映射F和G必然存在公共点x使得F(x)=G(x)。验证或反驳这一长期悬而未决的猜想是本文的动机。

Method: 通过在可分Hilbert空间中构建反例来否定Horn猜想。关键思想是利用Oversteegen和Rogers构造的有限树之间的可交换阶梯映射（至少两个有限树之间恰好可交换且无重合点的映射对），并借助某种逆极限或树嵌入技术将其提升到可分Hilbert空间的紧凸子集上。

Result: 成功地给出了Horn猜想在可分Hilbert空间中的反例，构造出一对连续可交换映射F,G：K→K（K为可分Hilbert空间中的非空紧凸子集），使得对任意x∈K，F(x)≠G(x)。

Conclusion: Horn猜想在一般Banach空间（甚至可分Hilbert空间）中不成立，从而彻底否定了该猜想。

Abstract: Horn conjectured that whenever $K$ is a nonempty compact convex subset of a Banach space and $F,G:K\to K$ are continuous commuting maps, there exists $x\in K$ such that $F(x)=G(x)$. We give a counterexample in a separable Hilbert space. The construction uses an exactly commuting ladder of coincidence-free maps between finite trees, due to Oversteegen and Rogers.

</details>


### [114] [Schwartz-Class Gabor Windows and the Balian-Low Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06255)
*Andrei Caragea,Götz Pfander*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究晶格Gabor框架中窗口函数的衰减与光滑性，引入了辛指标间隙这一新的整数晶格参数，并以此为核心给出了Schwartz类窗口存在性的完整分类，同时将经典和混合Balian–Low定理统一到调制空间尺度下的辛指标间隙Balian–Low定理框架中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gabor框架中窗口函数的正则性（光滑性与衰减）与相空间晶格的几何结构之间存在深刻联系，但现有Balian–Low定理在统一分类窗口正则性与晶格之间的关系方面尚不完整，尤其缺乏一个能统一描述不同边界情形的晶格参数。作者希望通过引入新的整数参数来对该问题进行精确分类。

Method: 作者引入了一个整数晶格参数——辛指标间隙（symplectic index gap），用它来刻画给定晶格上存在正则、良局部化窗口的条件。对于辛有理晶格（包括实际应用中最常见的具有有理坐标向量的晶格），利用调制空间的尺度建立了辛指标间隙Balian–Low定理，证明了正则性必要条件也是充分的（除少数下间隙无穷远端点外）。

Result: 证明了Schwartz类Gabor框架窗口存在的充要条件是辛指标间隙至少等于信号空间维度（辛无理晶格采用扩展间隙约定）；将经典和混合Balian–Low定理纳入统一的调制空间理论框架；对辛有理晶格给出了调制空间正则性条件完备的充分必要条件分类。

Conclusion: 本文通过辛指标间隙参数实现了对Gabor框架窗口正则性与晶格几何关系的完整分类，将经典Balian–Low定理推广为统一的理论框架，同时为后续构造光滑且紧支撑的Gabor窗口（通过几何Tiling–ε–Packing定理）奠定了基础。

Abstract: Lattice Gabor frames consist of a window function and its time--frequency shifts by the elements of a lattice. This paper addresses the decay and smoothness achievable by a Gabor-frame window for a prescribed time--frequency/phase-space lattice, and gives a classification apart from the borderline cases specified below.
  Central to the paper is the introduction of an integer lattice parameter, the symplectic index gap. It governs the availability of regular, well-localized windows for a given lattice. For example, we show that a Schwartz-class Gabor-frame window exists exactly when this gap is at least the signal-space dimension, with the extended gap convention for symplectically irrational lattices.
  We formulate symplectic index gap Balian--Low theorems that place the classical and amalgam Balian--Low theorems in a common framework using the scale of modulation spaces. For symplectically rational lattices---which include those spanned by vectors with rational entries and which are the ones most relevant in applications---the necessary conditions for modulation-space regularity are also sufficient, except at the remaining lower-gap infinity endpoints.
  The companion paper "Tilings, Packings, and Smooth and Compactly Supported Gabor Windows" proves the associated geometric Tiling--$\varepsilon$--Packing theorem and constructs smooth, compactly supported Gabor windows.

</details>


### [115] [An interpolation problem for extended Gevrey regularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06258)
*Jelena Dimitrić,Đorđe Vučković,Milica Žigić*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究扩展Gevrey类中的插值问题，证明在合适的导数阶发散序列增长条件下，仅沿该序列的扩展Gevrey型估计可推出所有导数阶的对应估计，并显式量化了参数变化，同时建立了扩展Gelfand–Shilov空间的插值原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Gevrey类由序列M_n^{τ,σ}=n^{τn^σ} (σ>1)定义，其导数阶估计通常需要验证所有阶数的条件；若能仅沿一个发散序列的导数阶给出估计就足以推出全局估计，将大幅简化验证过程。此外，需澄清扩展Gevrey类的两种定义（含超几何因子h^{n^σ}与不含该因子）之间的等价性。
