Method: 首先在适当的导数阶发散序列增长条件下，建立扩展Gevrey类两种定义间的等价性；然后利用该等价性证明插值原理，即沿特定发散子序列的估计蕴含所有导数阶的估计，并显式刻画所导致的参数τ、σ的变化。进一步将此插值原理推广到扩展Gelfand–Shilov空间，给出对称刻画并利用定义序列的关联函数（associated function）进行表述。

Result: 证明了在一定增长条件下，沿发散导数阶子序列的估计足以推出全部导数阶的扩展Gevrey型估计，且参数变化被显式量化；建立了扩展Gevrey类两种定义（含与不含h^{n^σ}因子）的等价性；并将插值原理推广至扩展Gelfand–Shilov空间，获得了对称刻画及基于关联函数的表述。

Conclusion: 扩展Gevrey类与扩展Gelfand–Shilov空间具有仅依赖发散导数阶子序列即可确定全局正则性的插值性质，这为后续在超几何性函数类中简化估计验证提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study an interpolation problem in extended Gevrey classes defined by the sequences $M_n^{τ,σ}=n^{τn^σ}$, $n\in\mathbb N$, $τ>0$, $σ>1$. We show that, under a suitable growth condition on a divergent sequence of derivative orders, estimates of extended Gevrey type imposed only along this sequence imply corresponding estimates for all derivative orders. In particular, we explicitly quantify the change of the parameters in the resulting estimates. To this end, we first establish an equivalence between two definitions of the extended Gevrey classes, one involving the supergeometric factor $h^{n^σ}$ and another in which this factor is omitted. We then establish a corresponding interpolation principle for extended Gelfand--Shilov spaces, including a symmetric characterization and a formulation in terms of the associated function of the defining sequence.

</details>


### [116] [Powers, Roots, and Positivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06298)
*Charles R Johnson,Samir Mondal,Michael J. Tsatsomeros*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文系统研究标量正性概念到方阵的多种自然推广，考察这些矩阵正性类在整数幂和小数矩阵根运算下的闭包性质，并证明多类重要矩阵的正性在分数次幂下得以保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量正数在任意幂和根运算下保持闭包，但矩阵正性有多种推广定义，需要系统研究这些推广是否保有类似的闭包性质，以深化对不同矩阵正性概念结构特征的理解。

Method: 通过系统梳理已有结果并对多类矩阵正性类建立分数次幂下的正性保持定理；以Pick函数为核心分析工具，结合证明、反例和相关结果进行全面考察，并辅以汇总表格。

Result: 证明了几类重要矩阵类的正性在分数次幂（矩阵根）下得以保持，确认了关键结构特征在矩阵根运算下被保留；同时给出了证明、反例和系统化的汇总表格作为便捷参考。

Conclusion: 对矩阵正性各种推广在整数幂和分数矩阵根下的闭包性质给出了接近穷尽的考察和推进，其中Pick函数在此类分析中起到核心作用，为后续研究和实际应用提供了系统框架与参考。

Abstract: There is a remarkable variety of natural generalizations of the notion of scalar positivity to square matrices. The lists and tables indicate those that we consider here; they are practically exhaustive of those that arise in practice and theory. Since positive scalars are closed under taking arbitrary powers and roots, it is natural to ask which of these matrix generalizations enjoy analogous closure properties.
  Our purpose here is to examine and advance these generalizations with respect to their closure under integer powers and fractional matrix roots. In addition to surveying known results, we establish for several important classes of matrices that positivity is preserved under fractional powers, showing that key structural features are retained under matrix roots. The role of Pick functions in the analysis is emphasized throughout. Proofs, counterexamples, and related results are presented, and summary tables are included to provide a concise and useful reference.

</details>


### [117] [Universal Nonuniform Sampling of Positive Operator Orbits via Complete Stein--Pick Criteria and Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06525)
*Jian Wu*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究正算子系统的非均匀采样问题。给定[0,∞)上的局部有限集T，探讨离散时间精确可观测性{CA^n}_{n∈N}何时蕴含采样可观测性{CA^t}_{t∈T}对所有正算子A和观测算子C成立。通过Stein恒等式和采样观测Gramian的双谱表示，证明有限谱模型足以检验任意正系统的普适保持性，最终用各矩阵层的径向Stein-Pick锥进行刻画，常数与维数无关。标量部分准则给出0∈T且N_T(R)≍R，在交换情形[A,C*C]=0下充分但在一般情形不充分，聚类反例显示谱相互作用构成额外障碍。若自然密度存在，普适性与0∈T且0<d(T)<∞等价；0∈T、正下Beurling密度和有限上Beurling密度构成另一充分条件。本文还发展了对规则格的定量扰动理论，其中累积条件∑|t_k−Nk|²=o(K²)迫使二次比较函数在谱边界消失，识别出平方根为与固定参考格比较的临界尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正算子系统（positive operator systems）的框架下，研究非均匀采样何时能保持精确可观测性。核心动机在于：对于经典的均匀采样（整数时间指数），精确可观测性条件已知，但在非均匀采样情形下，需要确定哪些时间子集T对任意正算子系统都能保持可观测性——即所谓的"普适保持"问题。这类问题在无穷维控制论、算子半群理论和采样理论中具有基础性意义。

Method: (1) 利用Stein恒等式将采样问题转化为代数比较不等式；(2) 引入观测性Gramian的双谱表示，将连续参数t∈T的积分表示转化为谱域分析；(3) 证明有限谱模型（finite spectral models）足以刻画普适性，从而将无穷维问题约化为有限维矩阵问题；(4) 用径向Stein-Pick锥（radial Stein-Pick cones）在所有矩阵层上给出完整特征；(5) 对规则的格点扰动建立定量扰动理论，通过累积二次偏差o(K²)刻画临界行为；(6) 通过构造聚类反例和谱相互作用分析揭示非交换情形下的额外障碍。

Result: 标量准则：普适保持等价于0∈T且N_T(R)≍R。这些条件在交换情形[A,C*C]=0下充分但非一般充分（给出N_T(R)≍R的聚类反例）。如果自然密度存在，则普适性等价于0∈T且0<d(T)<∞；另外0∈T、正下Beurling密度和有限上Beurling密度是另一充分条件。扰动理论方面，∑_{k=0}^{K}|t_k−Nk|²=o(K²)使二次比较函数在谱边界消失，平方根为临界尺度。理论推广到有限和可数族正算子轨道。

Conclusion: 非均匀采样的普适可观测性保持问题得到完整刻画：标量层面由密度条件0∈T与N_T(R)≍R给出，所有矩阵层面由径向Stein-Pick锥描述。非交换情形的谱相互作用引入额外障碍，使标量条件不充分。扰动理论揭示平方根尺度为与规则格比较的临界阈值。这些结果统一了采样理论、算子理论和不变量理论中的相关现象。

Abstract: We study nonuniform sampling for positive operator systems. Given a locally finite set \(T\subset[0,\infty)\), we determine when exact observability of \(\{CA^n\}_{n\in\mathbb N}\) implies exact observability of \(\{CA^t\}_{t\in T}\) for every positive operator \(A\) and every observation operator \(C\). Using the Stein identity and a double spectral representation of the sampled observability Gramian, we show that finite spectral models suffice to test universal preservation for arbitrary positive systems. This yields a characterization in terms of radial Stein--Pick cones at all matrix levels, with constants independent of the dimension.
  The scalar part of this criterion yields \(0\in T\) and \(N_T(R)\asymp R\). These conditions are also sufficient in the commuting case \([A,C^*C]=0\), but not in general; a clustered counterexample with \(N_T(R)\asymp R\) shows that spectral interactions create an additional obstruction. This obstruction already occurs for a singly generated positive diagonal Carleson frame and, more generally, for any prescribed finite number of generating orbits. We nevertheless obtain two positive results: if the natural density exists, then \(T\) is universal exactly when \(0\in T\) and \(0<d(T)<\infty\), while \(0\in T\), positive lower Beurling density, and finite upper Beurling density give another sufficient condition. We also develop a quantitative perturbation theory relative to regular lattices. In particular, the cumulative condition \(\sum_{k=0}^{K}|t_k-Nk|^2=o(K^2)\) forces the associated quadratic comparison function to vanish at the spectral boundary. This identifies the square root as a critical scale for comparison with a fixed reference lattice and, for regular power perturbations, reveals the universal sampling threshold. Finally, the theory applies to finite and countable families of operator orbits generated by positive operators.

</details>


### [118] [Partition invariance of variation indices and classical $p$-variation spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06564)
*Donghan Kim*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了沿细化分划序列定义的连续路径变化指数，证明在自然几何假设下该指数与经典变化指数一致（对所有具有正Hölder正则性或全阶Dini模量的路径成立），并通过dyadic Faber–Schauder系数给出经典变化指数的刻画。文中还给出反例说明分划假设不可省略，并研究了临界指数下的空间包含关系、紧嵌入及嵌入非闭性等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典p-变差指数通常定义为对所有有限分划取上确界，但在实际计算或应用中往往难以直接使用全部分划。沿细化分划序列（如dyadic分划）的定义更易操作，但二者是否等价尚不清楚。本文旨在厘清在何种几何条件下这两种变化指数一致，从而为变差空间的实用刻画提供理论基础。

Method: 本文采用解析方法，通过对细化分划序列施加自然几何假设（如网格细化条件），利用Hölder正则性及Dini模量估计来比较沿分划序列的p次变差和上的上确界与经典定义下的变化指数。同时借助dyadic Faber–Schauder系数将变化指数转化为系数空间中的条件，并通过构造显式反例验证假设的不可去除性，最终证明嵌入的非闭性与紧性等空间性质。

Result: （1）在自然分划几何假设下，沿细化分划序列的变化指数对一切具有正Hölder正则性或全阶Dini模量的路径与经典变化指数相等；（2）通过dyadic Faber–Schauder系数给出了经典变化指数的等价刻画；（3）构造了显式反例说明分划假设不能一般性地省略；（4）即使在假设成立时，临界指数处的函数空间隶属关系仍可能依赖于特定分划序列；（5）证明了在消失经典p-变差路径空间中的次临界紧嵌入，并建立了系数空间与变差空间的严格层级；（6）经典p-变差空间到uniform bounded dyadic p-变差空间的连续嵌入的像不是闭的。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地回答了细化分划序列变化指数与经典变化指数的等价性问题：等价性在合理几何假设下成立，但假设不可省略且临界行为对分划敏感。研究结果从理论上厘清了变差指数的可计算刻画与空间嵌入结构，对随机分析、正则路径理论等领域中p-变差概念的实用处理具有参考价值。

Abstract: We study the variation index of a continuous path along a refining partition sequence, defined as the infimum of exponents $p\ge1$ for which the corresponding $p$-th variation sums are uniformly bounded. Under natural geometric assumptions on the partitions, we prove that this index coincides with the classical variation index, defined using all finite partitions, for every path with positive Hölder regularity. The result extends to paths with an all-orders Dini modulus and yields a characterization of the classical variation index in terms of dyadic Faber--Schauder coefficients. Explicit counterexamples show that the partition assumptions cannot in general be omitted. Even under these assumptions, however, membership in the corresponding function spaces at the critical exponent may still depend on the partition sequence. We further obtain compact subcritical embeddings into the space of paths with vanishing classical $p$-variation and establish a strict hierarchy of coefficient and variation spaces. Finally, we show that the continuous embedding of the classical $p$-variation space into the space of continuous paths with uniformly bounded dyadic $p$-th variation sums has nonclosed range.

</details>


### [119] [Equivalence between $N$-particle log-Sobolev inequalities and non-linear Łojasiewicz inequalities for a class of mean field systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06574)
*Pierre Monmarché*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了自由能Wasserstein梯度流的Polyak-Lojasiewicz常数λ与N粒子系统的log-Sobolev常数λ_N之间的关系。证明了Delgadino等人提出的猜想（λ>0蕴含liminf λ_N>0）在一般平均场模型下成立（但不保证λ_N收敛到λ），同时指出当自由能极小值不唯一时该蕴含关系失败并给出反例。此外，还将该问题推广到更一般的Lojasiewicz不等式（对应多项式收敛率）情形，以刻画相变临界点的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解平均场极限下粒子系统与连续体模型的收敛性质之间的关系是统计力学和随机动力学的核心问题。均匀（N无关）的log-Sobolev常数与正的PL常数之间的等价性对于判断系统处于快速松弛还是亚稳态相变具有关键意义，但反向蕴含（从平均场到粒子系统）此前仅作为猜想存在，缺乏严格证明。

Method: 论文采用分析方法：(1)对一般类别的平均场模型严格证明λ>0蕴含liminf λ_N>0（但不保证极限相等）；(2)构造自由能极小值不唯一的反例，证明此时反向蕴含失效；(3)将问题推广到更一般的Lojasiewicz不等式框架，处理多项式而非指数收敛率的情形，适用于相变临界点。

Result: 第一，作者证明了Delgadino-Gvalani-Pavliotis-Smith猜想的反向蕴含在一般平均场模型下成立，但λ_N不一定收敛到λ。第二，当自由能极小值不唯一时，该蕴含关系不成立，并提供显式反例。第三，在一般Lojasiewicz不等式的推广框架中给出了相应的结果，涵盖多项式收敛率的情形。

Conclusion: 本文严格解决了平均场极限中PL常数与log-Sobolev常数之间反向蕴含关系的猜想，澄清了其成立条件（极小值唯一性）与不成立的情形，并将分析推广至Lojasiewicz不等式框架以覆盖相变临界点的多项式收敛行为，为理解快速松弛与亚稳态之间的过渡提供了完整的理论刻画。

Abstract: The convergence rate of a free energy Wasserstein gradient flow is quantified by its so-called Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) constant $λ$, which relates the objective function to its dissipation along the flow. Such a flow is the mean-field limit as $N$ goes to infinity of a system of $N$ interacting particles, whose convergence rate in relative entropy towards its Gibbs measure is quantified by its log-Sobolev constant $λ_N$. Different behaviours of $λ_N$ as $N$ goes to infinity thus describe drastically different phenomena, such as fast relaxation or metastability, with many models undergoing phase transitions between these regimes, depending typically on temperature. Under fairly general conditions, a uniform-in-$N$ log-Sobolev constant (i.e. fast exponential convergence for the particle system) is known to induce a positive PL constant (i.e. exponential convergence for the mean-field flow). A conjecture was stated by Delgadino, Gvalani, Pavliotis and Smith according to which the converse implication was true ($λ>0$ implies $\liminf λ_N >0$), even with $\lim λ_N =λ$.
  First, we will prove this converse implication, although without the equality $\lim λ_N = λ$, for a general class of mean-field models. Second, we also notice that this implication fails if the free energy minimiser is not unique, and provide an explicit counter-example. Third, we also consider the same question of relating $N$-particle and mean-field inequalities in the context of more general Lojasiewicz inequalities, which correspond to polynomial (instead of exponential) convergence rates, and can describe the situation exactly at a phase transition.

</details>


### [120] [Convex-Hull Instability of the $γ_2$-Functional in $L^p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06614)
*Helen W. J. Zhang,Chengdong Zhao*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文对每个2<p<∞和整数r≥2，构造了L^p[0,1]单位球面上的有限集T，其大小、γ₂(T)上界与γ₂(conv T)下界之间呈现特定的错配关系，从而证明在L^p[0,1]和ℓ_p空间中Talagrand研究问题2.11.3的两个估计均失败。核心工具是一个多层级乘积原理，应用于尺度分离的欧几里得单纯形乘积，并由此得到ℓ_p^r(ℓ_2^{2^{128r}})的尖锐凸化剖面及更广泛的定量失败结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在解决Talagrand提出的研究问题2.11.3，该问题涉及有限集T与其凸包conv T之间高斯平均宽度（γ₂泛函）的比值估计。作者期望检验在每个L^p空间中这两个方向上（关于集的大小和r的依赖）的Talagrand估计是否成立。

Method: 作者构建了尺度分离的欧几里得单纯形乘积，提出并应用多层级乘积原理来放大γ₂(conv T)的下界。构造了显式的有限集T⊆S_{L^p[0,1]}，其中|T|≤2^{Cr²}，γ₂(T)≤Cr，并证明γ₂(conv T)≥cr^{3/2-1/p}。同时将该构造用于分析ℓ_p^r(ℓ_2^{2^{128r}})的凸化剖面。

Result: 对于每个固定的p>2，在L^p[0,1]和ℓ_p空间中Talagrand研究问题2.11.3的两个估计均失败。该构造给出了ℓ_p^r(ℓ_2^{2^{128r}})（2<p≤∞）的尖锐凸化剖面，并通过有限表示性，证明在一切余型指数大于2的无限维Banach空间中存在定量失败。

Conclusion: 该文彻底回答了Talagrand研究问题2.11.3在p>2情形的否面：γ₂与其凸包之间的维数/大小依赖关系无法按Talagrand的估计成立，且这种失败是普遍的（适用于余型大于2的所有无限维Banach空间）。构造方法（多层级乘积原理与尺度分离）可能具有更广泛的应用价值。

Abstract: For every $2<p<\infty$ and every integer $r\ge2$, we construct a finite set $T\subseteq S_{L^p[0,1]}$ such that $|T|\le2^{Cr^2}$, $γ_2(T)\le Cr$, and $γ_2(\conv T)\ge c r^{3/2-1/p}$. Consequently, for every fixed $p>2$, both estimates in Talagrand's Research Problem~2.11.3 fail in each of the spaces $L^p[0,1]$ and $\ell_p$. The lower bound follows from a multilevel product principle applied to a scale-separated product of Euclidean simplices. The same construction gives the sharp convexification profile of $\ell_p^r(\ell_2^{2^{128r}})$ for $2<p\le\infty$ and, by finite representability, quantitative failure in every infinite-dimensional Banach space with cotype index greater than $2$.

</details>


### [121] [Order closure, order adherence and Fatou norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06689)
*A. Avilés,M. A. Taylor,P. Tradacete*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文在向量格与Banach格理论中给出了两个结果：一是否定了Gao和Leung关于子格的$uo$-粘附性是否必然序闭的问题；二是构造了一个自包含的反例，说明具有弱Fatou范数的Banach格不一定能等价地赋予具有Fatou性质的格范数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gao和Leung此前提出了关于$uo$-粘附性与序闭性之间关系的一个开放问题，作者希望解决该问题。此外，关于弱Fatou范数与Fatou性质之间的等价格范数关系在文献中尚有模糊之处，作者希望通过反例澄清这一理论边界。

Method: 作者主要通过构造反例的方法：(1) 构造一个子格，其$uo$-粘附集不是序闭的，从而否定Gao-Leung的猜想；(2) 构造一个具有弱Fatou范数但无法赋予等价Fatou性质格范数的Banach格，并给出自包含的完整证明。

Result: 第一，构造出$uo$-粘附性不为序闭的子格反例，给出了Gao-Leung问题的否定答案。第二，给出了一个自包含的Banach格反例，表明弱Fatou范数并不蕴含存在等价的Fatou性质格范数。

Conclusion: 序粘附性与$uo$-粘附性在一般子格层面上并非总是保持序闭性；弱Fatou范数与Fatou性质（在等价范数意义下）之间的差距比此前认识到的更大，这两个概念不能简单地相互归结。

Abstract: We record two results in the theory of vector and Banach lattices related to order adherence. First, we give a negative answer to Gao and Leung's question on whether the $uo$-adherence of sublattices must be order closed. Second, we present a self-contained counterexample showing that a Banach lattice with a weakly Fatou norm need not admit any equivalent lattice norm with the Fatou property.

</details>


### [122] [Sharp Summability on Supports of Prescribed Combinatorial Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06847)
*Anderson Barbosa,Daniel Pellegrino,Anselmo Raposo,Eduardo Teixeira*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文解决了Bayart系数可和性理论中的两个尖锐性问题：一是确定了所有m≥2和所有给定组合维数d∈[1,m]下的最优乘积可和性指数，得到公式prod(m,d)=min{m/d, m−⌈d⌉+1}，从而解决了Bayart的Problem 5.2；二是为维数型Hardy–Littlewood界构造了一个同时达到所有各向异性参数下最优系数指数的单一无限支撑Λ⊂ℕ^m，揭示了Hilbert阈值处的值变行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bayart关于给定支撑上的系数可和性理论以组合维数作为控制多线性系数阵列集中的几何量，但该框架中仍存在两个尖锐性问题未解决：一是最优乘积可和性指数的精确值（Bayart的Problem 5.2），二是维数型Hardy–Littlewood界的锐性（sharpness）刻画。本文旨在解决这两个开放问题。

Method: 证明结合了离散Holder–Brascamp–Lieb模型、维数约化（dimension reduction）和稀疏块选择（sparse block selection）等工具。对于乘积问题，利用矩形增长（rectangle growth）和坐标截面（coordinate sections）这两个几何量刻画两种不同的约束；对于Hardy–Littlewood实现，则通过基数（cardinality）和单坐标纤维（one-coordinate fibers）来确定关键指数。

Result: 主要结果有二：（1）确定最优乘积可和性指数为prod(m,d)=min{m/d, m−⌈d⌉+1}，该答案是两个不同障碍的下包络：支撑在笛卡尔盒子内的全局增长，以及支撑沿坐标截面的集中。（2）构造了一个单一无穷支撑Λ，其组合维数恰为d，对满足Σ1/p_j<1的所有各向异性参数p同时达到最优系数指数。当Σ1/p_j≤1/2时，倒数最优指数为(d+1)/(2d)−(1/d)Σ1/p_j；当1/2≤Σ1/p_j<1时，其为1−Σ1/p_j。该支撑锐利地捕捉了Hilbert阈值处的值变行为：阈值以下指数保留维数依赖性，阈值以上维数依赖性消失。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了Bayart框架中的两个尖锐性开放问题，给出了乘积可和性最优指数的精确闭式公式，并构造了同时实现Hardy–Littlewood最优界的泛例支撑，从而完善了组合维数在多线性调和分析中作为精确几何参数的刻画。结果清晰地展示了支撑几何（矩形增长与坐标截面）与多维Hardy–Littlewood理论在Hilbert阈值处的分界行为之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Bayart's theory of coefficient summability on prescribed supports isolates combinatorial dimension as the geometric quantity governing the concentration of a multilinear coefficient array. We settle two sharpness questions arising from this framework. First, we determine the optimal product-summability exponent for every $m\geq 2$ and every prescribed combinatorial dimension $d\in[1,m]$, thereby settling Problem 5.2 of Bayart: \[ \operatorname{prod}(m,d) = \min\left\{\frac{m}{d},\,m-\lceil d\rceil+1\right\}. \] The answer is thus the lower envelope of two distinct obstructions: the global growth of the support inside Cartesian boxes and the concentration of the support along coordinate sections. We then complete the sharpness picture for the dimensional Hardy--Littlewood bound of Bayart. For every $d\in[1,m]$, we construct a single infinite support $Λ\subset\mathbb{N}^m$ of exact combinatorial dimension $d$ on which the optimal coefficient exponent is attained simultaneously for every anisotropic parameter $\mathbf p=(p_1,\ldots,p_m)$ satisfying $\sum_{j=1}^m 1/p_j<1$. If $\sum_{j=1}^m 1/p_j\leq 1/2$, the reciprocal optimal exponent is \[ \frac{d+1}{2d}-\frac{1}{d}\sum_{j=1}^m\frac{1}{p_j}, \] while if $1/2\leq\sum_{j=1}^m 1/p_j<1$, it is \[ 1-\sum_{j=1}^m\frac{1}{p_j}. \] The same support therefore captures sharply the change of behavior at the Hilbertian threshold: below it, the critical exponent retains the prescribed dimension; above it, the dimensional dependence disappears. The proofs combine discrete Holder--Brascamp--Lieb models, dimension reduction, and sparse block selection. Rectangle growth and coordinate sections govern the product problem, while cardinality and one-coordinate fibers determine the Hardy--Littlewood realization.

</details>


### [123] [An Improved Lower Bound for the Complex Grothendieck Constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07000)
*Shengtao Guo,Ethan X. Fang,Junwei Lu*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文通过区间算术严格验证，证明了经典复数Grothendieck常数K_G^ℂ > 1.35584631827168，将Davie下界与Haagerup上界之间的差距缩小了超过四分之一。该下界及其证明由Odin自动AI研究代理发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grothendieck常数是泛函分析与算子理论中的一个基本常数，其精确值长期以来悬而未决。经典复数Grothendieck常数的上下界之间存在显著差距，缩小这一差距对于理解该常数的本质具有重要意义。

Method: 证明包含数值部分和严格验证两部分。数值计算通过区间算术（interval arithmetic）进行严格验证，确保结果的数学严谨性。该下界和证明策略由Odin自动AI研究代理自动发现。

Result: 证明了K_G^ℂ > 1.35584631827168，成功将Davie下界与Haagerup上界之间的差距缩小超过四分之一，且结果经过区间算术严格验证。

Conclusion: 该工作显著推进了复Grothendieck常数的下界估计，同时展示了AI自动数学研究在解决经典问题中的潜力。

Abstract: We prove $K_G^{\mathbb{C}}>1.35584631827168$ for the classical complex Grothendieck constant, closing more than one quarter of the gap between Davie's lower bound and Haagerup's upper bound. The numerical part of the proof is rigorously verified by interval arithmetic. The lower bound and the proof were discovered by the Odin Automatic AI Research Agent.

</details>


### [124] [A Levin-Milman theorem for Young variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07132)
*N. G. Albuquerque,D. Bugajewska,E. Demétrio,J. B. Seoane-Sepúlveda*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该论文将Levin-Milman定理（闭线性子空间中若所有元素都有界Jordan变差则必为有限维）推广到Young变差乃至更一般的有限值非降规范函数φ情形。若闭线性子空间E中每个函数在某些有限尺度λ_f>0下满足有限φ变差，则E是有限维的，且无需连续性、凸性或倍性条件。据此推出每个无穷维闭子空间F与有限φ变差函数集的交集是F中的meagre F_σ子集。此外，对任意Young函数，缩放与原始有限变差族都是极大稠密线性但非空间性的；缩放族本身是Hamel维数为c的稠密向量子空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1940年Levin和Milman证明了C[0,1]中元素均具有有界Jordan变差的闭线性子空间必为有限维。本文旨在将该经典结果推广到更一般的Young变差及任意有限值非降规范函数的变差框架下，研究无限维闭子空间中具有有限广义变差函数的分布与代数结构性质。

Method: 证明结合了多种技术工具：(1) 保缩放类的下半连续正则化（lower-semicontinuous regularization）；(2) Baire均匀化（Baire uniformization）技术；(3) Helly选取原理；(4) 针对非齐次规范函数构建定量嵌套峰（nested-peaks）构造。

Result: ① 主要定理：若闭线性子空间E⊂C[0,1]中每个函数f在某尺度λ_f>0下满足Var_φ(λ_f f)<∞，则E为有限维，且不需要连续性、凸性或倍性假设。② 推论：每个无穷维闭子空间F与有限φ变差函数类的交集是F中的meagre且F_σ（第一类型贫瘠集）子集。③ 对任意Young函数，缩放族与原始有限变差族均为极大稠密线性但不是空间性的（not spaceable）。缩放族本身是Hamel维数为连续统势c的稠密向量子空间；而在缺乏局部倍性条件时，原始族本身不必是线性的。

Conclusion: 该文建立了广义变差意义下Levin-Milman型定理的统一框架，表明有限广义变差条件在闭子空间上的加性结构限制极其严格。由此揭示了无限维闭子空间中具有有限广义变差的元素极其稀少（meagre F_σ），同时刻画了有限变差族在拓扑与线性结构上的层次：缩放族构成极大的稠密线性空间但非空间性，为变差函数空间的代数结构理论提供了重要刻画。

Abstract: In 1940, Levin\footnotemark[1] and Milman proved that a closed linear subspace of $C[0,1]$ whose elements all have bounded Jordan variation must be finite-dimensional. We prove its analogue for variation in the sense of Young and, more generally, for every finite-valued nondecreasing gauge $\varphi:[0,\infty)\to[0,\infty)$ with $\varphi(0)=0<\varphi(t)$ for $t>0$. If $E$ is a closed linear subspace of $C[0,1]$ and every $f\in E$ satisfies $\Var_\varphi(λ_f f)<\infty$ at some scale $λ_f>0$, then $E$ is finite-dimensional. No continuity, convexity, or doubling condition is needed. The proof combines a lower-semicontinuous regularization that preserves the scaled class, Baire uniformization, Helly selection, and a quantitative nested-peaks construction for nonhomogeneous gauges. Consequently, for every infinite-dimensional closed subspace $F\subset C[0,1]$, the set $F\cap\cV_\varphi([0,1])$ is a meagre $F_σ$ subset of $F$. For every Young function, both the scaled and raw finite-variation families are maximal dense-lineable but not spaceable. The scaled family is itself a dense vector subspace of Hamel dimension $\mathfrak c$, whereas without local doubling the raw family need not itself be linear.

</details>


### [125] [A Family of Completely PPT Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07260)
*Kennett L. Dela Rosa,Iana Angela C. Fajardo,Cedie B. Reyes*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类广义线性映射Φ_{α,𝔅}^±(X)=α·tr(X)I_m±ΣB_sXB_s^*，给出了该映射分别成为k-正、k-余正和k-PPT的充要条件，并探讨了相关的奇异值不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-正性、k-余正性和k-PPT是量子信息论和算子理论中刻画映射正性的关键概念。对于形如α·tr(X)I±X的经典映射已有已知结果，本文旨在将其推广到带有多个加权项ΣB_sXB_s^*的更一般情形，建立统一的充要条件框架。

Method: 本文通过对参数α∈ℝ及矩阵族B_1,…,B_q∈M_{m,n}进行系统分析，分别推导出Φ_{α,𝔅}^±为k-正、k-余正和k-PPT的充要条件，并将这些结果应用于经典映射α·tr(X)I_n±X以恢复已有结论，同时结合正性约束推导奇异值不等式。

Result: 给出了关于α和B_s的显式充要条件，保证了映射Φ_{α,𝔅}^±分别具有k-正性、k-余正性和k-PPT性质；恢复了已知映射α·tr(X)I_n±X的相关结论；并获得了与这些正性约束相关联的奇异值不等式。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一类加权线性映射在多种正性概念下的完整刻画，统一并推广了已有的结果，为量子信道与算子理论中此类映射的更深入应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Given $α\in \mathbb R$ and $ B_1,\ldots,B_q\in M_{m,n}$, consider the map \[Φ^{\pm}_{α,\mathfrak{B}}(X)=α\mbox{tr}(X) I_m\pm \sum_{s=1}^qB_sXB_s^*\ \mbox{for all}\ X\in M_n.\] In this study, necessary and sufficient conditions on $α$ and $B_1,\ldots,B_q$ are given that guarantee $Φ_{α,\mathfrak{B}}^\pm$ is $k$-positive, $k$-copositive, and $k$-PPT, respectively. As application, known results about the map $α\mbox{tr}(X)I_n\pm X$ are recovered. Furthermore, singular value inequalities associated with these positivity constraints are considered.

</details>


### [126] [Spectral structure and eigenmatrices of self-affine measures with $p$-digits in $ \bf{\mathbb{R}^{n}} $](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07468)
*Jia-Long Chen,Wen-Hui Ai*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究素数p下的自仿测度μ_{Q,D}的正交基性质。在特定p元数字集D和整数扩张对角矩阵Q的设定下，论文给出了最大正交族E_Λ成为L²(μ_{Q,D})正交基的充分条件，以及整数矩阵R使得E_Λ和E_{RΛ}同时成为正交基的充要条件，并进一步给出了实对角矩阵成为第二类谱特征矩阵的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自仿测度的谱性质（谱性、谱特征矩阵等）是分形几何与调和分析交叉领域的重要研究方向。此前已有关于自仿测度正交基和谱性的若干结果，但对于素数p>2且数字集满足特定零集结构的自仿测度，其最大正交族何时构成正交基、以及谱特征矩阵的刻画问题尚未完全解决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 论文首先假设数字集D满足其傅里叶变换的零集具有特定模p格结构，在此设定下对Q=p·diag[q,...,q]情形，利用调和分析与Fourier基理论，给出最大正交族E_Λ为L²(μ_{Q,D})正交基的充分条件。随后通过代数与数论工具刻画整数矩阵R的条件，使E_Λ和E_{RΛ}同时为正交基。最后在Q=p·diag[l_1,...,l_n]的一般情形下，给出实对角矩阵为第二型谱特征矩阵的充要条件。

Result: 论文获得了三类主要结果：(1) 对Q=p·diag[q,...,q]，给出了最大正交族构成正交基的充分条件；(2) 给出了整数矩阵R使得两个正交族同时构成正交基的充要条件；(3) 对一般的Q=p·diag[l_1,...,l_n]，给出了实对角矩阵为第二型谱特征矩阵的充要条件。

Conclusion: 本文系统地推广并深化了特殊自仿测度的谱性理论，特别是在素数p和特定数字集的条件下，完整刻画了正交基的存在性以及谱特征矩阵的条件，为后续研究更一般自仿测度的谱性质提供了重要理论基础。

Abstract: For a prime number $p > 2$, let $\bm{0} \in D \subset \mathbb{Z}^n$ be a $p$-element digit set satisfying $
\mathcal{Z}(\widehatδ_D) =\cup_{j=1}^{p-1}(\frac{j}{p}\bm{a}+\mathbb{Z}^{n}) $
for some \( \bm{a} \in \{ (i_1, \dots, i_n)^t : i_k \in [1, p-1] \cap \mathbb{Z}, 1\leq k\leq n \} \), where $\mathcal{Z}(\widehatδ_D)$ is the zero set of the Fourier transform of $δ_D$. Let $Q$ be an integer expansive diagonal matrix in $\mathbb{R}^{n}$, the self-affine measure $μ_{Q,D}$ is defined by \[ μ_{Q,D}(\cdot) = \frac{1}{\#D} \sum_{d \in D} μ_{Q,D}(Q(\cdot) - d). \] In this paper, we first provide sufficient condition for a maximal orthogonal family $E_Λ=\{ e^{-2πi \langle λ, x \rangle} : λ\in Λ\subset\mathbb{R}^{n}\}$ to be an orthogonal basis of $L^2(μ_{Q,D})$ when $Q = p\operatorname{diag}[q, \dots, q]$ with $q\geq 1$. Then we obtain necessary and sufficient conditions for the integer matrix $R$ such that $E_Λ$ and $E_{RΛ}$ are both orthogonal basis of $L^{2}(μ_{Q,D})$. Furthermore, for $Q = p\operatorname{diag}[l_1, \dots, l_n]$ with $|l_1 \dots l_n| > 1$, we give a necessary and sufficient condition under which the real diagonal matrix is the second type spectral eigenmatrices of $μ_{Q,D}$.

</details>


### [127] [Cholesky-Vinberg and Log-Vinberg means on the Vinberg cone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07555)
*Khalid Koufany*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维Vinberg锥Ω上的两种均值：Cholesky-Vinberg均值和Log-Vinberg均值。前者由与Ω相关的单可解群H得到，具有H等变性，其插值曲线是两种带挠率的平坦度量联络的测地线；后者通过在关联Vinberg代数（clan）的全局对数坐标中进行仿射插值定义，所得黎曼度量完备且平坦，其权重Fréchet均值具有显式公式。两种构造都保持定义Ω的零模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将矩阵均值理论从正定矩阵锥推广到更一般的稀疏结构锥（Vinberg锥），特别是探索在保持矩阵稀疏性的同时定义有几何意义的均值。

Method: 利用Vinberg锥Ω与单可解李群H的对应关系构造Cholesky-Vinberg均值（基于H等变性和平坦度量联络），并利用Vinberg代数（clan）的全局对数坐标构造Log-Vinberg均值（基于仿射插值和权重Fréchet均值）。

Result: Cholesky-Vinberg均值具有H等变性，其插值曲线为两个带挠率的平坦度量联络的测地线；Log-Vinberg均值诱导的黎曼度量完备且平坦，且权重Fréchet均值可通过显式公式计算。两者的构造均保持Ω定义中的零模式。

Conclusion: 论文对五维正定3×3稀疏矩阵锥上的均值理论给出了系统性的几何构造，提供了两种具有不同几何性质的均值方法，并证明了它们在保留稀疏结构的同时具有优良的几何性质。

Abstract: We study two means on the five-dimensional Vinberg cone $Ω$, viewed as a sparse cone of positive definite $3\times3$ matrices. The Cholesky-Vinberg mean is obtained from the simply transitive solvable group $H$ associated with $Ω$. It is $H$-equivariant, and its interpolation curves are geodesics for two flat metric connections with torsion. One of the corresponding metrics is the canonical Hessian metric of the cone. The Log-Vinberg mean is defined by affine interpolation in the global logarithmic coordinates of the associated Vinberg algebra, or clan. The resulting Riemannian metric is complete and flat, and its weighted Fréchet means have explicit formulas. Both constructions preserve the zero pattern defining $Ω$.

</details>


### [128] [Weakly LUR norms and the Schur property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07935)
*Szymon Draga,Tomasz Kania*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文给出巴拿赫空间的一个刻画：可分实或复巴拿赫空间不满足Schur性质，当且仅当它存在一个等价Gâteaux光滑、弱局部一致凸（WLUR）但不局部一致凸（MLUR）的范数。该构造通过对LUR单位球以弱紧集扩张并添加加权Hilbert空间项实现，结果范数可任意接近于任何给定的等价LUR范数，且保持了Gâteaux与Fréchet光滑性。对一般实/复巴拿赫空间，此类重赋范存在当且仅当空间是LUR可重赋范的且不满足Schur性质，文中给出了相应的直接构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无限维巴拿赫空间几何理论中，Schur性质与各种凸性/光滑性质之间的内在联系是核心议题。作者旨在精确刻画Gâteaux光滑、弱局部一致凸但非中点局部一致凸这类范数的存在性与Schur性质的否认之间的等价关系，从而深化对巴拿赫空间单位球结构和可重赋范性的理解。

Method: 核心构造策略是将某个LUR单位球通过弱紧集进行扩张，再叠加一个带权重的Hilbert空间平方项。这种组合使得范数保持Gâteaux/Fréchet光滑性并能任意接近给定的等价LUR范数。作者首先建立可分情形下的等价刻画，然后利用遍历性/直接构造方法推广到任意（不要求可分的）实或复巴拿赫空间。

Result: 主要结果：可分空间反Schur性质⟺存在等价Gâteaux光滑WLUR但非MLUR范数；此类范数在可分非Schur空间的所有等价范数中稠密；任意空间情形下，该重赋范的存在性等价于「LUR可重赋范且不满足Schur性质」，并给出了直接构造。

Conclusion: 该研究精确界定了WLUR与MLUR在几何刻画上的差异，揭示了LUR可重赋范性、Schur性质与退化（非中点局部一致凸性）之间的深层联系，为巴拿赫空间凸性层级理论提供了新的构造工具和完备刻画。

Abstract: A separable real or complex Banach space fails the Schur property if and only if it admits an equivalent Gâteaux smooth, weakly locally uniformly rotund norm which is not midpoint locally uniformly rotund. The construction enlarges an LUR unit ball by a weakly compact set and adds a weighted Hilbert-space term. The resulting norms can be chosen arbitrarily close to any prescribed equivalent LUR norm, and are Gâteaux smooth whenever the prescribed norm is Gâteaux smooth; Fréchet smoothness is preserved as well. Weakly locally uniformly rotund norms which are not midpoint locally uniformly rotund are dense among all equivalent norms on each separable non-Schur space. For arbitrary real or complex Banach spaces, such a renorming exists exactly when the space is LUR-renormable and fails the Schur property. We give a direct construction for this last assertion.

</details>


### [129] [Interchanging limits in the mutual information of two intervals separated by one site](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08210)
*Carlo Bellavita,Jani A. Virtanen*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了在自由费米子链中，两个被一个格点分开的区间之间的极限互信息的计算中，正则化参数ε趋零与块长度取极限的先后顺序不影响最终结果，均得到相同的常数2log2−1，且误差具有一致上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（J. Phys. A 53 (2020), 345303）中，两区间间极限互信息的计算采用了先取块长至无穷、后让ε→0的特定顺序。本文旨在验证这一结果对极限顺序的稳健性，即证明相反顺序及更一般的同步极限均给出相同常数，从而确保该极限定义的内在一致性。

Method: 将熵函数替换为依赖于ε>0的正则化版本，分别考察（1）先取块长无穷再令ε→0，（2）先令ε→0再取块长无穷，以及（3）两参数同时趋于极限等三种情形的极限行为。通过严格估计正则化引入的误差，证明误差被(1+ε)log(1+ε)−εlogε一致地界定（与两个块长度无关），从而推得所有极限顺序均收敛到同一常数。

Result: 证明了相反的极限顺序同样给出常数2log2−1；同时证明了无论两个块长度与ε以何种相对速率同时趋于极限，极限结果不变。正则化造成的误差具有统一上界(1+ε)log(1+ε)−εlogε，该上界与两个块长度无关。

Conclusion: 该极限互信息的值2log2−1对正则化参数与块长度取极限的顺序具有完全的稳健性，表明该结果是该物理系统中一个良定义且内在确定的量。

Abstract: In the paper J. Phys. A: Math. Theor. 53 (2020), 345303, the limiting mutual information between two intervals separated by one lattice site in a free-fermion chain was evaluated. The calculation replaces the entropy function by a regularized version depending on $\varepsilon>0$, takes the block lengths to infinity, and only then lets $\varepsilon$ tend to zero. We prove that the opposite order gives the same constant $2\log2-1$. Moreover, we show that the same limit is obtained whenever both block lengths tend to infinity and $\varepsilon$ tends to zero simultaneously, with no restriction on their relative rates. In fact, the error caused by the regularization is bounded by $(1+\varepsilon)\log(1+\varepsilon)-\varepsilon\log\varepsilon$, uniformly in both block lengths.

</details>


### [130] [On the contractivity of the Riesz projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08603)
*Xiangdi Fu,Dilong Li*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了Riesz投影从L^q到L^{4(1-1/q)}的收缩性（对1<q<∞成立），填补了Brevig、Ortega-Cerdà、Seip和Zhao猜想中此前未解决的参数范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brevig等人提出的关于Riesz投影收缩性的猜想在部分参数范围内尚未得到证明，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者通过给出一个严谨的数学证明，验证Riesz投影作为算子从L^q空间到L^{4(1-1/q)}空间是收缩的，覆盖了1<q<∞的全部参数范围。

Result: 成功证明了Riesz投影在1<q<∞情形下从L^q到L^{4(1-1/q)}是收缩算子，解决了猜想中此前未解决的参数范围。

Conclusion: 该结果完整验证了Brevig等人提出的猜想，填补了参数区间上的证明空缺，是对调和分析与函数空间理论的重要贡献。

Abstract: This note presents a proof that the Riesz projection is contractive from $L^q$ to $L^{4(1-1/q)}$ for the case $1<q<\infty$. This fills in the previously unresolved parameter range of a conjecture of Brevig, Ortega-Cerdà, Seip, and Zhao.

</details>


### [131] [On the pullback on spaces of distributions with prescribed microregularity with respect to a general Banach space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08741)
*Stevan Pilipović,Bojan Prangoski,Stefan Tutić*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了以Banach空间E度量的波前集位于闭锥形子集L中的分布空间D'^E_L(U)。证明了微分同胚f:O→U的拉回映射f^*构成拓扑同构f^*:D'^E_L(U)→D'^E_{f^*L}(O)。当E为L^p-Sobolev空间W^{r,p}(R^n)时，进一步研究了常数秩光滑映射的拉回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广经典分布理论中关于波前集与拉回映射的已有结果（如Hörmander关于正则映射拉回的经典理论），将其从通常的C^∞波前集框架推广到更一般的Banach空间度量情形，并研究Sobolev型波前集下常数秩映射的拉回性质。

Method: 构造满足温和技术假设的Banach空间E上的分布空间D'^E_L(U)，利用波前集的函子性质与微局部分析技巧，证明微分同胚拉回是拓扑同构；在E为L^p-Sobolev空间的特殊情形下，利用Sobolev空间的正则性理论处理常数秩光滑映射的拉回。

Result: 证明了微分同胚的拉回在该分布空间上是拓扑同构；在Sobolev波前集的情形下，建立了常数秩光滑映射的拉回结果。

Conclusion: 本文将分布拉回理论从经典波前集框架推广至Banach空间度量的更一般框架，为Sobolev型波前集下的微局部分析提供了新的工具与理论支撑。

Abstract: We consider the space $\mathcal{D}'^E_L(U)$ of all distributions on the open set $U$ whose wave front set measured with respect to a Banach space $E$ lies in a closed conic subset $L$ of $U\times(\mathbb{R}^n\backslash\{0\})$. The Banach space $E$ only satisfies mild technical assumptions. We show that the pullback by a diffeomorphism $f:O\rightarrow U$ is a topological isomorphism $f^*:\mathcal{D}'^E_L(U)\rightarrow\mathcal{D}'^E_{f^*L}(O)$. In the case when $E$ is the $L^p$-Sobolev space $W^{r,p}(\mathbb{R}^n)$ of order $r\in\mathbb{R}$, we study the pullback by a smooth map $f:O\rightarrow U$ of constant rank.

</details>


### [132] [Sharp Metric Cotype Inequalities for $L_1$ via Nonlinear Cut Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08749)
*Qingjin Cheng,Yue Wang,Bo Xiang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文确定了L₁空间中p-矩环面不等式的最优阶为 m^p n^{(1-p/2)_+} + n，并由此推导出相应metric cotype不等式在m=O(n^{1/q})的锐利尺度上成立，解决了Mendel和Naor关于L₁锐利metric cotype的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在解决Mendel和Naor提出的关于L₁空间锐利metric cotype的公开问题，即确定L₁空间中p-矩环面不等式的最优阶以及相应的metric cotype不等式的最佳尺度。

Method: 证明方法结合了有限割表示（finite cut representations）、非线性平滑估计（nonlinear smoothing estimate）以及一个精确的三元舍入恒等式（exact ternary rounding identity）。

Result: 对每个1≤p<∞和偶数m≥4，确定了L₁中p-矩环面不等式的最优阶为m^p n^{(1-p/2)_+}+n，取p次根后的比较常数独立于p,m,n。由此对每个2≤q<∞和1≤p≤q，metric cotype不等式在锐利尺度m=O(n^{1/q})上成立；特别地，二次不等式在m=O(√n)下成立。

Conclusion: 通过确定L₁中p-矩环面不等式的最优阶，作者在锐利尺度上完整回答了Mendel和Naor提出的L₁锐利metric cotype问题，其中二次情形m=O(√n)是一个重要特例。

Abstract: For every $1\leq p<\infty$ and even integer $m\geq4$, we determine the optimal order of the $p$-moment torus inequality for $L_1$: it is $m^p n^{(1-p/2)_+}+n$, with comparison constants independent of $p,m,n$ after taking $p$-th roots. Consequently, for every $2\leq q<\infty$ and $1\leq p\leq q$, the corresponding metric cotype inequality holds at the sharp scale $m=O(n^{1/q})$. In particular, the quadratic inequality holds with $m=O(\sqrt{n})$, answering the $L_1$ sharp metric cotype question of Mendel and Naor. The proof combines finite cut representations with a nonlinear smoothing estimate and an exact ternary rounding identity.

</details>


### [133] [The Optimal Scaling Parameter in Metric Cotype for Alexandrov Spaces of Nonnegative Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08754)
*Qingjin Cheng,Longfa Sun,Yipeng Zhang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文确定了非负曲率完备Alexandrov空间中度量cotype 2不等式的最优标度参数阶，该参数随离散环面维数n线性增长，回答了Eskenazis、Mendel和Naor提出的第17个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Eskenazis、Mendel和Naor提出的开放问题（第17问），确定非负曲率完备Alexandrov空间上度量cotype 2常数的最优标度参数阶，此前该问题尚未有精确解答。

Method: 采用二进Bernoulli稀释（dyadic Bernoulli-thinning）论证建立上界，该论证仅依赖于Lang–Schroeder–Sturm不等式；利用固定Wasserstein空间P_2(R^3)的雪花（snowflake）普适性建立下界。

Result: 在符号向量归一化下，最优度量cotype 2常数的平方介于max{1,n/m}和e⌈n/m⌉之间（对每个二进m≥2成立），表明最优标度参数随离散环面维数n线性增长。

Conclusion: 度量cotype 2不等式中标度参数的最优阶为O(n)，即随离散环面维数线性增长，其上界与下界之间的差距仅为常数因子e。

Abstract: We determine the optimal order of the scaling parameter in the metric cotype $2$ inequality for complete Alexandrov spaces of nonnegative curvature. It grows linearly with the dimension $n$ of the discrete torus. This answers Question 17 of Eskenazis, Mendel, and Naor. In their sign-vector normalization on $\bigl(\mathbb{Z}/(2m\mathbb{Z})\bigr)^n$, the square of the optimal metric cotype $2$ constant for this class lies between $\max\{1,n/m\}$ and $e\lceil n/m\rceil$ for every dyadic $m\geq 2$. The upper bound follows from a dyadic Bernoulli-thinning argument using only the Lang--Schroeder--Sturm inequality. Snowflake universality of the fixed Wasserstein space $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathbb{R}^3)$ gives the $n/m$ lower bound.

</details>


### [134] [A negative solution to the complemented subspace problem for Banach spaces with unconditional bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08921)
*Antonio Acuaviva*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文给出了Banach空间中带无条件基的补子空间问题在实域和复域上的否定解。对任意ρ>0，构造了可分超自反空间X_ρ上的范数小于1+ρ的投影P_ρ，使得Z_ρ=P_ρ(X_ρ)及其对偶具有Schauder基但不具有无条件基。在实数域上，两空间具有Gordon-Lewis局部无条件结构但缺乏Dubinsky-Pełczyński-Rosenthal局部无条件结构，否证了Figiel-Johnson-Tzafriri猜想。此外，该构造还否证了可分实Banach格补子空间问题，并证明可分实Banach格类不是素类。论文附带Lean 4形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典补子空间问题探讨Banach空间的补子空间是否继承了某些结构性质。本文聚焦于带无条件基的空间中的补子空间是否必然具有无条件基，以及局部无条件结构（GL-lust与DPR-lust）之间的关系。此问题与Banach格理论、超自反空间理论和基理论密切相关，涉及Figiel-Johnson-Tzafriri提出的公开猜想。

Method: 作者构造了形如X_ρ=(⊕_{j=1}^∞ ℓ_{p_j}^{N_j})₂的空间（其中p_j↓2），这是一个可分超自反空间。在此空间上构造范数小于1+ρ的投影P_ρ，使像空间Z_ρ具有Schauder基（对偶亦然）但失去无条件基。核心工具是精心选择有限维ℓ_p^n块以打破无条件性质，同时利用GL-lust与DPR-lust之间的差异来分析局部结构。此外，通过修改构造来处理可分实Banach格的素性（primary）问题，并使用Lean 4对主要结果进行形式化证明。

Result: (1) 对任意ρ>0，在实域和复域上均构造出可分超自反空间X_ρ及其投影P_ρ（‖P_ρ‖<1+ρ），使补子空间Z_ρ及其对偶有Schauder基但无无条件基，给出补子空间问题的否定解。(2) 在实域上，Z_ρ和Z_ρ*具有GL-lust但缺乏DPR-lust，否证了Figiel-Johnson-Tzafriri关于GL-lust蕴含DPR-lust的猜想。(3) 所得空间不同构于任何Banach格，从而否定解决可分Banach格补子空间问题。(4) 可分实Banach格类不是素类。(5) 全部主要结果通过Lean 4完成了形式化验证。

Conclusion: 本文通过精巧的⊕ℓ_{p_j}^{N_j}型构造，同时解决了多个长期开放问题：带无条件基空间的补子空间问题、Figiel-Johnson-Tzafriri猜想以及可分Banach格补子空间问题，并给出Banach格类非素的结果。Lean 4形式化进一步保证了论证的严谨性，这对泛函分析中此类高度技术性证明的可信度具有重要意义。

Abstract: We give a negative solution to the complemented subspace problem for Banach spaces with unconditional bases over both the real and complex fields. For every $ρ>0$, we construct a projection $P_ρ$ of norm less than $1+ρ$ on a separable superreflexive space \begin{equation*}
  X_ρ=\left(\bigoplus_{j=1}^{\infty}\ell_{p_j}^{N_j}\right)_2,
  \qquad p_j\downarrow2, \end{equation*} such that $Z_ρ=P_ρ(X_ρ)$ and its dual $Z_ρ^*$ have Schauder bases but admit no unconditional bases. Over the real field, both spaces have Gordon--Lewis local unconditional structure (GL-lust) but fail Dubinsky--Pełczyński--Rosenthal local unconditional structure (DPR-lust), disproving a conjecture of Figiel, Johnson and Tzafriri. In particular, neither is isomorphic to a Banach lattice, giving a negative solution to the separable Banach-lattice complemented subspace problem. A modification of the construction also shows that the class of separable real Banach lattices is not primary. A Lean 4 formalisation of the main results accompanies the paper.

</details>


### [135] [Some weighted modular inequalities in variable Lebesgue spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09029)
*Megha Madan,Arun Pal Singh*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文建立了广义积分算子 $T_\varphi$ 在加权变指数 Lebesgue 空间 $L_w^{p(x)}$ 上对非负非增函数成立的模不等式的充分必要条件，并给出了模不等式导出范数不等式的条件及若干示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广义积分算子在加权变指数 Lebesgue 空间上的模不等式及其成立条件，为该类算子在该空间上的有界性理论提供理论基础。

Method: 通过建立权重条件的充要性，分析模不等式成立的精确判据；进一步探讨模不等式与范数不等式之间的蕴含关系。

Result: 给出了广义积分算子 $T_\varphi$ 的模不等式成立的充要权重条件；给出了模不等式转化为范数不等式的充分条件；并通过具体例子验证了主要结果，从而获得若干已知算子的模不等式。

Conclusion: 本文为加权变指数 Lebesgue 空间上的广义积分算子的模不等式提供了完整刻画，拓展了该领域的研究结果。

Abstract: We establish a necessary and sufficient weight condition so that a modular inequality holds for a generalized integral operator $T_φ$ on non-negative non-increasing functions in weighted variable Lebesgue spaces $L_w^{p(x)}.$ We give a condition under which the mentioned modular inequality leads to a norm inequality. Lastly, examples are given to demonstrate our main result, to obtain modular inequalities for some well known operators.

</details>


### [136] [Sharp Fractional Riesz Estimates on the Hypercube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09040)
*Yong Jiao,Sijie Luo,Dmitriy Zanin,Dejian Zhou*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文在n维超立方体上证明了一个尖锐的分数阶Riesz型估计：对于1<p≤2，‖∇f‖_{L_p} ≤ c_abs(p-1)^{-2}‖Δ^{1/p}f‖_{L_p}，其中指数1/p是最优的，解决了Efraim和Lust-Piquard提出的开放问题。同时建立了高阶推广，并以此为工具简化了最优短时预估和Bernstein-Markov型不等式的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在解决由Efraim和Lust-Piquard于2008年提出、后来被Ivanisvili和Volberg在2022年再次强调的开放问题：在Walsh超立方体上，分数阶Riesz估计中的最优分数幂指数是否就是1/p。

Method: 针对超立方体上配备的均匀测度、Walsh梯度∇和Walsh拉普拉斯算子Δ，作者证明了对于1<p≤2成立分数阶Riesz估计‖∇f‖_{L_p(Ω_n;ℓ_2^n)} ≤ c_abs(p-1)^{-2}‖Δ^{1/p}f‖_{L_p(Ω_n)}，其中指数1/p被证明是最优的。此外，作者还建立了该结果的高阶版本。

Result: 成功证明了分数指数1/p的最优性并给出了完整证明，同时给出了高阶推广。作为应用，获得了最优短时估计∇e^{-tΔ}的更简洁证明，以及d-有界次数函数的Bernstein-Markov型不等式。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了超立方体上尖锐分数阶Riesz估计的开放问题，所获结果不仅深化了对Walsh分析结构的理解，还由此导出了若干重要不等式（短时估计和Bernstein-Markov型不等式）的简化证明。

Abstract: Let $Ω_{n}=\{-1,1\}^n$ be the $n$-dimensional hypercube equipped with the normalized uniform measure, let $\nabla$ be the Walsh gradient and let $Δ$ be the Walsh Laplacian. For every $1<p\leq 2$ we prove the following estimate \[ \|\nabla f\|_{L_p(Ω_n;\ell_2^n)} \leq c_{\rm abs}(p-1)^{-2}\|Δ^{1/p}f\|_{L_p(Ω_n)}. \] The exponent $\frac1p$ is optimal, thus this settles the open problem on the sharp fractional Riesz estimate by Efraim and Lust-Piquard \cite{E-LP2008} which was subsequently highlighted by Ivanisvili and Volberg \cite{I-V2022}. We also establish the higher-order counterpart. As applications of our results, we obtain simpler proofs of the optimal short-time estimate for $\nabla e^{-tΔ}$, and the Bernstein-Markov type inequality for $d$-bounded degree functions.

</details>


<div id='math.CA'></div>

# math.CA [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Characterization on spectra of self-similar measures and the dual spectral set conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05830)
*Li-Xiang An,Yan-Song Fu,Ming-Xuan Jiang*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究自复制平移集成为自相似测度谱的充分必要条件，将一维结果推广到高维（与乘积形式Hadamard三元组关联），并构造了显式自复制谱，证明了双谱集猜想对该类平移集成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱测度与谱集理论是谐波分析中的经典问题。既有研究（Łaba-Wang 2002, Dutkay-Lai 2017）已在一维情形刻画了自复制平移集成为谱的条件，但高维情形、特别是与乘积形式Hadamard三元组相联系的自相似测度这一更一般框架尚缺乏系统性研究。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以自第一作者与Lai提出的乘积形式Hadamard三元组为理论基础，对与其关联的自相似测度，给出自复制平移集成为其谱的充要条件；利用该充要条件显式构造自复制谱；进而借助所得结构性质证明双谱集猜想对该类平移集成立。证明涉及谱测度、正交指数系与Hadamard矩阵等工具。

Result: 建立了高维情形下自复制平移集为谱的充要判定准则，推广了一维已有结论；给出了具体的构造性谱例；并验证了双谱集猜想在自复制平移集类中的成立性。

Conclusion: 文章在乘积形式Hadamard三元组框架下系统解决了自复制平移集的谱刻画问题，深化了对高维谱测度结构的理解，并为双谱集猜想的进一步研究提供了新证据。

Abstract: A discrete set $Λ$ is called a {\it spectrum} of a Borel probability measure $μ$ if the exponential functions $\{e^{2πi \langleλ, x\rangle}:λ\inΛ\}$ form an orthonormal basis for $L^2(μ)$. In this work, we first give necessary and sufficient conditions for a self-replicating translation set to be a spectrum of the self-similar measure associated with a product-form Hadamard triple, which were discovered by the first-named author and Lai [Adv. Math. 431 (2023) Paper No.109257]. These results extend the studies by Łaba and Wang [J. Funct. Anal. 193 (2002) 409-420], Dutkay and Lai [J. Math. Pures Appl. 107 (2017) 183-204] in $\mathbb R$. As an application, we can explicitly construct such a self-replicating spectrum. Finally, we prove that the dual spectral set conjecture holds for a self-replicating translation set.

</details>


### [138] [Regular closed gradient ranges and asymptotic gradient values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05951)
*Tao Hu*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意非零紧支撑C¹函数在Rⁿ上的梯度值域是其内部的闭包；对于一般C¹函数，梯度值域的闭包等于值域内部闭包与渐近梯度值集合的并集。此外，还证明了R²ⁿ中无边界的紧致光滑Lagrangian子流形及其在辛同胚下的像具有正则闭的动量投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究C¹函数梯度值域的拓扑结构性质，特别是紧支撑情形下的梯度值域是否为其内部的闭包这一基本几何分析问题，以及对称辛几何中Lagrangian子流形的动量投影性质。

Method: 通过拓扑和分析方法，将C¹函数的梯度值域与其内部闭包和渐近梯度值联系起来处理紧支撑情形与一般情形；结合辛几何工具研究Lagrangian子流形在辛同胚下动量投影的正则闭性。

Result: 对所有n≥1证明了紧支撑非零C¹函数梯度值域为其内部的闭包；给出了任意C¹函数梯度值域闭包的结构分解公式；证明了紧致光滑无边界Lagrangian子流形及其辛同胚像的动量投影是正则闭的。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了C¹函数梯度值域的闭包结构，填补了紧支撑情形下的理论空白，并将正则闭性结果推广到辛同胚下的Lagrangian子流形，深化了对梯度映射和辛流形投影性质的理解。

Abstract: We prove that the gradient range of every nonzero compactly supported $C^1$ function on $\mathbb{R}^n$ is the closure of its interior, for every $n\geq1$. More generally, for an arbitrary $C^1$ function, the closure of its gradient range is the union of the closure of the range's interior and the set of asymptotic gradient values. We also prove that compact smooth Lagrangian submanifolds without boundary in $\mathbb{R}^{2n}$ and their images under symplectic homeomorphisms have regular closed momentum projections.

</details>


### [139] [A solution to the Erdős Problem #1040](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06050)
*Ioannis Tzachristas*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了对任意紧集K⊂ℂ，若其对数容量cap(K)=1，则单位lemniscate的最小平面面积ϑ(K)=0，无需对K的边界作任何正则性假设。这推广了Krishnapur-Lundberg-Ramachandran的光滑边界结果，并结合Ghosh-Ramachandran的容量大于一的结果及一个无界集上的初等论证，最终回答了Erdős问题1040中的消逝性问题：每个闭无限集F（若其超越直径为至少1）都有ϑ(F)=0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决Erdős问题1040中的"消逝问题"（vanishing question），即在什么条件下单位lemniscate的最小面积可以任意小。此前的结果仅限于光滑边界或容量大于一的情形，本文的目标是将结论推广到任意紧集（无需正则性假设），从而给出该问题的一般性回答。

Method: 证明的关键步骤包括：(1) 构造一个中心的调和多项式，在K的polynomial hull中除面积任意小的集合外处处为正；(2) 通过外部调和测度的Fourier平均，将该调和多项式实现为关于某个带号测度（其密度相对于平衡测度有界）的对数势；(3) 施加一个正的扰动并用经验测度进行L^1逼近，最终生成所需的多项式。

Result: 对于任意紧集K，若cap(K)=1，则ϑ(K)=0。结合已有的容量大于一的定理以及对无界闭集的初等论证，可以推出：每个闭无限集F⊂ℂ（超越直径≥1）都满足ϑ(F)=0，从而完整回答了Erdős问题1040中的消逝问题。

Conclusion: 本文去除了先前结果中对边界光滑性的依赖，将Erdős问题1040的消逝性回答推广到了最一般的紧集（容量为一）和任意闭无限集（超越直径至少一）的情形，达到了该问题的最终形式。

Abstract: For a compact set $K\subset\mathbb{C}$, let $\vartheta(K)$ be the infimum of the planar areas of the unit lemniscates of all monic polynomials with zeros in $K$, allowing arbitrary degree and repeated zeros. We prove that $\vartheta(K)=0$ whenever $\operatorname{cap}(K)=1$, with no regularity assumption on $K$. The proof uses a centered harmonic polynomial that is positive on all but a set of arbitrarily small area in the polynomial hull of $K$. A Fourier average of exterior harmonic measures realizes this polynomial as the logarithmic potential of a signed measure having bounded density with respect to the equilibrium measure. A positive perturbation and an $L^1$ approximation by empirical measures then produce the required polynomials. This extends the smooth-boundary result of Krishnapur, Lundberg, and Ramachandran to arbitrary compact sets of capacity one. Together with the capacity-greater-than-one theorem of Ghosh and Ramachandran and an elementary argument for unbounded sets, it follows that $\vartheta(F)=0$ for every closed infinite set $F\subset\mathbb{C}$ of transfinite diameter at least one, answering the vanishing question in Erdős Problem 1040.

</details>


### [140] [Terminating Zero-Balanced Hypergeometric Series Using Divided Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06168)
*Fatma Zürnacı Yetiş*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文运用差商方法研究终止型零平衡超几何级数，利用差商产生的显式乘积求值，给出了零平衡3F2(1)和4F3(1)求和公式的替代证明，并将其推广到一般的终止型r+1Fr(1)情形。此外，结合拉格朗日表示与差商的莱布尼茨法则，推导出终止型r+2Fr+1(1)级数的有限卷积变换及其零平衡特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差商与超几何级数之间存在密切关系，近期研究表明差商可有效用于推导终止型超几何恒等式。本文旨在将该方法应用于终止型零平衡超几何级数，以提供新证明并扩展理论结果。

Method: 利用差商产生的显式乘积求值（explicit product evaluations）分别证明零平衡3F2(1)和4F3(1)的求和公式，并将论证推广至一般的终止型r+1Fr(1)情形；同时将拉格朗日表示（Lagrange representation）与差商的莱布尼茨法则相结合，推导终止型r+2Fr+1(1)级数的有限卷积变换。

Result: 差商方法不仅能复现已知的零平衡求和公式，还得到了终止型超几何级数的有限卷积变换及其零平衡特例。

Conclusion: 差商方法在终止型零平衡超几何级数求和与变换中具有广泛适用性，既提供了既有公式的替代证明路径，又生成了新的结构结果（有限卷积变换）。

Abstract: There is a close relationship between divided differences and hypergeometric series, and recent studies have shown that divided differences can be used effectively to derive terminating hypergeometric identities. In this paper, we apply this approach to terminating zero-balanced hypergeometric series. Using explicit product evaluations arising from divided differences, we give alternative proofs of zero-balanced ${}_3F_2(1)$ and ${}_4F_3(1)$ summation formulas and then extend the argument to the general terminating ${}_{r+1}F_r(1)$ case. We also combine the Lagrange representation with the Leibniz rule for divided differences to derive a finite convolution transformation for a terminating ${}_{r+2}F_{r+1}(1)$ series. Thus, the divided-difference approach not only reproduces the known zero-balanced summation formulas but also yields a finite convolution transformation for terminating hypergeometric series, together with its zero-balanced specialization.

</details>


### [141] [Exact Asymptotics of the Multidimensional Nikolskii Constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06240)
*D. V. Gorbachev*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了多维归一化$L^1$ Nikolskii常数$\mathcal L^*(d)$的精确渐近行为：当$d\to\infty$时，$\mathcal L^*(d)=(\frac{\pi}{2}+o(1))2^{-d}$。同时获得了极值函数$\varphi_d$的正零点的渐近结果及其极限分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在精确确定高维Nikolskii常数的渐近行为，这是一个在逼近论和函数分析中重要的常数。此前仅知道该常数的大致阶，本文目标是获得其精确的渐近系数（$\pi/2$）以及极值函数零点的分布规律。

Method: 方法包含两个方向：(1) 下界估计：通过构造一个容许函数并对其进行渐近分析来获得；(2) 上界估计：将$x^{d+1}\varphi_d(x)$表示为二阶微分方程$u''+V_d(x)u=0$的两个解之积，将该方程与Bessel模型进行比较，并利用相对典范乘积（relative canonical products）进行分析。

Result: 获得了Nikolskii常数$\mathcal L^*(d)$的精确主项渐近$\mathcal L^*(d)\sim(\pi/2)2^{-d}$；对每个固定维度获得了极值函数$\varphi_d$的正零点渐近公式，并确定了当$d\to\infty$时这些零点的极限分布。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了多维Nikolskii常数精确渐近的主项问题，确立了精确的指数衰减速率$2^{-d}$及乘法常数$\pi/2$，并揭示了极值函数零点的渐近结构，为理解高维极值问题提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We prove the exact asymptotics of the multidimensional normalized $L^1$ Nikolskii constant \[ \mathcal L^*(d)=\Bigl(\fracπ{2}+o(1)\Bigr)2^{-d}, \quad d\to\infty. \] In addition, for each fixed dimension we obtain asymptotics for the positive zeros of the extremal function $\varphi_d$, and determine their limiting distribution as $d\to\infty$. The lower bound follows from the construction of an admissible function and its asymptotic analysis. For the upper bound, we represent $x^{d+1}\varphi_d(x)$ as the product of two solutions of the equation $u''+V_d(x)u=0$, compare this equation with a Bessel model, and analyze relative canonical products.

</details>


### [142] [Lifting curved Kakeya sets to linear Kakeya sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06299)
*Arian Nadjimzadah*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了许多曲率Kakeya问题可以提升为高维经典Kakeya问题。若经典Kakeya集合猜想在所有维度成立，则满足满维曲率Kakeya集的曲线族在Hörmander型族中是稠密的，这与Bourgain条件的无处稠密性形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究曲率Kakeya问题与经典Kakeya问题之间的联系，回答Guo-Guth-Nadjimzadah-Shen-Zhang提出的问题，并揭示经典Kakeya猜想在低维曲率Kakeya问题上的根基。

Method: 采用提升（lifting）技术，将弯曲Kakeya问题变换为高维中的经典Kakeya问题，利用经典Kakeya集合猜想作为推断工具。

Result: 若经典Kakeya集合猜想在所有维度成立，则Hörmander型曲线族中具有满维曲率Kakeya集的族是稠密的；线性Kakeya集合猜想可推出对Wisewell研究的二次曲线Kakeya集的相当完整的理解。

Conclusion: 经典Kakeya猜想在高维中的成立依赖于低维中一系列广泛的曲率Kakeya问题，且曲率Kakeya问题与经典Kakeya问题之间存在深刻的联系。

Abstract: We prove that many curved Kakeya problems, originally motivated by Hörmander's oscillatory integral problem, lift to the classical Kakeya problem in higher dimensions.
  As a consequence, if the classical Kakeya set conjecture were true in all dimensions, the families of curves whose curved Kakeya sets have full dimension are dense among Hörmander-type families. This is in contrast to the nowhere dense Bourgain's condition, which is a necessary condition for best-case curved Kakeya maximal function estimates. Furthermore the linear Kakeya set conjecture would imply a fairly complete understanding of Kakeya sets of quadratic curves, as first studied systematically by Wisewell. These results give a better understanding of a question posed by Guo--Guth--Nadjimzadah--Shen--Zhang, and they more generally show that the classical Kakeya conjecture in high dimensions rests on a broad range of curved Kakeya problems in lower dimensions.

</details>


### [143] [A counterexample to the Gowers $U^k$ uncertainty conjecture for $k\ge 6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06531)
*Iana Vranesko*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了Donoho-Stark不确定性原理的更高阶推广在k≥6时失败。作者构造了一个反例：在Z₃上的两点信号f=δ₀-δ₁，其左端量为2/3·(2(k+1))^{1/(k+1)}，当k≥6时该值严格小于1，且趋近于2/3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Donoho-Stark不确定性原理断言N^d≤|E||Σ|，Aldahleh等人将其细化为一阶形式1≤|E|·‖1_Σ‖_{U²}^{4/3}。存在一个自然猜想：对于所有k≥2，高阶推广形式1≤|E|·‖1_Σ‖_{U^k}^{2^k/(k+1)}应该成立，且陪集情形达到等号。验证或否证这一猜想是核心动机。

Method: 作者构造了Z₃上的反例信号f=δ₀-δ₁（两个点的差分信号）。在该信号下，左端量被计算出等于2/3·(2(k+1))^{1/(k+1)}。通过数学分析证明：当k≥6时该表达式严格小于1；当k→∞时该表达式趋近于2/3。

Result: 证明了上述关于U^k范数的高阶不确定性原理猜想对每个k≥6都不成立。反例中左端量的渐近极限为2/3，说明该猜想不仅在个别高k值失效，而且在k趋于无穷时存在系统性偏离下界的差距。

Conclusion: 该高阶推广猜想被证伪（对k≥6）。这表明简单的U^k范数高阶推广并不总能蕴含不确定性原理，需要更精细的理论框架。反例的极限行为表明即使k很大，该量也仅收敛于2/3，与所需的1之间存在不可逾越的间隙。

Abstract: The Donoho--Stark uncertainty principle asserts $N^d\le|E|\,|Σ|$, and the additive energy uncertainty principle of Aldahleh--Iosevich--Iosevich--Jaimangal--Mayeli--Pack refines it to $1\le|E|\cdot\|1_Σ\|_{U^2}^{4/3}$. It has been conjectured that the natural higher-order analogue \[ 1\le |E|\cdot\|1_Σ\|_{U^k}^{2^k/(k+1)} \] holds for every $k\ge 2$, the coset case giving equality. We show that this fails for every $k\ge 6$. The counterexample is the two-point signal $f=δ_0-δ_1$ on $\mathbb{Z}_3$, for which the left-hand quantity equals $2/3(2(k+1))^{1/(k+1)}$; this is $<1$ precisely when $k\ge 6$, and tends to $2/3$.

</details>


### [144] [Some constructions of restricted Kakeya sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06556)
*Cheng Chen,Jianbin Li,Longhui Li,Bochen Liu,Ruichen Sun,Haoran Wang,Saijie Zhou*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究带有"中心位置约束"的Kakeya集。对任意不可数Borel集A⊂R^d（d≥2），构造了一个Lebesgue测度为零的紧集，使得该集合在每个方向上包含一个中心落在A中的单位线段。而当A为可数集时，任何此类Kakeya集（即使不要求紧致）都必须具有正Lebesgue测度。这一结果证明了可数性是唯一的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Kakeya集问题关注包含每个方向单位线段的集合。本文引入一个额外的限制：这些单位线段的中心必须属于给定的集合A。作者希望探究当A为可数集与不可数集时，此类Kakeya集的Lebesgue测度行为是否存在本质差异，从而精确定位阻碍零点集构造的条件。

Method: 对于任意不可数Borel集A⊂R^d，作者构造了一个测度为零的紧集，使其在每个方向上包含一个中心属于A的单位线段。该构造充分利用了A的不可数性与Borel结构，通过适当的几何与分析技术实现。

Result: 主要结论：当A为不可数Borel集时，存在测度为零的紧Kakeya集（在中心属于A的限制下）；而当A为可数集时，任何满足该限制的Kakeya集（无论是否紧致）必须具有正Lebesgue测度。这说明"A的可数性"是能否构造零测Kakeya集的唯一决定性因素。

Conclusion: 该结果完整刻画了在中心位置约束下Kakeya集可达到零测度的充分必要条件：A必须为不可数集（且为Borel集以保证可构造性）。可数性是唯一的障碍。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider Kakeya sets with the additional restriction that centers of the unit line segments belong to a given set. In particular, for every uncountable Borel set $A\subset\mathbb{R}^d$, $d\geq 2$, we construct a compact subset of $\mathbb{R}^d$ of Lebesgue measure zero that contains, in every direction, a unit line segment whose center lies in $A$. Notice that every such Kakeya set (not even necessarily compact) must have positive Lebesgue measure if $A$ is countable. So our result shows that the countability is in fact the only obstruction.

</details>


### [145] [Admissible characterization in delay equations and robustness of exponential dichotomies against small-delay perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07407)
*Shuang Chen,Weinian Zhang*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文针对非自治延迟方程中指数二分法在小时滞扰动下的鲁棒性问题，提出了一种基于两对Banach空间的可容许性刻画方法，可得到显式的二分指数，并通过算子扰动方法证明小时滞扰动保持指数二分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非自治延迟方程中指数二分法对小时滞扰动的鲁棒性面临根本性障碍：缺乏有效的可容许性刻画。与常微分方程不同，延迟方程的变分常数公式需要将相空间扩展到不连续函数空间，且对过去状态的依赖使得通过可容许性建立指数二分法更为困难（需要构造合适的可容许对并通过可容许性估计不稳定/稳定子空间的增长/衰减率）。

Method: 作者提出了一种基于两对Banach空间的可容许性刻画方法，该方法能产生显式的二分指数（dichotomy exponents）。通过该刻画结合算子扰动方法，分析小时滞扰动对指数二分性的保持性质。

Result: 证明了基于两对Banach空间的可容许性刻画能够给出显式的二分指数，并且利用该刻画和算子扰动方法，证明了小时滞扰动能够保持指数二分性。

Conclusion: 本文解决了非自治延迟方程中指数二分法对小时滞扰动的鲁棒性问题，为延迟方程提供了有效的可容许性刻画框架，并证明了小时滞扰动下指数二分性的保持。

Abstract: Robustness of exponential dichotomies against small-delay perturbations presents a fundamental obstacle: the lack of an effective admissible characterization in nonautonomous delay equations. Considerable efforts have been devoted to obtaining such a characterization for differential equations in Banach spaces. However, even unlike ordinary differential equations, the variation of constants formula for delay equations requires extending the phase space to a space of discontinuous functions, and the dependence on the past states leads to another challenge in establishing exponential dichotomy via admissibility, i.e., constructing a suitable admissible pair and then estimating the growth/decay rates along the unstable/stable subspaces via the admissibility property. In this paper, we provide an admissible characterization based on two pairs of Banach spaces that yields explicit dichotomy exponents. Through this characterization and an operator perturbation method, we prove that small-delay perturbations preserve exponential dichotomies.

</details>


### [146] [Two-parameter variational estimates for averages over tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07422)
*Juyoung Lee,Sanghyuk Lee,Feng Zhang,Shuijiang Zhao*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究$\mathbb{R}^3$中环面（torus）上的双参数变分不等式，引入了一种结合矩形增量和边界变分的局部双参数$r$-变分范数，建立了该变分算子到端点的sharp $L^p$-$L^q$有界性，并与单参数情形对比揭示了本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单参数变分不等式已有较完善的发展，但其多参数版本远未被充分理解。作者希望探索双参数变分不等式，以捕捉跨两个参数的振荡行为——这是极大函数无法刻画的信息。

Method: 引入了局部双参数$r$-变分范数，该范数结合了矩形增量（rectangular increments）与沿边界的变分。证明方法综合了双参数传播子的平方函数估计与通过混合范数插值得到的局部光滑化估计。

Result: 建立了双参数变分算子在$\mathbb{R}^3$环面上的sharp $L^p$-$L^q$有界性并达到端点；同时获得了对应单参数局部变分算子的sharp $L^p$有界性到端点，揭示了两者有界性区域的本质区别。

Conclusion: 双参数变分算子不仅逐点控制双参数局部极大函数，还捕捉了极大函数不具备的参数间振荡信息。单参数与双参数算子有界性区域的差异表明多参数结构带来了本质上不同的调和分析行为。

Abstract: One-parameter variational inequalities are well developed, whereas their multi-parameter counterparts remain much less understood. We explore two-parameter variational inequalities for averages over tori in $\mathbb{R}^3$. To capture the underlying two-parameter structure, we introduce a local two-parameter $r$-variation norm that combines rectangular increments with variations along the boundary. The resulting variation operator pointwise dominates the corresponding two-parameter local maximal function and, unlike the maximal function, also captures oscillation across the two parameters. We establish sharp $L^p$--$L^q$ bounds for this variation operator up to endpoints. For comparison, we also obtain sharp $L^p$ bounds up to endpoints for the corresponding local one-parameter variation operator, revealing a genuine difference between the one- and two-parameter boundedness regions. The proof combines square function estimates for two-parameter propagators with local smoothing estimates through mixed-norm interpolation.

</details>


### [147] [Boundedness of Multilinear Hilbert Transforms Along Moment Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07513)
*Jingwei Guo,Xiaochun Li,Guoqing Zhan*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了沿矩曲线（moment curves）的多线性Hilbert变换的有界性，通过对平移和非平移多线性仿积（paraproducts）建立一致估计，并应用Sobolev平滑不等式。作为方法副产品，还获得了沿矩曲线的多线性极大函数的相同界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多线性Hilbert变换沿矩曲线的有界性问题是调和分析中的一个重要开放问题，涉及多线性奇异积分算子在特殊曲线上的行为，其研究有助于深入理解多线性振荡积分和奇异性结构。

Method: 文章采用两步策略：首先建立平移与非平移多线性仿积的一致（uniform）估计，然后应用Sobolev平滑不等式将这些估计整合到沿矩曲线的多线性Hilbert变换的框架中。该方法同时自然地推广到多线性极大函数的情形。

Result: 成功证明了沿矩曲线的多线性Hilbert变换的有界性，并作为副产物获得了沿矩曲线的多线性极大函数的相同有界性结论。

Conclusion: 通过仿积估计与Sobolev平滑不等式的结合，完整解决了沿矩曲线的多线性Hilbert变换的有界性问题，并推广到极大函数版本，为该类奇异积分算子的理论提供了新的统一方法。

Abstract: We prove the boundedness of multilinear Hilbert transforms along moment curves by establishing uniform estimates for both translated and untranslated multilinear paraproducts and by applying a Sobolev smoothing inequality. As a byproduct of our method, we obtain the same bounds for multilinear maximal functions along moment curves.

</details>


### [148] [Piñeiro mixed orthogonal polynomials and bidiagonal factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07678)
*Amílcar Branquinho,Juan E. F. Díaz,Ana Foulquié-Moreno,Manuel Mañas*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究秩一测度矩阵相关的混合多重正交多项式及其递推关系。通过在阶梯线上利用Gauss–Borel分解识别相应的带状递推矩阵，并利用初等Christoffel变换将该递推矩阵分解为上下双对角因子。针对混合Piñeiro系统，推导了围道积分和广义超几何表示，以及递推和分解系数的公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合多重正交多项式是多重正交多项式理论中一类重要的推广，与秩一测度矩阵相关的混合系统相比由多个测度定义的系统更具一般性。理解其代数结构与递推性质是正交多项式理论的核心问题，同时递推矩阵的双对角分解在数值算法和连分数理论中具有潜在应用价值。

Method: 本文采用以下方法：（1）在阶梯线(step line)上通过对矩矩阵(moment matrix)进行Gauss–Borel分解来识别对应的带状递推矩阵；（2）利用初等Christoffel变换（elementary Christoffel transformations）实现递推矩阵的上下双对角因子分解；（3）针对混合Piñeiro系统，推导其围道积分表示与广义超几何函数表示，并给出递推系数及因子化系数的显式公式。

Result: 成功建立了秩一测度矩阵混合多重正交多项式在阶梯线上的带状递推矩阵结构，并通过Christoffel变换获得该矩阵的双对角分解。此外，对混合Piñeiro系统给出了围道积分与广义超几何表示，以及相应的递推系数和因子化系数的闭合公式。

Conclusion: 本文系统地将Gauss–Borel因式分解框架推广到秩一测度矩阵情形下的混合多重正交多项式，揭示了其递推结构的代数本质，并为混合Piñeiro系统提供了完整的解析表示，丰富和深化了混合多重正交多项式理论。

Abstract: We study mixed multiple orthogonal polynomials associated with rank-one matrices of measures and their recurrence relations. On the step line we identify the corresponding banded recurrence matrix through the Gauss--Borel factorization of the moment matrix. Elementary Christoffel transformations lead to a factorization of this recurrence matrix into lower and upper bidiagonal factors. For the mixed Piñeiro system we derive contour-integral and generalized hypergeometric representations and formulas for the recurrence and factorization coefficients.

</details>


### [149] [Polynomial Bohnenblust--Hille Bounds for product of cyclic groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07758)
*Joseph Slote,Alexander Volberg*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文针对有限循环群乘积C_K^N上的函数，提出一个自包含的证明，表明按相互作用阶（active坐标数）限制的维度无关Bohnenblust–Hille常数呈多项式增长：对p_d=2d/(d+1)及其定义的γ_K，有(Σ_{s(α)≤d}|f̂(α)|^{p_d})^{1/p_d} ≤ C_K d^{4γ_K+5}‖f‖_∞。此估计同样适用于全次数类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bohnenblust–Hille不等式在多线性分析与调和分析中具有重要地位，其维度无关形式及按相互作用阶限制的常数增长行为（指数型还是多项式型）是核心问题。该文旨在对一般的有限循环群情形给出一个关于相互作用阶多项式增长的确定性证明。

Method: 文中给出了自包含的证明方法，针对K≥2的循环群乘积构造Fourier系数在低维（s(α)≤d）截断下的l^{p_d}范数估计，引入显式指数γ_K，并通过组合与解析论证建立关于阶数d的多项式型上界。

Result: 得到主要不等式：(Σ_{s(α)≤d}|f̂(α)|^{p_d})^{1/p_d} ≤ C_K d^{4γ_K+5}‖f‖_∞，其中γ_2=1/2，γ_K=K log(K-1)/(4(K-2))（K≥3）。该结果推广到canonical total-degree类。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了有限循环群情形下按相互作用阶限制的Bohnenblust–Hille常数具有多项式增长（O(d^{4γ_K+5})），而非指数增长，为该领域提供了结构性的上界结果。

Abstract: Fix an integer $K\ge2$, and let $C_K^N\cong(\Z/K\Z)^N$ be the product of cyclic groups of order $K$. For a Fourier character $χ_α$, let $s(α)$ be the number of active coordinates. We give a self-contained proposed proof that the dimension-free Bohnenblust--Hille constants governed by interaction order grow polynomially: if $p_d=2d/(d+1)$ and \[
  γ_2=\frac12,
  \qquad
  γ_K=\frac{K\log(K-1)}{4(K-2)}\quad(K\ge3), \] then \[
  \left(\sum_{s(α)\le d}
  \abs{\wh f(α)}^{p_d}\right)^{1/p_d}
  \le C_K d^{4γ_K+5}\norm f_\infty. \] The same estimate follows for the canonical total-degree class.

</details>


### [150] [A sharp covering theorem and Solyanik estimates for Euclidean balls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07880)
*Mayukh Mukherjee*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文解决了Han和Lu提出的有限族欧氏球覆盖估计猜想，并以此确定了有质心Hardy-Littlewood极大算子的最优Solyanik渐近行为，以及修正极大算子的最优弱(1,1)增长速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决两个相关的公开猜想：(1) Han和Lu关于欧氏球族覆盖估计的猜想——如何用倍增数可控的子族覆盖原并集；(2) Hagelstein和Parissis关于有质心Hardy-Littlewood极大算子Solyanik渐近行为的猜想1(b)。这些问题在调和分析和几何测度论中具有重要意义，Solyanik常数刻画了极大算子在临界点附近的邻域估计行为。

Method: 作者证明了对任意有限欧氏球族及其(1+δ)-膨胀，可选取一个子族覆盖原并集，且该子族中未膨胀球的基数(重数)至多为C_n δ^{-(n-1)/2}，维数依赖常数C_n仅依赖于维数n。这一覆盖估计成为推导Solyanik渐近的核心技术工具。利用该覆盖引理，结合极大算子与覆盖理论之间的标准联系，推导出最优的Solyanik渐近指数2/(n+1)。对于修正的极大算子，同样利用该覆盖框架建立弱(1,1)最优增长阶(k-1)^{-(n-1)/2}，并推广至L^p和Fefferman-Stein界。

Result: 论文的主要结果包括：(1) 证明了Han-Lu覆盖猜想，即子族重数为O(δ^{-(n-1)/2})；(2) 确定了有质心Hardy-Littlewood极大算子关于欧氏球的Solyanik渐近为C_n(α)-1 ≈ (1-α)^{2/(n+1)}，指数2/(n+1)为最优；(3) 对修正的有质心极大算子，确定了其弱(1,1)常数随k→1的最优增长率为(k-1)^{-(n-1)/2}，该界对Radon测度一致成立；(4) 获得了相应的L^p和Fefferman-Stein估计。

Conclusion: 本文通过建立精确的覆盖估计，完整解决了两类长期悬而未决的猜想：覆盖维数依赖性指标δ^{-(n-1)/2}被证明是最优的，由此导出的Solyanik指数2/(n+1)和弱(1,1)增长率均达到最优精度。这些结果深化了我们对有质心极大算子几何特性的理解，也为涉及欧氏球族覆盖的后续研究提供了基础工具。

Abstract: For every finite family of Euclidean balls in $\mathbb{R}^n$, $n\ge2$, and every $0<δ<1/2$, we select a subfamily whose $(1+δ)$-dilations cover the original union and whose undilated balls have multiplicity at most $C_nδ^{-(n-1)/2}$. This proves the covering estimate conjectured by Han and Lu \cite{HL}. As an application, we determine the optimal Solyanik asymptotic \[ \mathcal C_n(α)-1\asymp_n(1-α)^{2/(n+1)} \qquad(α\uparrow1) \] for the uncentered Hardy--Littlewood maximal operator over Euclidean balls, which is Conjecture~1(b) of Hagelstein and Parissis \cite{HP14}. For the modified uncentered maximal operators, we determine the optimal weak $(1,1)$ growth rate $(k-1)^{-(n-1)/2}$ as $k\downarrow1$, uniformly over Radon measures. We also obtain the corresponding $L^p$ and Fefferman--Stein bounds.

</details>


### [151] [Boundary value problems for linear differential-algebraic equations: solvability via the Kronecker canonical form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07980)
*Anar Assanova,Carsten Trunk,Roza Uteshova,Henrik Winkler*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究了常系数微分代数方程的两点边值问题的可解性，针对一般奇异情形（而非以往假设的规则矩阵对），利用Kronecker规范形将问题分解为简单子系统，并通过参数化方法将边值问题转化为代数方程组，给出了解存在唯一性的判定准则及完整求解框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于微分代数方程两点边值问题的研究通常假设关联的矩阵对是规则的，但实际应用中常遇到一般奇异情形，现有方法无法覆盖。因此需要发展一套系统性的分析框架来处理更一般的奇异情况。

Method: 1. 利用Kronecker规范形将原问题分解为若干更简单的子系统，便于逐一分析；2. 采用参数化方法将边值问题转化为代数方程组，从而借助代数理论进行可解性分析。

Result: 推导出了解的存criterion在与唯一性的判定准则，建立了求解此类边值问题的完整框架，并通过多个说明性算例验证了所得结果的有效性与适用性。

Conclusion: 本文超越了以往对规则矩阵对的限制，将可解性分析推广到一般奇异情形，提供了一套系统的理论工具和求解策略，为实际工程中出现的奇异微分代数方程边值问题提供了理论保障与方法支撑。

Abstract: The solvability of two-point boundary value problems for constant-coefficient differential-algebraic equations is investigated. Unlike previous studies, which often assume that the matrix pair associated with the equation is regular, we consider the general singular case. Using the Kronecker canonical form, we decompose the problem into simpler subsystems, enabling a systematic analysis of solvability. The method of parameterization reduces the boundary value problem to a system of algebraic equations. We derive criteria for the existence and uniqueness of solutions and provide a comprehensive framework for solving such boundary value problems. Several illustrative examples are presented and show the applicability of the results.

</details>


### [152] [An Askey-Type Confluence Scheme for Hahn-Like Multiple Orthogonality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08048)
*Manuel Mañas*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文将Wolfs引入的关于普通I型/II型多重正交性的Jacobi-like和Laguerre-like系统嵌入统一的Askey式合流方案中。通过同时应用Bernstein变换到q-Jacobi-like权重，得到一个正有限格点Hahn-like祖先系统，用多重beta积分表示，q=1时退化到经典Hahn权重，并收敛回到连续系统。参数与标度极限产生Kravchuk-like、两种Meixner-like、两种Charlier-like、Jacobi-like、两种Laguerre-like和Hermite-like族，每条箭头均通过显式超几何正交性数据实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重正交多项式系统在数学物理中有重要应用，但Wolfs引入的Jacobi-like和Laguerre-like系统缺乏统一的离散化与合流框架。作者希望将这些系统置于单一Askey型合流方案中，从而系统地理解离散与连续系统之间、以及不同正交族之间的互连关系，并为每个箭头提供显式的超几何正交构造。

Method: (1) 利用Bernstein变换将q-Jacobi-like权重同时变换为有限格点Hahn-like权重，构造多重beta积分表示。(2) 通过参数与标度极限从Hahn-like祖先导出各类离散与连续正交族。(3) 利用终项超几何函数(如_{q+2}F_{q+1}(1))显式构造near-diagonal多重指标下的Hahn-like type-II多项式，证明精确的逆Bernstein恒等式。(4) 构造归一化type-I形式，在分离与非零假设下通过有限终项超几何和恢复单个多项式分量并证明唯一性。(5) 通过系数提取与无穷远重构处理有限极点项（Kampé de Fériet块），在非反射分支上用Lauricella–Horn类有限和完成极限，获得Kravchuk–Charlier、Meixner–Charlier、Meixner–Laguerre和Charlier–Hermite的分区段分量合流。

Result: 建立了从Hahn-like祖先到Kravchuk-like、双Meixner-like、双Charlier-like、Jacobi-like、双Laguerre-like和Hermite-like的完整Askey型合流图谱。q=1时所有公式退化为经典族。q>1时两种Meixner-like系统以及两种Charlier-like系统在归一化行的置换下不等价，表明新增了离散层面的结构差异性。每个合流箭头均有显式超几何正交性数据支持。

Conclusion: 该工作将多重Jacobi-like与Laguerre-like系统纳入统一的Askey式合流方案，通过Bernstein变换构建了有限格点Hahn-like祖先及其全部合流后裔。不仅建立了经典q=1极限的一致性，还揭示了q>1时Meixner-like与Charlier-like系统对行置换的敏感性，为多重正交多项式的分类与互连提供了系统性的补充。

Abstract: We embed the Jacobi-like and Laguerre-like systems for ordinary type-I/type-II multiple orthogonality introduced by Wolfs into a single Askey-type confluence scheme. Applying the Bernstein transform simultaneously to the $q$ Jacobi-like weights produces a positive finite-lattice Hahn-like ancestor, represented by multiple beta integrals, reducing to the classical Hahn weight for $q=1$, and converging back to the continuous system. Parameter and scaling limits then yield Kravchuk-like, two Meixner-like, two Charlier-like, Jacobi-like, two Laguerre-like, and Hermite-like families.
  We realize every arrow through explicit hypergeometric orthogonality data. For each near-diagonal multi-index $1\leq |\mathbf m|\leq N$, the Hahn-like type-II polynomial is a terminating ${}_{q+2}F_{q+1}(1)$ series satisfying an exact inverse Bernstein identity. We construct the normalized type-I form and, under explicit separation and nonvanishing assumptions, recover its individual polynomial components by finite sums of terminating hypergeometric functions and prove uniqueness. Finite-pole terms regroup into terminating Kampé de Fériet blocks. Their limits are obtained by coefficient extraction and reconstruction at infinity on the unreflected Kravchuk-like and Meixner-II-like branches, and by finite Lauricella--Horn sector sums on the reflected branches. This yields sectorwise component confluence along the Kravchuk--Charlier, Meixner--Charlier, Meixner--Laguerre, and Charlier--Hermite arrows. For $q=1$ all formulas reduce to the classical families; for $q>1$ the two Meixner-like systems, and likewise the two Charlier-like systems, are inequivalent under permutation of normalized rows.

</details>


### [153] [Full-radius dimension-free maximal inequalities for discrete Euclidean balls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08433)
*Kaiwen Jin,Qingtang Su*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了对所有1<p≤∞，整数格Z^d上欧几里得球归一化平均在全体半径上的维数无关极大不等式，解决了归因于Stein的l^2问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Stein提出的关于离散欧几里得球平均极大算子的维数无关l^2有界性问题，这是一个长期悬而未决的问题。

Method: 在整数平方半径处使用双鞍点展开，将球乘子与归一化离散高斯函数在零频率和奇偶频率处进行比较。一阶估计给出全半径l^2界；对1<p<2，结合高阶余项估计、余项的维数一致l^1界以及逐层插值方法。

Result: 对所有1<p≤∞建立了维数无关的极大不等式，推广了此前仅针对某些p范围的结果。

Conclusion: 完全解决了Stein关于离散球平均极大算子的l^2问题，并将其推广到全范围1<p≤∞。

Abstract: For every $1<p\le\infty$, we prove dimension-free maximal inequalities over all radii for normalized averages over Euclidean balls in $\mathbb Z^d$. In particular, this settles the $\ell^2$ question attributed to Stein. The proof uses a two-saddle expansion at integer squared radii to compare ball multipliers with normalized discrete Gaussians at the zero and parity frequencies. First-order estimates give the full-radius $\ell^2$ bound. For $1<p<2$, we combine higher-order residual estimates with dimension-uniform $\ell^1$ bounds for the residuals and levelwise interpolation to obtain the full range.

</details>


### [154] [Symmetric products of Laguerre zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08480)
*K. Castillo*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明并大幅推广了Gazeau、Josse-Michaux和Monceau关于Hermite多项式极值正零点的猜想。该猜想源于相平面的有限维量子化。作者证明：对参数α>-1的m次广义Laguerre多项式L_m^(α)，当m≥2j-1时，其第j小与第j大零点的乘积随m严格递增。通过经典Hermite-Laguerre恒等式，该结果证明了原始猜想对次数N≥4的Hermite多项式H_N在两种奇偶性下均成立。此外，还解决了一个涉及相应Jacobi矩阵形变的一参数扩展问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究源于对相平面有限维量子化问题的探索。Gazeau等人提出的猜想涉及Hermite多项式极值正零点的单调性，这是一个在量子物理和正交多项式理论交叉领域的开放问题。证明并推广该猜想不仅能验证物理推导的正确性，还能深化对Hermite多项式与Laguerre多项式零点结构之间深层联系的理解。此外，通过处理相关的Jacobi矩阵形变推广，研究试图建立更一般的数学框架，揭示这类零点极值规律的本质。

Method: 本文的方法核心是将Hermite多项式的零点问题转化为广义Laguerre多项式的零点分析。作者利用经典Hermite-Laguerre恒等式建立两类多项式零点之间的联系，从而证明了对广义Laguerre多项式L_m^(α)，其第j小与第j大零点乘积在m≥2j-1时关于m的严格单调递增性质。这一核心结果随后通过恒等关系直接推导出Hermite多项式情形下原始猜想的证明。文章还进一步利用Jacobi矩阵的形变技术，建立了含一个参数的扩展结果。

Result: 研究取得了三项主要成果：（1）证明了Gazeau等人的原始猜想，即对每个N≥4，Hermite多项式H_N在两种奇偶性下其极值正零点的乘积均满足所期望的单调性；（2）将该猜想大幅推广到广义Laguerre多项式L_m^(α)（α>-1）的情形，证明了其第j小与第j大零点的乘积在m≥2j-1时随m严格递增；（3）进一步解决了一个涉及Jacobi矩阵形变的一参数推广问题，扩展了结果的应用范围。

Conclusion: 本文不仅完整证明了Gazeau等人提出的关于Hermite多项式极值正零点的猜想，而且通过引入广义Laguerre多项式的框架大幅推广了该结果。这一工作揭示了Hermite与Laguerre多项式零点结构之间的深层互连，并为有限维量子化理论提供了坚实的数学基础。通过Jacobi矩阵形变的扩展，所建立的方法论可为更广泛的正交多项式零点性质研究提供参考。

Abstract: We prove and substantially extend a conjecture of Gazeau, Josse-Michaux, and Monceau concerning the extreme positive zeros of Hermite polynomials, which arose from a finite-dimensional quantisation of the phase plane. For the generalised Laguerre polynomial $L_m^{(α)}$ of degree $m$ and parameter $α>-1$, the product of its $j$th smallest and $j$th largest zeros is strictly increasing with $m$ for each fixed positive integer $j$, once $m\ge2j-1$. The classical Hermite-Laguerre identities then prove the original conjecture for the degree-$N$ Hermite polynomial $H_N$, in both parities and for every $N\ge4$. As a further consequence, a one-parameter extension involving a deformation of the corresponding Jacobi matrix is settled.

</details>


### [155] [Intersections and Minkowski Sums of Four-Corner Cantor Dusts with the Unit Circle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08568)
*Jiangtao Li,Zhao Shen,Yufeng Wu*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类自相似集（由迭代函数系统{λx, λx+1-λ}生成）与其自身的笛卡尔积C_λ与单位圆S^1的交集E_λ，以及闵可夫斯基和A_λ=C_λ+S^1。对于交集问题，证明了当λ>λ_∞≈0.305854时E_λ为无穷集，回答了一部分Jiang-Kong-Li-Wang提出的问题，并在更宽范围给出基数与Hausdorff维数的结果；对于闵可夫斯基和问题，证明了A_λ在1/4&lt;λ&lt;1/3范围内具有非空内部，补全了Simon-Taylor的分类并推广到一般正则闭曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在回答与自相似集（Cantor集）笛卡尔积同单位圆相交的基数与维数问题，以及该笛卡尔积与单位圆的闵可夫斯基和是否具有非空内部的问题。前者对应Jiang、Kong、Li和Wang提出的公开问题，后者回应了Simon和Taylor遗留的开问题范围。

Method: 利用自相似集K_λ的结构特性，构造C_λ与单位圆S^1的显式相交点，通过分析λ的临界阈值（如λ_∞、(\sqrt{3}-1)/2、\sqrt{2}-1）逐步建立起不同参数范围内交集E_λ的基数与Hausdorff维数的下界估计。对于闵可夫斯基和问题，通过精细的几何与组合论证证明在1/4&lt;λ&lt;1/3范围内A_λ具有非空内部，并将结果推广到任意正则C^1闭曲线Γ的情形。

Result: 对于交集问题：证明了E_λ在λ_∞&lt;λ&lt;1/2时为无穷集（特别地，C_{1/3}\cap S^1为无穷集）；当(\sqrt{3}-1)/2&lt;λ&lt;1/2时E_λ具有连续统基数；当\sqrt{2}-1&lt;λ&lt;1/2时dim_ H E_λ>0；且在λ→1/2附近给出了dim_H E_λ的定量下界，并证明了其趋于1。对于闵可夫斯基和问题：证明了A_λ在1/4&lt;λ&lt;1/3范围内具有非空内部，结合Simon-Taylor的既有结果得到A_λ具有非空内部当且仅当1/4&lt;λ&lt;1/2的完整分类；进一步推广为C_λ+Γ对任意正则C^1闭曲线Γ在1/4&lt;λ&lt;1/2时具有非空内部。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了Cantor自相似集笛卡尔积与单位圆相交及闵可夫斯基和的结构，解决了两个公开问题，并给出了更一般闭曲线情形的推广，为自相似集与几何体的交互提供了完整的参数分类结果。

Abstract: For $0<λ<1/2$, let $K_λ$ be the attractor of the iterated function system $\{λx, λx+1-λ\}$, and put $C_λ=K_λ\times K_λ$. We study the intersection \[ E_λ=C_λ\cap S^1 \] and the Minkowski sum \[ A_λ=C_λ+S^1, \] where $S^1$ is the unit circle. For the intersection problem, let $λ_{\infty}\approx 0.305854$ be the unique root in $(1/4,1/2)$ of $2x^3-3x^2+4x-1=0$. We prove that $E_λ$ is infinite for every $λ_{\infty}<λ<1/2$. In particular, $C_{1/3}\cap S^1$ is infinite, answering the first part of a question of Jiang, Kong, Li and Wang. Moreover, $E_λ$ has the cardinality of the continuum for $(\sqrt{3}-1)/2<λ<1/2$, and $\dim_{\rm H} E_λ>0$ for $\sqrt{2}-1<λ<1/2$. We also establish quantitative lower bounds for $\dim_{\rm H} E_λ$ for $λ$ near $1/2$; in particular, $\dim_{\rm H} E_λ$ approaches $1$ as $λ\uparrow1/2$.
  For the Minkowski sum problem, we prove that $A_λ$ has nonempty interior throughout the previously open range $1/4<λ<1/3$, answering a question of Simon and Taylor. Together with earlier results of Simon and Taylor, our theorem yields the complete classification: $A_λ$ has nonempty interior in $\mathbb{R}^2$ if and only if $1/4<λ<1/2$. More generally, we prove that $C_λ+Γ$ has nonempty interior for every regular $C^1$ closed curve $Γ$ whenever $1/4<λ<1/2$.

</details>


### [156] [Wavelet orthonormal bases for nonexpansive dilations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08746)
*Jordy Timo van Velthoven,Felix Voigtlaender*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文构造了针对非扩张膨胀和整数平移的小波标准正交基，这些基不承认小波集，也不满足Calderón求和公式，并证明了对于行列式为1的膨胀矩阵存在小波基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的研究表明，小波集的存在性与小波基的构造密切相关。本文旨在探索在更一般的非扩张膨胀条件下，小波标准正交基是否可以存在，即使这些基不具备传统意义上小波集的性质或满足Calderón求和公式。

Method: 构造针对非扩张膨胀（nonexpansive dilations）和整数平移的小波标准正交基，这种构造不依赖于小波集的存在性，而是采用其他数学手段直接构造正交基。

Result: 成功构造了不承认小波集的小波标准正交基；这些基不满足Calderón求和公式；特别地，证明了存在针对行列式为1的膨胀矩阵的小波基。

Conclusion: 小波标准正交基的存在并不意味着必然存在小波集，也不意味着Calderón求和公式必然成立。即便对于行列式为1（即面积保持）的膨胀矩阵，依然可以构造出有效的正交小波基。

Abstract: We construct wavelet orthonormal bases for nonexpansive dilations and integer translations that do not admit a wavelet set. In addition, these orthonormal bases do not satisfy the Calderón sum formula. In particular, we show the existence of a wavelet basis for a dilation matrix with determinant one.

</details>


### [157] [Estimates for $L^p$ variants of Gowers norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08916)
*Vjekoslav Kovač,Keith M. Rogers*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究Gowers型泛函（由多重自相关的L^p范数定义），证明其关于L^q范数的尖锐界，并刻画了欧氏空间及局部紧阿贝尔群上的近极值函数。同时建立了两个降次不等式族并研究其近极值情形，由此证明了Gowers范数对数凸性估计中的常数严格小于1，证实了Bennett-Tao猜想，最后刻画了该泛函满足三角不等式的参数范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究源于Bennett和Tao提出的关于Gowers范数对数凸性的一个猜想，该猜想涉及精确常数是否为1的问题，需要更精细的Gowers型泛函分析工具来解决。

Method: 通过定义以多重自相关的L^p范数为基础的Gowers型泛函，在欧氏空间和局部紧阿贝尔群上建立尖锐界并刻画近极值函数，同时构造两类降次不等式，系统分析其极值行为。

Result: 证明了Gowers型泛函关于L^q范数的尖锐界及其近极值函数特征；建立了两个降次不等式族；证明了Gowers范数对数凸性估计中的常数严格小于1，解决了Bennett-Tao猜想；刻画了该泛函满足三角不等式的所有参数值。

Conclusion: 本文不仅证实了Bennett-Tao猜想（对数凸性常数严格小于1），还构建了一套刻画Gowers型泛函分析性质的完整理论框架，包括尖锐界、近极值函数分类及三角不等式的参数判定，为后续研究提供了系统工具。

Abstract: Motivated by a log-convexity question of Bennett and Tao, we consider Gowers-type functionals defined by $L^p$ norms of multiple autocorrelations. We prove sharp bounds in terms of $L^q$ norms and characterise the near-extremisers on Euclidean spaces as well as locally compact abelian groups. We also establish two families of degree-lowering inequalities and study their near-extremisers. As a byproduct of this broader theory, we show that the constant in the log-convexity estimate for Gowers norms is strictly less than unity, confirming the aforementioned conjecture of Bennett and Tao. Finally, we characterise the values of the parameters for which these Gowers-type functionals necessarily satisfy the triangle inequality on nonnegative measurable functions.

</details>

Abstract: For $d\geqslant3$, let $p^*=\frac{2d}{d-2}$. We prove that the Born-Jordan distribution maps $L^{2,p^*}(\mathbb R^d)\times L^{2,p^*}(\mathbb R^d)$ boundedly into $L^{p^*}(\mathbb R^{2d})$. The Lorentz index $p^*$ cannot be increased in either variable. In particular, this gives the endpoint $L^2\times L^2\to L^{p^*}$ estimate, settling the critical boundedness problem raised by Stra, Svela, and Trapasso \cite{SST26}.

### [159] [On the Two-Weight Problem for One-Sided Maximal Operators in Higher Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08975)
*Dinghuai Wang*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于单侧Hardy–Littlewood极大算子M_d^+，在d≥3且p>1时，二维权重Muckenhoupt条件A_{p,d}^+不再刻画加权弱型(p,p)不等式。作者构造了权重对(w,v)，满足A_{p,d}^+(w,v)<∞但算子从L^p(v)到L^{p,∞}(w)的算子范数为无穷。证明利用有限二维Hardy算子嵌入三维格点极大算子再转移到连续情形，并通过张量扩展推广到所有d≥3的维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史上，Sawyer（1986）在一维和Forzani、Martín-Reyes与Ombrosi（2011）在二维分别证明了自然的两权重Muckenhoupt条件刻画了弱型(p,p)不等式。Ombrosi和Nazarov最近在三维、p=1的情形给出了否定答案。本文旨在进一步探明在更高维度和更一般的p>1情形下，这一刻画是否仍然成立。

Method: 证明的关键是构造一个有限维的二维Hardy算子，其弱算子范数以c_p(log N)^{1/p'}为下界。将该算子嵌入三维格点极大算子结构中，再通过传递技巧转移到连续情形，构造出满足A_{p,d}^+条件但破坏弱型不等式的权重对。最后利用张量积扩展将反例从三维推广到任意d>3的维度。

Result: 对于每个1<p<∞且每个d≥3，存在权重w和v，使得A_{p,d}^+(w,v)<∞但\|M_d^+\|_{L^p(v)→L^{p,∞}(w)}=∞，即两权重Muckenhoupt条件在d≥3、p>1时不再刻画弱型(p,p)不等式。

Conclusion: 两权重Muckenhoupt条件对单侧Hardy–Littlewood极大算子的弱型不等式刻画仅在d=1和d=2时成立。从d≥3开始（无论p>1取何值），该刻画失效，这确认了该问题在维度上的本质转变。

Abstract: For the one-sided Hardy--Littlewood maximal operator $M_d^+$ on $\mathbb R^d$, the natural two-weight Muckenhoupt condition was shown by Sawyer in 1986 to characterize the weak $(p,p)$ inequality in dimension one, and by Forzani, Martín-Reyes and Ombrosi in 2011 in dimension two. At the endpoint $p=1$ in dimension three, Ombrosi and Nazarov have recently given negative answers to both the Fefferman--Stein-type question and the related weighted weak-type $(1,1)$ question \cite{OmbrosiNazarov} (personal communication). In this paper, we prove that the two-weight characterization fails for every $d\geq 3$ and $p>1$. More precisely, for every $1<p<\infty$ and every $d\geq 3$, there exist weights $w$ and $v$ such that $$ A_{p,d}^+(w,v)<\infty, \qquad \|M_d^+\|_{L^p(v)\to L^{p,\infty}(w)}=\infty. $$ The proof uses a finite two-dimensional Hardy operator whose weak operator norm is bounded below by $c_p(\log N)^{1/p'}$. This operator is embedded into the three-dimensional lattice maximal operator and then transferred to the continuous setting. A tensor extension yields the same failure in every dimension $d>3$.

</details>
